===Original===
The five o’clock train, having given itself a spasmodic jerk, began to move slowly out of Paddington Station. The platform past which it was gliding was crowded with a number of the fauna always to be seen at railway stations at such moments, but in their ranks there was no sign of Mr. Ralston McTodd: and Psmith, as he sat opposite Lord Emsworth in a corner seat of a first-class compartment, felt that genial glow of satisfaction which comes to the man who has successfully taken a chance. Until now, he had been half afraid that McTodd, having changed his mind, might suddenly appear with bag and baggage﻿—an event which must necessarily have caused confusion and discomfort. His mind was now tranquil. Concerning the future he declined to worry. It would, no doubt, contain its little difficulties, but he was prepared to meet them in the right spirit; and his only trouble in the world now was the difficulty he was experiencing in avoiding his lordship’s legs, which showed a disposition to pervade the compartment like the tentacles of an octopus. Lord Emsworth rather ran to leg, and his practice of reclining when at ease on the base of his spine was causing him to straddle, like Apollyon in Pilgrim’s Progress, “right across the way.” It became manifest that in a journey lasting several hours his society was likely to prove irksome. For the time being, however, he endured it, and listened with polite attention to his host’s remarks on the subject of the Blandings gardens. Lord Emsworth, in a train moving in the direction of home, was behaving like a horse heading for his stable. He snorted eagerly, and spoke at length and with emotion of roses and herbaceous borders.

===Chinese===
五点钟的那班火车抽搐似的猛一颠簸，开始缓缓驶出帕丁顿车站。它滑过的站台上，挤满了这种时刻车站上总归看得见的那类众生相，可队列里偏偏不见拉尔斯顿·麦克托德先生的踪影。皮史密斯坐在头等车厢靠窗的角落里，与埃姆斯沃思勋爵相对，心头泛起一阵志得意满的暖意——这正是冒险侥幸得手之人特有的那种暖意。直到此刻，他还一直隐隐担心麦克托德临时变卦，忽然带着行李家当冒将出来——那势必引起一场混乱与尴尬。如今他心里踏实了。至于未来，他拒绝为之操心。未来无疑会有些小小难关，但他已备好以正确的心态从容应对；眼下他在世上唯一的烦恼，就是得费劲避开勋爵大人的两条腿——那两条腿大有弥漫整节车厢之势，宛如章鱼的触手。埃姆斯沃思勋爵的腿相当富余，而他安适时惯于往尾椎骨上一躺的做派，更叫他双腿大叉，活像《天路历程》里的亚玻伦，「横断通衢」。显然，在长达数小时的旅程里，与他做伴恐怕要令人头疼。不过眼下皮史密斯暂且忍着，还客客气气、聚精会神地听着东道主大谈布兰丁斯的花园。埃姆斯沃思勋爵坐在朝家驶去的火车上，那做派活像一匹望见马厩的老马。他喷着响鼻，絮絮地、饱含深情地大谈玫瑰和花境。

===Original===
“It will be dark, I suppose, by the time we arrive,” he said regretfully, “but the first thing tomorrow, my dear fellow, I must take you round and show you my gardens.”

===Chinese===
「我估摸着，等咱们到的时候天该黑了，」他怅然说道，「可明天一早，亲爱的伙计，我头一件事就是要领你逛逛，看看我的花园。」

===Original===
“I shall look forward to it keenly,” said Psmith. “They are, I can readily imagine, distinctly oojah-cum-spiff.”

===Chinese===
「我将热切期待，」皮史密斯说，「我可以想见，它们想必是妙到了 oojah-cum-spiff（好得不能再好）的地步。」

===Original===
“I beg your pardon?” said Lord Emsworth with a start.

===Chinese===
「你说什么？」埃姆斯沃思勋爵吓了一跳。

===Original===
“Not at all,” said Psmith graciously.

===Chinese===
「没什么，」皮史密斯宽宏大量地说。

===Original===
“Er﻿—what did you say?” asked his lordship after a slight pause.

===Chinese===
「呃——你方才说什么来着？」勋爵大人顿了顿，问道。

===Original===
“I was saying that, from all reports, you must have a very nifty display of garden-produce at your rural seat.”

===Chinese===
「我是说，据各方传闻，您的乡间宅邸里，园圃物产必定陈列得十分漂亮。」

===Original===
“Oh, yes. Oh, most,” said his lordship, looking puzzled. He examined Psmith across the compartment with something of the peering curiosity which he would have bestowed upon a new and unclassified shrub. “Most extraordinary!” he murmured. “I trust, my dear fellow, you will not think me personal, but, do you know, nobody would imagine that you were a poet. You don’t look like a poet, and, dash it, you don’t talk like a poet.”

===Chinese===
「哦，是的。哦，可不，」勋爵大人说，满脸困惑。他隔着车厢打量皮史密斯，那细瞧的好奇劲儿，活像在端详一株新发现的、尚未归类的灌木。「真是非同寻常！」他喃喃道，「亲爱的伙计，相信你不会怪我多嘴——你可知道，谁也看不出你是个诗人。你长得不像诗人，而且，见鬼，你说话也不像诗人。」

===Original===
“How should a poet talk?”

===Chinese===
「诗人该是怎么个说法？」

===Original===
“Well﻿ ﻿…” Lord Emsworth considered the point. “Well, Miss Peavey﻿ ﻿… But of course you don’t know Miss Peavey﻿ ﻿… Miss Peavey is a poetess, and she waylaid me the other morning while I was having a most important conference with McAllister on the subject of bulbs and asked me if I didn’t think that it was fairies’ teardrops that made the dew. Did you ever hear such dashed nonsense?”

===Chinese===
「这个嘛……」埃姆斯沃思勋爵琢磨了一下，「嗯，皮维小姐……不过你当然不认得皮维小姐……皮维小姐是位女诗人，前几天早上，我正跟麦卡利斯特开一场顶要紧的球茎会议，她半路把我拦住，问我露水难道不是仙子的泪珠凝成的。你听过这种混账话没有？」

===Original===
“Evidently an aggravated case. Is Miss Peavey staying at the castle?”

===Chinese===
「显然是病得不轻。皮维小姐也在堡里住着？」

===Original===
“My dear fellow, you couldn’t shift her with blasting-powder. Really this craze of my sister Constance for filling the house with these infernal literary people is getting on my nerves. I can’t stand these poets and whatnot. Never could.”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的伙计，用炸药都轰不走她。我妹妹康斯坦丝这股子往家里塞文人的邪火劲儿，实在让我神经受不了。这帮诗人和什么什么家，我受不了。从来就受不了。」

===Original===
“We must always remember, however,” said Psmith gravely, “that poets are also God’s creatures.”

===Chinese===
「不过我们须得时时记取，」皮史密斯正色道，「诗人亦属上帝的造物。」

===Original===
“Good heavens!” exclaimed his lordship, aghast. “I had forgotten that you were one. What will you think of me, my dear fellow! But, of course, as I said a moment ago, you are different. I admit that when Constance told me that she had invited you to the house I was not cheered, but, now that I have had the pleasure of meeting you﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「老天爷！」勋爵大人愕然失声，「我倒忘了你也是其中一位。你会怎么看我呢，亲爱的伙计！不过，当然啦，我方才也说了，你与众不同。我承认，康斯坦丝告诉我说邀了你来住的时候，我心里并不痛快，可如今既然有幸与你相识……」

===Original===
The conversation had worked round to the very point to which Psmith had been wishing to direct it. He was keenly desirous of finding out why Mr. McTodd had been invited to Blandings and﻿—a still more vital matter﻿—of ascertaining whether, on his arrival there as Mr. McTodd’s understudy, he was going to meet people who knew the poet by sight. On this latter point, it seemed to him, hung the question of whether he was about to enjoy a delightful visit to a historic country house in the society of Eve Halliday﻿—or leave the train at the next stop and omit to return to it.

===Chinese===
话头恰恰绕到了皮史密斯一心想引到的地方。他正急切想弄明白麦克托德先生何以受邀来布兰丁斯，还有——更要命的一层——想探清楚：他以麦克托德先生替补者的身份到达之后，会不会撞见认识这位诗人的熟人。在他看来，后一个问题，直接决定着他此行究竟是在伊芙·哈利迪的相伴下、在一座历史悠久的乡间宅邸里快活做客——还是在下一站下车，一去不返。

===Original===
“It was extremely kind of Lady Constance,” he hazarded, “to invite a perfect stranger to Blandings.”

===Chinese===
「康斯坦丝夫人邀一位素昧平生的人来布兰丁斯，」他试探道，「实在是天大的好意。」

===Original===
“Oh, she’s always doing that sort of thing,” said his lordship. “It didn’t matter to her that she’d never seen you in her life. She had read your books, you know, and liked them: and when she heard that you were coming to England, she wrote to you.”

===Chinese===
「哦，她老干这种事，」勋爵大人说，「她这辈子没见过你，一点关系都没有。她读过你的书，你知道，还挺喜欢：后来听说你要来英国，就给你写了信。」

===Original===
“I see,” said Psmith, relieved.

===Chinese===
「原来如此，」皮史密斯松了口气。

===Original===
“Of course, it is all right as it has turned out,” said Lord Emsworth handsomely. “As I say, you’re different. And how you came to write that﻿ ﻿… that﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「当然啦，如今事情这样收场，也就皆大欢喜了，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵大方地说，「正如我所说，你与众不同。再说，你怎么会写出那种……那种……」

===Original===
“Bilge?” suggested Psmith.

===Chinese===
「胡话？」皮史密斯提示道。

===Original===
“The very word I was about to employ, my dear fellow﻿ ﻿… No, no, I don’t mean that﻿ ﻿… I﻿—I﻿ ﻿… Capital stuff, no doubt, capital stuff﻿ ﻿… but﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我要找的正是这个词，亲爱的伙计……不，不，我不是那个意思……我——我……想必是妙笔，毫无疑问，妙笔……不过……」

===Original===
“I understand.”

===Chinese===
「我明白。」

===Original===
“Constance tried to make me read the things, but I couldn’t. I fell asleep over them.”

===Chinese===
「康斯坦丝曾逼着我读那些东西，可我读不下去。读着读着就睡着了。」

===Original===
“I hope you rested well.”

===Chinese===
「但愿您睡得安稳。」

===Original===
“I﻿—er﻿—the fact is, I suppose they were beyond me. I couldn’t see any sense in the things.”

===Chinese===
「我——呃——实话说，我估摸那些东西超出了我的理解力。我瞧不出那些玩意儿有什么道理。」

===Original===
“If you would care to have another pop at them,” said Psmith agreeably, “I have a complete set in my bag.”

===Chinese===
「您若有意再领教一回，」皮史密斯和颜悦色地说，「我行李里带着一整套呢。」

===Original===
“No, no, my dear fellow, thank you very much, thank you a thousand times. I﻿—er﻿—find that reading in the train tries my eyes.”

===Chinese===
「不不，亲爱的伙计，多谢多谢，千恩万谢。我——呃——发觉在车上看书伤眼睛。」

===Original===
“Ah! You would prefer that I read them aloud?”

===Chinese===
「啊！那您宁可我朗诵给您听？」

===Original===
“No, no.” A look of hunted alarm came into his lordship’s speaking countenance at the suggestion. “As a matter of fact, I generally take a short nap at the beginning of a railway journey. I find it refreshing and﻿—er﻿—in short, refreshing. You will excuse me?”

===Chinese===
「不不，」一听这话，勋爵大人那张富于表情的脸上顿时露出被人追捕的惊惶。「实话说，坐火车出门，我照例要先打个盹。我觉得这样提神，而且——呃——总而言之，提神。你多包涵？」

===Original===
“If you think you can get to sleep all right without the aid of my poems, certainly.”

===Chinese===
「您若觉得不用我的诗催眠也能睡着，那当然请便。」

===Original===
“You won’t think me rude?”

===Chinese===
「你不会怪我失礼吧？」

===Original===
“Not at all, not at all. By the way, am I likely to meet any old friends at Blandings?”

===Chinese===
「不会不会。对了，我在布兰丁斯能碰上什么旧相识吗？」

===Original===
“Eh? Oh no. There will be nobody but ourselves. Except my sister and Miss Peavey, of course. You said you had not met Miss Peavey, I think?”

===Chinese===
「嗯？哦，没有。就我们自家人。当然，还有我妹妹和皮维小姐。你说过还没见过皮维小姐，对吧？」

===Original===
“I have not had that pleasure. I am, of course, looking forward to it with the utmost keenness.”

===Chinese===
「尚未有此荣幸。自然，我正以最大的热忱期待着。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth eyed him for a moment, astonished: then concluded the conversation by closing his eyes defensively. Psmith was left to his reflections, which a few minutes later were interrupted by a smart kick on the shin, as Lord Emsworth, a jumpy sleeper, began to throw his long legs about. Psmith moved to the other end of the seat, and, taking his bag down from the rack, extracted a slim volume bound in squashy mauve. After gazing at this in an unfriendly manner for a moment, he opened it at random and began to read. His first move on leaving Lord Emsworth at the florist’s had been to spend a portion of his slender capital on the works of Ralston McTodd in order not to be taken at a disadvantage in the event of questions about them at Blandings: but he speedily realised, as he dipped into the poems, that anything in the nature of a prolonged study of them was likely to spoil his little holiday. They were not light summer reading.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵愕然打量了他片刻，随即闭起眼睛自卫，就此结束了谈话。皮史密斯独自寻思起来，没几分钟，小腿上便挨了利落的一脚——埃姆斯沃思勋爵睡觉不老实，两条长腿开始四下乱蹬。皮史密斯挪到座位另一头，从行李架上取下提包，抽出一本浅紫色软面装帧的薄薄小书。他不友好地瞪了它一阵，随手翻开读了起来。在花匠铺子门口与埃姆斯沃思勋爵分手后，他头一件事就是从微薄的本钱里掏出一笔，买下拉尔斯顿·麦克托德的全集，免得在布兰丁斯被人问起时措手不及。可刚一翻进去他就很快明白：对这等文字下任何旷日持久的功夫，只怕要糟蹋了他这趟小小的假期。它们可不是消夏的轻松读物。


===Original===
> “Across the pale parabola of Joy﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
> 「穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线……」


===Original===
A gurgling snort from the other end of the compartment abruptly detached his mind from its struggle with this mystic line. He perceived that his host had slipped even further down on to his spine and was now lying with open mouth in an attitude suggestive of dislocation. And as he looked, there was a whistling sound, and another snore proceeded from the back of his lordship’s throat.

===Chinese===
包厢另一头传来一声咕噜噜的鼾响，蓦地把他从这句玄诗的苦斗中拽了出来。他这才看清，东道主又往尾椎骨上滑溜了一截，正张着嘴躺在那里，那姿势险些教人疑心脱了臼。正看着，一声呼哨，勋爵大人的喉咙深处又奏起一阵鼾乐。

===Original===
Psmith rose and took his book of poems out into the corridor with the purpose of roaming along the train until he should find an empty compartment in which to read in peace.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯站起身，携着诗集走进过道，打算沿车厢遛一遛，找间空包厢安安静静读书。

===Original===
With the two adjoining compartments he had no luck. One was occupied by an elderly man with a retriever, while the presence of a baby in the other ruled it out of consideration. The third, however, looked more promising. It was not actually empty, but there was only one occupant, and he was asleep. He was lying back in the far corner with a large silk handkerchief draped over his face and his feet propped up on the seat opposite. His society did not seem likely to act as a bar to the study of Mr. McTodd’s masterpieces. Psmith sat down and resumed his reading.

===Chinese===
相邻的两间包厢他都运气不佳。一间里坐着位带条金毛犬的老先生，另一间里有个婴儿，直接断了念想。第三间倒颇有指望。虽说不算全空，但只有一位乘客，而且睡着了。那人仰在里头角落里，脸上盖着一方大绸帕，双脚跷在对面的座位上。有他做伴，想来不至于妨碍钻研麦克托德先生的大作。皮史密斯坐下，接着读书。


===Original===
> “Across the pale parabola of Joy﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
> 「穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线……」


===Original===
Psmith knitted his brow. It was just the sort of line which was likely to have puzzled his patroness, Lady Constance, and he anticipated that she would come to him directly he arrived and ask for an explanation. It would obviously be a poor start for his visit to confess that he had no theory as to its meaning himself. He tried it again.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯皱起眉头。这正是那种多半也让他恩主康斯坦丝夫人犯难的句子，他料定自己前脚到，她后脚就会来讨讲解。倘若一进门就招认自己也对它的含义毫无定见，这趟做客可就开场砸了。他又试了一遍。


===Original===
> “Across the pale parabola of Joy﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
> 「穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线……」


===Original===
A sound like two or three pigs feeding rather noisily in the middle of a thunderstorm interrupted his meditations. Psmith laid his book down and gazed in a pained way across the compartment. There came to him a sense of being unfairly put upon, as towards the end of his troubles it might have come upon Job. This, he felt, was too much. He was being harried.

===Chinese===
一声仿佛雷雨正酣时两三口猪闹哄哄抢食的动静打断了他的冥想。皮史密斯搁下书，苦着脸朝包厢那头望去。一种遭人不公欺压之感涌上心头，好比约伯在磨难将尽时的滋味。这，他感觉，实在过了头。他这是被人滋扰了。

===Original===
The man in the corner went on snoring.

===Chinese===
角落里那位接着打鼾。

===Original===
---

===Chinese===
---

===Original===
There is always a way. Almost immediately Psmith saw what Napoleon would have done in this crisis. On the seat beside the sleeper was lying a compact little suitcase with hard, sharp edges. Rising softly, Psmith edged along the compartment and secured this. Then, having balanced it carefully on the rack above the sleeper’s stomach, he returned to his seat to await developments.

===Chinese===
天无绝人之路。皮史密斯几乎立刻看出了拿破仑处此危局会怎么办。酣睡者身旁的座位上，正搁着一只棱角坚硬锋利的紧凑小手提箱。皮史密斯轻手轻脚起身，沿包厢蹭过去，把箱子弄到手。然后，把它小心平衡在酣睡者肚子上方的行李架上，便回座位静候事态发展。

===Original===
These were not long in coming. The train, now flying at its best speed through open country, was shaking itself at intervals in a vigorous way as it raced along. A few seconds later it apparently passed over some points, and shivered briskly down its whole length. The suitcase wobbled insecurely, hesitated, and fell chunkily in the exact middle of its owner’s waistcoat. There was a smothered gulp beneath the handkerchief. The sleeper sat up with a jerk. The handkerchief fell off. And there was revealed to Psmith’s interested gaze the face of the Hon. Freddie Threepwood.

===Chinese===
事态来得很快。火车正以全速飞驰在旷野上，一路疾奔，不时狠狠抖它一抖。几秒之后，它显然碾过了一组道岔，从头到尾利索地一哆嗦。手提箱晃晃悠悠站不稳，迟疑片刻，结结实实砸在主人马甲的正中央。绸帕底下发出一声闷哼。酣睡者猛地坐起。绸帕滑落。皮史密斯饶有兴味的目光里，赫然现出弗雷迪·思里普伍德少爷的那张脸。

### II / II

===Original===
“Goo!” observed Freddie. He removed the bag from his midriff and began to massage the stricken spot. Then suddenly perceiving that he was not alone he looked up and saw Psmith.

===Chinese===
「咕！」弗雷迪喊了一声。他把箱子从小腹上挪开，揉搓起受创的部位。随即猛然察觉包厢里并非只有自己一人，他抬头看见了皮史密斯。

===Original===
“Goo!” said Freddie, and sat staring wildly.

===Chinese===
「咕！」弗雷迪又喊了一声，直勾勾地干瞪着眼。

===Original===
Nobody is more alive than we are to the fact that the dialogue of Frederick Threepwood, recorded above, is not bright. Nevertheless, those were his opening remarks, and the excuse must be that he had passed through a trying time and had just received two shocks, one after the other. From the first of these, the physical impact of the suitcase, he was recovering; but the second had simply paralysed him. When, the mists of sleep having cleared away, he saw sitting but a few feet away from him on the train that was carrying him home the very man with whom he had plotted in the lobby of the Piccadilly Palace Hotel, a cold fear gripped Freddie’s very vitals.

===Chinese===
弗雷德里克·思里普伍德上文这段对白不算精彩，没有人比我们更清楚这一点。然而这毕竟是他的开场白，还请多多包涵：他方才渡过了一段煎熬时光，先后挨了两记惊吓。头一记——箱子的物理撞击——他正缓过来；后一记却把他整个人吓瘫了。当睡雾散尽，他看见在同一列载他回家的火车上，几步开外端坐着的，正是那位在皮卡迪利宫酒店门厅里与他密谋过的仁兄，一股寒意顿时攫住了弗雷迪的五脏六腑。

===Original===
Freddie’s troubles had begun when he just missed the twelve-fifty train. This disaster had perturbed him greatly, for he could not forget his father’s stern injunctions on the subject. But what had really upset him was the fact that he had come within an ace of missing the five o’clock train as well. He had spent the afternoon in a motion-picture palace, and the fascination of the film had caused him to lose all sense of time, so that only the slow fade-out on the embrace and the words “The End” reminded him to look at his watch. A mad rush had got him to Paddington just as the five o’clock express was leaving the station. Exhausted, he had fallen into a troubled sleep, from which he had been aroused by a violent blow in the waistcoat and the nightmare vision of Psmith in the seat across the compartment. One cannot wonder in these circumstances that Freddie did not immediately soar to the heights of eloquence.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪的倒霉事始于误了十二点五十那班车。这场祸事让他大为慌乱，因为父亲在这事上的严令他没敢忘。可真正叫他魂飞魄散的是，五点钟这班车他也险些没赶上。他一下午泡在一家电影院里，影片的魅力让他全然忘了时辰，直到拥抱镜头缓缓淡出、「剧终」二字亮起，才提醒他看表。一顿狂奔，赶在五点快车正离站时冲进了帕丁顿。他筋疲力尽，坠入一觉不安稳的酣睡，又被马甲上重重一击惊醒，睁眼便看见包厢对面座位上皮史密斯这场梦魇般的幻影。处此境况，弗雷迪没能立刻口吐莲花、妙语如珠，也怪不得他。

===Original===
The picture which the Hon. Frederick Threepwood had selected for his patronage that afternoon was the well-known super-super-film, *Fangs of the Past*, featuring Bertha Blevitch and Maurice Heddlestone﻿—which, as everybody knows, is all about blackmail. Green-walled by primeval hills, bathed in the golden sunshine of peace and happiness, the village of Honeydean slumbered in the clear morning air. But off the train from the city stepped A Stranger﻿—(The Stranger﻿—Maxwell Bannister). He inquired of a passing rustic﻿—(The Passing Rustic﻿—Claude Hepworth)﻿—the way to the great house where Myrtle Dale, the Lady Bountiful of the village﻿ ﻿… well, anyway, it is all about blackmail, and it had affected Freddie profoundly. It still coloured his imagination, and the conclusion to which he came the moment he saw Psmith was that the latter had shadowed him and was following him home with the purpose of extracting hush-money.

===Chinese===
弗雷德里克·思里普伍德少爷那天下午光顾赏识的影片，是无人不晓的超级大片《往日獠牙》（*Fangs of the Past*），由伯莎·布莱维奇与莫里斯·赫德尔斯顿领衔——众所周知，通篇讲的是敲诈勒索。洪荒群山环抱出一方绿壁，沐浴着和平幸福的金色阳光，蜂蜜迪安村在清晨澄澈的空气里酣睡。然而，自城中来的火车上走下一位陌生人——（陌生人——麦克斯韦·班尼斯特）。他向路过的一位乡民——（路过的乡民——克劳德·赫普沃思）——打听通往大宅的路，大宅里住着村里的慈善太太默特尔·戴尔……总而言之，通篇讲的是敲诈勒索，而它对弗雷迪影响至深。此刻它仍染着他的想象力，所以他一见皮史密斯，得出的结论便是：对方盯了他的梢，一路尾随他回家，为的是敲封口钱。

===Original===
While he was still gurgling wordlessly, Psmith opened the conversation.

===Chinese===
他还在无言地干咽，皮史密斯开了腔。

===Original===
“A delightful and unexpected pleasure, comrade. I thought you had left the Metropolis some hours since.”

===Chinese===
「幸会幸会，出其不意的喜相逢，同志。我还当你几个钟头前就离开伦敦城了呢。」

===Original===
As Freddie sat looking like a cornered dormouse a voice from the corridor spoke.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪坐在那儿，活像被逼到墙角的一只睡鼠，正待无言地咽口水，过道里忽然有人说话。

===Original===
“Ah, there you are, my dear fellow!”

===Chinese===
「啊，你可在这儿，亲爱的伙计！」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth was beaming in the doorway. His slumbers, like those of Freddie, had not lasted long. He had been aroused only a few minutes after Psmith’s departure by the arrival of the retriever from the next compartment, which, bored by the society of its owner, had strolled off on a tour of investigation and, finding next door an old acquaintance in the person of his lordship, had jumped on the seat and licked his face with such hearty good will that further sleep was out of the question. Being awake, Lord Emsworth, as always when he was awake, had begun to potter.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵满面春风地站在门口。他与弗雷迪一样，觉也没睡长。皮史密斯前脚走，后脚邻厢那条金毛犬就到了——它嫌主人无趣，独自出来巡察一番，发现隔壁是位老相识勋爵大人，便跳上座位，热肠善意地把他的脸舔了个遍，教人再无睡意。既然醒了，埃姆斯沃思勋爵便一如醒时的做派，开始闲逛起来。

===Original===
When he saw Freddie his amiability suffered a shock.

===Chinese===
一看见弗雷迪，他的和气便挨了当头一棒。

===Original===
“Frederick! I thought I told you to be sure to return on the twelve-fifty train!”

===Chinese===
「弗雷德里克！我记得我吩咐过你，务必坐十二点五十的车回来！」

===Original===
“Missed it, guv’nor,” mumbled Freddie thickly. “Not my fault.”

===Chinese===
「没赶上，老爸，」弗雷迪含混不清地咕哝，「不能怪我。」

===Original===
“H’mph!” His father seemed about to pursue the subject, but the fact that a stranger and one who was his guest was present apparently decided him to avoid anything in the shape of family wrangles. He peered from Freddie to Psmith and back again. “Do you two know each other?” he said.

===Chinese===
「哼！」他父亲似要追究下去，但碍着一位陌生客人当面，显然还是决定别闹什么家庭口角。他把目光从弗雷迪挪到皮史密斯，又挪回去。「你们俩认识？」他说。

===Original===
“Not yet,” said Psmith. “We only met a moment ago.”

===Chinese===
「还不算认识，」皮史密斯说，「方才才碰的面。」

===Original===
“My son Frederick,” said Lord Emsworth, rather in the voice with which he would have called attention to the presence of a slug among his flowers. “Frederick, this is Mr. McTodd, the poet, who is coming to stay at Blandings.”

===Chinese===
「我儿子弗雷德里克，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵说，那口气颇像提醒人注意花坛里混进了一条蛞蝓。「弗雷德里克，这位是麦克托德先生，诗人，来布兰丁斯小住的。」

===Original===
Freddie started, and his mouth opened. But, meeting Psmith’s friendly gaze, he closed the orifice again without speaking. He licked his lips in an overwrought way.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪一激灵，嘴张开了。可一碰上皮史密斯友善的目光，他把嘴又闭上了，没出声，只神经质地舔了舔嘴唇。

===Original===
“You’ll find me next door, if you want me,” said Lord Emsworth to Psmith. “Just discovered that George Willard, very old friend of mine, is in there. Never saw him get on the train. His dog came into my compartment and licked my face. One of my neighbours. A remarkable rose-grower. As you are so interested in flowers, I will take you over to his place some time. Why don’t you join us now?”

===Chinese===
「要找我的话，我在隔壁，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵对皮史密斯说，「刚发现乔治·威拉德在里头，我的老朋友。上车的工夫竟没瞧见他。他的狗跑进我包厢舔了我的脸。是我的老邻居。玫瑰种得出神入化。你既然这么爱花，改天我带你去他那儿逛逛。这会儿何不过来同坐？」

===Original===
“I would prefer, if you do not mind,” said Psmith, “to remain here for the moment and foster what I feel sure is about to develop into a great and lasting friendship. I am convinced that your son and I will have much to talk about together.”

===Chinese===
「若不见怪，」皮史密斯说，「我想暂且留在此地，培植一段我确信即将发展为伟大而长久的友谊。我深信，令郎与我在一起会有许多话可谈。」

===Original===
“Very well, my dear fellow. We will meet at dinner in the restaurant-car.”

===Chinese===
「很好，亲爱的伙计。餐车吃晚饭时见。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth pottered off, and Psmith rose and closed the door. He returned to his seat to find Freddie regarding him with a tortured expression in his rather prominent eyes. Freddie’s brain had had more exercise in the last few minutes than in years of his normal life, and he was feeling the strain.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵踱了出去，皮史密斯起身关上门。回到座位，只见弗雷迪正用一双相当凸出的眼睛，苦大仇深地望着他。弗雷迪的脑子这几分钟得到的运动，比他平常几年的加起来还多，他只觉得吃不消。

===Original===
“I say, what?” he observed feebly.

===Chinese===
「我说，啊？」他有气无力地说。

===Original===
“If there is anything,” said Psmith kindly, “that I can do to clear up any little difficulty that is perplexing you, call on me. What is biting you?”

===Chinese===
「若有何等小事令你困惑，」皮史密斯和蔼地说，「我若能代为排解，尽管吩咐。什么事咬着你了？」

===Original===
Freddie swallowed convulsively.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪痉挛似的咽了口唾沫。

===Original===
“I say, he said your name was McTodd!”

===Chinese===
「我说，他刚才管你叫麦克托德！」

===Original===
“Precisely.”

===Chinese===
「正是。」

===Original===
“But you said it was Psmith.”

===Chinese===
「可你自己说的是皮史密斯。」

===Original===
“It is.”

===Chinese===
「也没错。」

===Original===
“Then why did father call you McTodd?”

===Chinese===
「那老爸为什么叫你麦克托德？」

===Original===
“He thinks I am. It is a harmless error, and I see no reason why it should be discouraged.”

===Chinese===
「他以为我是。这是个无害的误会，我看不出有什么理由去纠正。」

===Original===
“But why does he think you’re McTodd?”

===Chinese===
「可他怎么会当你是麦克托德呢？」

===Original===
“It is a long story, which you may find tedious. But, if you really wish to hear it﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「说来话长，只怕你听着腻烦。不过，你若真想听……」

===Original===
Nothing could have exceeded the raptness of Freddie’s attention as he listened to the tale of the encounter with Lord Emsworth at the Senior Conservative Club.

===Chinese===
听皮史密斯讲述在老保守党俱乐部与埃姆斯沃思勋爵相遇的始末时，弗雷迪那副全神贯注，无出其右者。

===Original===
“Do you mean to say,” he demanded at its conclusion, “that you’re coming to Blandings pretending to be this poet blighter?”

===Chinese===
「你是说，」故事收尾他质问道，「你要顶着这个诗人小子的大名，混到布兰丁斯去？」

===Original===
“That is the scheme.”

===Chinese===
「计画如此。」

===Original===
“But why?”

===Chinese===
「可这是图什么呀？」

===Original===
“I have my reasons, Comrade﻿—what is the name? Threepwood? I thank you. You will pardon me, Comrade Threepwood, if I do not go into them. And now,” said Psmith, “to resume our very interesting chat which was unfortunately cut short this morning, why do you want me to steal your aunt’s necklace?”

===Chinese===
「我自有缘由，同志——贵姓是？思里普伍德？谢谢。恕我暂时不便奉告，思里普伍德同志。现在，」皮史密斯说，「咱们接着聊今早不幸被打断的那场极有趣的谈话——你为什么要我偷你姑妈的项链？」

===Original===
Freddie jumped. For the moment, so tensely had the fact of his companion’s audacity chained his interest, he had actually forgotten about the necklace.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪蹦了起来。方才那阵子，同伴这份斗胆把他的兴味绷得太紧，项链的事他竟然真忘了。

===Original===
“Great Scott!” he exclaimed. “Why, of course!”

===Chinese===
「我的老天！」他叫道，「可不是嘛！」

===Original===
“You still have not made it quite clear.”

===Chinese===
「你始终没说清楚。」

===Original===
“It fits splendidly.”

===Chinese===
「这下可严丝合缝了。」

===Original===
“The necklace?”

===Chinese===
「项链？」

===Original===
“I mean to say, the great difficulty would have been to find a way of getting you into the house, and here you are, coming there as this poet bird. Topping!”

===Chinese===
「我是说，本来最大的难题是怎么把你弄进宅子，这下可好，你顶着这位诗人老兄的名头自己上门了。绝了！」

===Original===
“If,” said Psmith, regarding him patiently through his eyeglass, “I do not seem to be immediately infected by your joyous enthusiasm, put it down to the fact that I haven’t the remotest idea what you’re talking about. Could you give me a pointer or two? What, for instance, assuming that I agreed to steal your aunt’s necklace, would you expect me to do with it, when and if stolen?”

===Chinese===
「假如，」皮史密斯隔着单片眼镜耐心打量着他，「我并没有立刻被你这份欢腾的热情感染，请归因于我全然听不懂你在说什么。能否指点一二？比方说，假设我应允去偷你姑妈的项链，偷到手之后——假设偷得成——你指望我拿它怎么办？」

===Original===
“Why, hand it over to me.”

===Chinese===
「什么怎么办？交给我呗。」

===Original===
“I see. And what would you do with it?”

===Chinese===
「明白了。那你拿它怎么办？」

===Original===
“Hand it over to my uncle.”

===Chinese===
「交给我姨父。」

===Original===
“And whom would he hand it over to?”

===Chinese===
「那他又交给谁？」

===Original===
“Look here,” said Freddie, “I might as well start at the beginning.”

===Chinese===
「听我说，」弗雷迪道，「我还是从头讲起吧。」

===Original===
“An excellent idea.”

===Chinese===
「好主意。」

===Original===
The speed at which the train was now proceeding had begun to render conversation in anything but stentorian tones somewhat difficult. Freddie accordingly bent forward till his mouth almost touched Psmith’s ear.

===Chinese===
火车此刻的时速，已使得不用洪亮嗓门便难以交谈。弗雷迪于是凑上前去，嘴巴几乎贴上皮史密斯的耳朵。

===Original===
“You see, it’s like this. My uncle, old Joe Keeble﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「是这么回事。我姨父，老乔·基布尔……」

===Original===
“Keeble?” said Psmith. “Why,” he murmured meditatively, “is that name familiar?”

===Chinese===
「基布尔？」皮史密斯说。「咦，」他若有所思地喃喃，「这名字为何这样耳熟？」

===Original===
“Don’t interrupt, old lad,” pleaded Freddie.

===Chinese===
「别打岔，老伙计，」弗雷迪央求道。

===Original===
“I stand corrected.”

===Chinese===
「恕我知罪。」

===Original===
“Uncle Joe has a stepdaughter﻿—Phyllis her name is﻿—and some time ago she popped off and married a cove called Jackson﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「乔姨父有个继女——叫菲利斯——前些日子她跟一个姓杰克逊的家伙私奔，成了亲……」

===Original===
Psmith did not interrupt the narrative again, but as it proceeded his look of interest deepened. And at the conclusion he patted his companion encouragingly on the shoulder.

===Chinese===
这番叙述皮史密斯没有再打断，但随着故事展开，他神色中的兴味愈见浓厚。待到讲完，他鼓励地拍了拍同伴的肩膀。

===Original===
“The proceeds, then, of this jewel-robbery, if it comes off,” he said, “will go to establish the Jackson home on a firm footing? Am I right in thinking that?”

===Chinese===
「这么说，」他说，「这桩珠宝劫案倘若得手，赃款将用于给杰克逊家奠定一份安稳基业？我理解得对吗？」

===Original===
“Absolutely.”

===Chinese===
「半点不差。」

===Original===
“There is no danger﻿—you will pardon the suggestion﻿—of you clinging like glue to the swag and using it to maintain yourself in the position to which you are accustomed?”

===Chinese===
「那你总不至于——恕我冒昧——像胶水似的粘住赃物不放，拿它维持你过惯的排场吧？」

===Original===
“Absolutely not. Uncle Joe is giving me﻿—er﻿—giving me a bit for myself. Just a small bit, you understand. This is the scheme. You sneak the necklace and hand it over to me. I push the necklace over to Uncle Joe, who hides it somewhere for the moment. There is the dickens of a fuss, and Uncle Joe comes out strong by telling Aunt Constance that he’ll buy her another necklace, just as good. Then he takes the stones out of the necklace, has them reset, and gives them to Aunt Constance. Looks like a new necklace, if you see what I mean. Then he draws a cheque for twenty thousand quid, which Aunt Constance naturally thinks is for the new necklace, and he shoves the money somewhere as a little private account. He gives Phyllis her money, and everybody’s happy. Aunt Constance has got her necklace, Phyllis has got her money, and all that’s happened is that Aunt Constance’s and Uncle Joe’s combined bank balance has had a bit of a hole knocked in it. See?”

===Chinese===
「绝不至于。乔姨父会分我——呃——分我一小份。就一小份，你懂的。计画是这样：你把项链顺出来，交给我。我把项链转给乔姨父，他先把它藏个地方。家里闹个天翻地覆，然后乔姨父挺身而出，对康斯坦丝姑妈说他再买条一模一样好的。他把项链上的宝石拆下来，重新镶过，送给康斯坦丝姑妈。看着就跟新的一样，你明白我的意思。然后他开一张两万镑的支票，康斯坦丝姑妈自然当是付新项链的钱，他把这笔钱往哪儿一存，成了笔小小的私房。他把钱给菲利斯，人人皆大欢喜。康斯坦丝姑妈有了项链，菲利斯有了钱，全部损失不过是康斯坦丝姑妈和乔姨父两家的银行存款合计破了个小洞。明白？」

===Original===
“I see. It is a little difficult to follow all the necklaces. I seemed to count about seventeen of them while you were talking, but I suppose I was wrong. Yes, I see, Comrade Threepwood, and I may say at once that you can rely on my cooperation.”

===Chinese===
「明白了。只是这些项链绕得人有点晕。你说话的当口，我数着足有十七条，想必是我数岔了。是，我明白了，思里普伍德同志，而且我此刻便可奉告：我的合作，你尽管信赖。」

===Original===
“You’ll do it?”

===Chinese===
「你干？」

===Original===
“I will.”

===Chinese===
「我干。」

===Original===
“Of course,” said Freddie awkwardly, “I’ll see that you get a bit all right. I mean﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「当然，」弗雷迪局促地说，「我自会给你一份辛苦钱。我是说……」

===Original===
Psmith waved his hand deprecatingly.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯摆手制止。

===Original===
“My dear Comrade Threepwood, let us not become sordid on this glad occasion. As far as I am concerned, there will be no charge.”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的思里普伍德同志，在这大喜的日子里，咱们别沾染铜臭。就我而言，分文不取。」

===Original===
“What! But look here﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「什么！可你听我说……」

===Original===
“Any assistance I can give will be offered in a purely amateur spirit. I would have mentioned before, only I was reluctant to interrupt you, that Comrade Jackson is my boyhood chum, and that Phyllis, his wife, injects into my life the few beams of sunshine that illumine its dreary round. I have long desired to do something to ameliorate their lot, and now that the chance has come I am delighted. It is true that I am not a man of affluence﻿—my bank-manager, I am told, winces in a rather painful manner whenever my name is mentioned﻿—but I am not so reduced that I must charge a fee for performing, on behalf of a pal, a simple act of courtesy like pinching a twenty thousand pound necklace.”

===Chinese===
「我所能提供的协助，将纯粹以业余精神奉上。本该早提的，只是不愿打断你——杰克逊同志是我总角之交，而他的夫人菲利斯，为我这灰暗的生涯投射着仅有的几缕阳光。我早就想为他们做点什么来改善境遇，如今机会送上门来，我高兴还来不及。诚然，我并非富有之人——听说银行经理一听我的名字，脸上便要颇为痛苦地一抽——但也还不至于沦落到替朋友顺手牵羊偷条两万镑的项链这点小殷勤，都要收个费用。」

===Original===
“Good Lord! Fancy that!”

===Chinese===
「老天爷！真没想到！」

===Original===
“Fancy what, Comrade Threepwood?”

===Chinese===
「没想到什么，思里普伍德同志？」

===Original===
“Fancy your knowing Phyllis and her husband.”

===Chinese===
「没想到你竟认得菲利斯和她丈夫。」

===Original===
“It is odd, no doubt. But true. Many a whack at the cold beef have I had on Sunday evenings under their roof, and I am much obliged to you for putting in my way this opportunity of repaying their hospitality. Thank you!”

===Chinese===
「确是奇事。然而属实。多少个礼拜天晚上，我在他们屋檐下蹭过冷牛肉的边角，你这番成全，正好容我报答他们的款待之情。谢谢！」

===Original===
“Oh, that’s all right,” said Freddie, somewhat bewildered by this eloquence.

===Chinese===
「哦，这没什么，」弗雷迪说，被这番宏论弄得有点晕头转向。

===Original===
“Even if the little enterprise meets with disaster, the reflection that I did my best for the young couple will be a great consolation to me when I am serving my bit of time in Wormwood Scrubbs. It will cheer me up. The jailers will cluster outside the door to listen to me singing in my cell. My pet rat, as he creeps out to share the crumbs of my breakfast, will wonder why I whistle as I pick the morning’s oakum. I shall join in the hymns on Sundays in a way that will electrify the chaplain. That is to say, if anything goes wrong and I am what I believe is technically termed ‘copped.’ I say ‘if,’ ” said Psmith, gazing solemnly at his companion. “But I do not intend to be copped. I have never gone in largely for crime hitherto, but something tells me I shall be rather good at it. I look forward confidently to making a nice, clean job of the thing. And now, Comrade Threepwood, I must ask you to excuse me while I get the half-nelson on this rather poisonous poetry of good old McTodd’s. From the cursory glance I have taken at it, the stuff doesn’t seem to mean anything. I think the boy’s *non compos*. *You* don’t happen to understand the expression ‘Across the pale parabola of Joy,’ do you?﻿ ﻿… I feared as much. Well, pip-pip for the present, Comrade Threepwood. I shall now ask you to retire into your corner and amuse yourself for a while as you best can. I must concentrate, concentrate.”

===Chinese===
「即便这桩小小的事业万一翻了船，想起我已为这对年轻夫妇竭尽绵力，我在沃姆伍德·斯克拉布斯监狱服刑的那段日子里，也可引为莫大慰藉。它会让我打起精神。狱卒们将聚在牢门外，听我在号子里唱歌。我的心爱的老鼠，蹑足出来分食我早餐的面包屑时，会纳闷我理早晨的麻絮怎么还吹口哨。礼拜天唱赞美诗，我的加入将让牧师先生为之触电。当然，这是说万一出了岔子，我成了行话所谓的『栽了』的这种情况。我说『万一』，」皮史密斯庄重地望着同伴。「但我并不打算栽。以往我不大涉足犯罪一行，可冥冥中有个声音告诉我，我于此道颇有天分。我满心期待把这事办得干净漂亮。现在，思里普伍德同志，恕我失陪，我得先把麦克托德老兄这册毒害人的诗拿个擒拿手。粗粗扫了几眼，这些东西似乎不知所云。我看这小子是 *non compos*（神志昏昏）。『穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线』这个说法，*你* 该不至于懂吧？……果然不出我所料。好吧，回头再见，思里普伍德同志。现在请你退回你的角落，自行寻乐片刻。我必须全神贯注，全神贯注。」

===Original===
And Psmith, having put his feet up on the opposite seat and reopened the mauve volume, began to read. Freddie, his mind still in a whirl, looked out of the window at the passing scenery in a mood which was a nice blend of elation and apprehension.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯双脚往对面座位上一跷，重新翻开那本浅紫色的书，读了起来。弗雷迪脑子里仍旧一团乱麻，望着窗外掠过的景色，心头滋味，恰是欣喜与忧惧的绝佳配比。

### III / III

===Original===
Although the hands of the station clock pointed to several minutes past nine, it was still apparently early evening when the train drew up at the platform of Market Blandings and discharged its distinguished passengers. The sun, taken in as usual by the never-failing practical joke of the Daylight Saving Act, had only just set, and a golden afterglow lingered on the fields as the car which had met the train purred over the two miles of country road that separated the little town from the castle. As they passed in between the great stone gateposts and shot up the winding drive, the soft murmur of the engines seemed to deepen rather than break the soothing stillness. The air was fragrant with indescribable English scents. Somewhere in the distance sheep-bells tinkled; rabbits, waggling white tails, bolted across the path; and once a herd of agitated deer made a brief appearance among the trees. The only thing that disturbed the magic hush was the fluting voice of Lord Emsworth, on whom the spectacle of his beloved property had acted as an immediate stimulant. Unlike his son Freddie, who sat silent in his corner wrestling with his hopes and fears, Lord Emsworth had plunged into a perfect Niagara of speech the moment the car entered the park. In a high tenor voice, and with wide, excited gestures, he pointed out to Psmith oaks with a history and rhododendrons with a past: his conversation as they drew near the castle and came in sight of the flowerbeds taking on an almost lyrical note and becoming a sort of anthem of gladness, through which, like some theme in the minor, ran a series of opprobrious observations on the subject of Angus McAllister.

===Chinese===
车站大钟的针虽已指过九点好几分钟，火车驶进布兰丁斯集镇站台、吐下这批贵客时，天色看上去仍是傍晚。《夏令时法》这场从不失手的恶作剧照例把太阳哄了下去，它才刚落山，接站的小汽车嗡嗡碾过小镇与城堡之间那两英里乡路时，金色的余晖仍恋在田野上。驶进两根大石门柱之间、冲上蜿蜒车道时，发动机轻柔的低吟非但没有打破、反而加深了那份抚慰人心的静谧。空气里弥漫着难以言传的英伦芬芳。远处什么地方，羊铃叮当；兔子甩着白尾巴，蹿过小径；还有一次，一群惊惶的鹿在树影间短暂亮相。唯一搅扰这魔幻般沉寂的，是埃姆斯沃思勋爵那支长笛般的嗓音——眼见心爱的产业，他立刻来了精神。不像儿子弗雷迪缩在角落里不言不语，在希望与忧惧间辗转，埃姆斯沃思勋爵打从汽车驶进园子那一刻起，就一头扎进了一挂尼亚加拉瀑布般的话流。他用高亢的假声，配以激动的大手势，给皮史密斯一一指点哪棵橡树有来历、哪丛杜鹃有掌故；临近城堡、花圃在望时，他的谈吐更带上近乎咏叹的调门，化作一支欢乐颂——只是其中贯穿始终的，如同一支小调主题，是对安格斯·麦卡利斯特的一连串痛诋。

===Original===
Beach, the butler, solicitously scooping them out of the car at the front door, announced that her ladyship and Miss Peavey were taking their after-dinner coffee in the arbour by the bowling-green; and presently Psmith, conducted by his lordship, found himself shaking hands with a strikingly handsome woman in whom, though her manner was friendliness itself, he could detect a marked suggestion of the formidable. Aesthetically, he admired Lady Constance’s appearance, but he could not conceal from himself that in the peculiar circumstances he would have preferred something rather more fragile and drooping. Lady Constance conveyed the impression that anybody who had the choice between stealing anything from her and stirring up a nest of hornets with a short walking-stick would do well to choose the hornets.

===Chinese===
管家比奇在前门口殷勤地把他们从车里搀出来，禀报说夫人和皮维小姐正在草地滚球场旁的凉亭里用晚餐后的咖啡。不多时，皮史密斯便由勋爵大人引着，与一位美貌惊人的妇人握上了手。这位妇人虽然态度亲切之至，他却能从中察觉出一股咄咄逼人的凌厉。从审美角度，他激赏康斯坦丝夫人的姿容，却无法瞒过自己：处此微妙的境地，他倒宁愿来位纤弱一点、垂柳一点的女主人。康斯坦丝夫人给人的印象是：谁若可以在「偷她点东西」与「拿根手杖去捅马蜂窝」之间任选其一，还是挑马蜂窝为妙。

===Original===
“How do you do, Mr. McTodd?” said Lady Constance with great amiability. “I am so glad you were able to come after all.”

===Chinese===
「你好，麦克托德先生？」康斯坦丝夫人极为和蔼地说。「你能最终光临，我真是太高兴了。」

===Original===
Psmith wondered what she meant by “after all,” but there were so many things about his present situation calculated to tax the mind that he had no desire to probe slight verbal ambiguities. He shook her hand and replied that it was very kind of her to say so.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯纳罕她这「最终」二字何所指，但眼下的处境里费心猜疑的事太多，这点小小的语义含糊也就无心细究了。他握了握她的手，回答说您太客气了。

===Original===
“We are quite a small party at present,” continued Lady Constance, “but we are expecting a number of people quite soon. For the moment Aileen and you are our only guests. Oh, I am sorry, I should have﻿ ﻿… Miss Peavey, Mr. McTodd.”

===Chinese===
「眼下我们人很少，」康斯坦丝夫人接着说，「不过很快就要来一大批。此刻艾琳和你是我们仅有的两位客人。哦，对不起，我本该……皮维小姐，这位是麦克托德先生。」

===Original===
The slim and willowy female who during this brief conversation had been waiting in an attitude of suspended animation, gazing at Psmith with large, wistful eyes, stepped forward. She clasped Psmith’s hand in hers, held it, and in a low, soft voice, like thick cream made audible, uttered one reverent word.

===Chinese===
那位窈窕纤细的女郎，在短短这段对话里一直处于假死状态候着，睁着一双大而惆怅的眼睛望着皮史密斯，此刻款款上前。她把皮史密斯的手握进自己手里，握着不放，用一种低柔的、仿佛浓奶油发了声的嗓音，吐出一个虔诚的字眼。

===Original===
“*Maître!*”

===Chinese===
「*Maître*！（法语：大师！）」

===Original===
“I beg your pardon?” said Psmith. A young man capable of bearing himself with calm and dignity in most circumstances, however trying, he found his poise wobbling under the impact of Miss Aileen Peavey.

===Chinese===
「你说什么？」皮史密斯说。这位青年无论处境多么难堪，素来能持重自若，此刻在艾琳·皮维小姐的冲击下，也不禁方寸微乱。

===Original===
Miss Peavey often had this effect on the less soulful type of man, especially in the mornings, when such men are not at their strongest and best. When she came into the breakfast-room of a country house, brave men who had been up a bit late the night before quailed and tried to hide behind newspapers. She was the sort of woman who tells a man who is propping his eyes open with his fingers and endeavouring to correct a headache with strong tea, that she was up at six watching the dew fade off the grass, and didn’t he think that those wisps of morning mist were the elves’ bridal-veils. She had large, fine, melancholy eyes, and was apt to droop dreamily.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐对灵魂性欠奉的男人常起这种效用，清晨尤甚，因为这类男人清晨最不在状态。她走进一乡间宅邸的早餐室时，前夜熬夜稍久的好汉们也要发怵，纷纷往报纸后头躲。她就是那种女人：见一个男人正用手指撑着眼皮、就着浓茶压头痛，也要告诉他，她清晨六点就起来看露水从草上褪去，还说那些晨雾的雾缕，难道不是精灵们的新娘头纱。她生着一双大而美的忧郁眼睛，动辄梦一般地垂首。

===Original===
“Master!” said Miss Peavey, obligingly translating.

===Chinese===
「大师！」皮维小姐体贴地翻译道。

===Original===
There did not seem to be any immediate comeback to a remark like this, so Psmith contented himself with beaming genially at her through his monocle: and Miss Peavey came to bat again.

===Chinese===
这句话似乎没有立时的回法，皮史密斯便只管隔着单片眼镜朝她和气地微笑：皮维小姐则再度上场击球。

===Original===
“How wonderful that you were able to come﻿—after all!”

===Chinese===
「你终究还是来了——多奇妙呀！」

===Original===
Again this “after all” motive creeping into the theme.﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
这「终究」的动机又潜进了这支曲子。……

===Original===
“You know Miss Peavey’s work, of course?” said Lady Constance, smiling pleasantly on her two celebrities.

===Chinese===
「皮维小姐的大作，你当然知道？」康斯坦丝夫人说，愉快地望着她这两位名流。

===Original===
“Who does not?” said Psmith courteously.

===Chinese===
「谁人不知？」皮史密斯谦逊地说。

===Original===
“Oh, *do* you?” said Miss Peavey, gratification causing her slender body to perform a sort of ladylike shimmy down its whole length. “I scarcely hoped that you would know my name. My Canadian sales have not been large.”

===Chinese===
「哦，*真的*吗？」皮维小姐说，喜得那纤瘦的身躯自上而下打了个淑女式的颤。「我几乎不敢指望你知道我的名字。我在加拿大的销路不算大。」

===Original===
“Quite large enough,” said Psmith. “I mean, of course,” he added with a paternal smile, “that, while your delicate art may not have a universal appeal in a young country, it is intensely appreciated by a small and select body of the intelligentsia.”

===Chinese===
「足够大了，」皮史密斯说。「当然，我的意思是，」他带着长辈式的微笑补充道，「你那精致的艺术，在新兴国度或许未能人见人爱，却为一小群精选的知识界人士所深深激赏。」

===Original===
And if that was not the stuff to give them, he reflected with not a little complacency, he was dashed.

===Chinese===
如果这还不算给他们一点厉害，他颇为自得地想道，那我真算白活了。

===Original===
“Your own wonderful poems,” replied Miss Peavey, “are, of course, known the whole world over. Oh, Mr. McTodd, you can hardly appreciate how I feel, meeting you. It is like the realisation of some golden dream of childhood. It is like﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「你自己的那些美妙诗篇，」皮维小姐答道，「自然是名满全球。啊，麦克托德先生，你简直无从体会我此刻的心情，得见尊颜。这就像童年某个金色梦想的实现。就像……」

===Original===
Here the Hon. Freddie Threepwood remarked suddenly that he was going to pop into the house for a whisky and soda. As he had not previously spoken, his observation had something of the effect of a voice from the tomb. The daylight was ebbing fast now, and in the shadows he had contrived to pass out of sight as well as out of mind. Miss Peavey started like an abruptly awakened somnambulist, and Psmith was at last able to release his hand, which he had begun to look on as gone beyond his control for ever. Until this fortunate interruption there had seemed no reason why Miss Peavey should not have continued to hold it till bedtime.

===Chinese===
这时，弗雷迪·思里普伍德少爷忽然发话，说他要进屋去喝杯威士忌苏打。此前他一言未发，这句插话颇有几分坟头起棺之效。天光此刻消逝得飞快，他在暮影里设法做到了既无人见、也无人想。皮维小姐像个猛然被唤醒的梦游者似的惊起，皮史密斯那只手总算得以收回——他本来已开始认为这只手从此脱离他的管辖了。若非这番侥幸的打岔，皮维小姐原可以一直握到就寝时分。

===Original===
Freddie’s departure had the effect of breaking a spell. Lord Emsworth, who had been standing perfectly still with vacant eyes, like a dog listening to a noise a long way off, came to life with a jerk.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪一走，如同破了一道魔咒。方才一直目光涣散、一动不动站着的埃姆斯沃思勋爵——活像一条狗在听远处的响动——猛然回魂。

===Original===
“I’m going to have a look at my flowers,” he announced.

===Chinese===
「我去看看我的花，」他宣布。

===Original===
“Don’t be silly, Clarence,” said his sister. “It’s much too dark to see flowers.”

===Chinese===
「别傻了，克莱伦斯，」他妹妹说。「这么黑，看什么花。」

===Original===
“I could smell ’em,” retorted his lordship argumentatively.

===Chinese===
「我闻得着，」勋爵大人好辩地回嘴。

===Original===
It seemed as if the party must break up, for already his lordship had begun to potter off, when a newcomer arrived to solidify it again.

===Chinese===
聚会眼看要散，勋爵大人已经起步踱开，忽有一位新客到来，又把它凝住了。

===Original===
“Ah, Baxter, my dear fellow,” said Lord Emsworth. “Here we are, you see.”

===Chinese===
「啊，巴克斯特，亲爱的伙计，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵说。「你看，我们在这儿呢。」

===Original===
“Mr. Baxter,” said Lady Constance, “I want you to meet Mr. McTodd.”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特先生，」康斯坦丝夫人说，「我给你引见麦克托德先生。」

===Original===
“Mr. McTodd!” said the new arrival, on a note of surprise.

===Chinese===
「麦克托德先生！」新来者语带惊讶地说。

===Original===
“Yes, he found himself able to come after all.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，他发现自己终究还是能来的。」

===Original===
“Ah!” said the Efficient Baxter.

===Chinese===
「啊！」能干的巴克斯特说。

===Original===
It occurred to Psmith as a passing thought, to which he gave no more than a momentary attention, that this spectacled and capable-looking man was gazing at him, as they shook hands, with a curious intensity. But possibly, he reflected, this was merely a species of optical illusion due to the other’s spectacles. Baxter, staring through his spectacles, often gave people the impression of possessing an eye that could pierce six inches of harveyised steel and stick out on the other side. Having registered in his consciousness the fact that he had been stared at keenly by this stranger, Psmith thought no more of the matter.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯脑中闪过一个念头，也只给了它一瞬的注意：握手之际，这位戴眼镜、精干模样的人正以一种古怪的强烈劲儿盯着他。不过他随即想道，这或许只是对方那副眼镜造成的某种视错觉。巴克斯特隔着镜片瞪人的时候，常给人一种错觉，仿佛他那只眼睛能洞穿六英寸厚的哈维钢甲板，还从另一头戳出来。他把「被这位生人狠狠盯过」这一事实在意识里登记完毕，便不再多想。

===Original===
In thus lightly dismissing the Baxterian stare, Psmith had acted injudiciously. He should have examined it more closely and made an effort to analyse it, for it was by no means without its message. It was a stare of suspicion. Vague suspicion as yet, but nevertheless suspicion. Rupert Baxter was one of those men whose chief characteristic is a disposition to suspect their fellows. He did not suspect them of this or that definite crime: he simply suspected them. He had not yet definitely accused Psmith in his mind of any specific tort or malfeasance. He merely had a nebulous feeling that he would bear watching.

===Chinese===
就这样轻轻放过了巴克斯特式的凝视，皮史密斯行事失于轻率。他本该细加审视、着力剖析一番，因为这凝视绝非没有信息量。那是一种猜疑的凝视。眼下的猜疑还模糊，但终究是猜疑。鲁珀特·巴克斯特属于这么一类人：其首要特征便是猜疑同胞的习性。他不猜疑他们干了这样或那样具体的坏事：他就是猜疑他们本身。他心里尚未明确指控皮史密斯任何具体的侵权或不端。他只有一种朦朦胧胧的感觉：此人须得盯着。

===Original===
Miss Peavey now fluttered again into the centre of things. On the arrival of Baxter she had withdrawn for a moment into the background, but she was not the woman to stay there long. She came forward holding out a small oblong book, which, with a languishing firmness, she pressed into Psmith’s hands.

===Chinese===
这时皮维小姐又翩然回到事件中央。巴克斯特来时她暂退居背景，但她不是久居人后的女人。她上前递出一本小小的长方形簿子，以一种楚楚的执拗，把它按进皮史密斯手中。

===Original===
“Could I persuade you, Mr. McTodd,” said Miss Peavey pleadingly, “to write some little thought in my autograph-book and sign it? I have a fountain-pen.”

===Chinese===
「我能否恳请您，麦克托德先生，」皮维小姐恳求道，「在我的签名簿里题写一点小小的思想，再签上名？我带着自来水笔呢。」

===Original===
Light flooded the arbour. The Efficient Baxter, who knew where everything was, had found and pressed the switch. He did this not so much to oblige Miss Peavey as to enable him to obtain a clearer view of the visitor. With each minute that passed the Efficient Baxter was finding himself more and more doubtful in his mind about this visitor.

===Chinese===
凉亭里灯光大亮。能干的巴克斯特素知万物所在，找着了开关，一按。他此举与其说是为皮维小姐效劳，不如说是要把自己这位来客看得更清楚些。时间每过一分，能干的巴克斯特心里对这位来客的疑虑便深一分。

===Original===
“There!” said Miss Peavey, welcoming the illumination.

===Chinese===
「来啦！」皮维小姐迎接光明。

===Original===
Psmith tapped his chin thoughtfully with the fountain-pen. He felt that he should have foreseen this emergency earlier. If ever there was a woman who was bound to have an autograph-book, that woman was Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯用自来水笔若有所思地敲着下巴。他觉得自己本该预见这一危机。世上若有一个注定备有签名簿的女人，那就是皮维小姐。

===Original===
“Just some little thought﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「随便一点小小的思想……」

===Original===
Psmith hesitated no longer. In a firm hand he wrote the words “Across the pale parabola of Joy﻿ ﻿…” added an unfaltering “Ralston McTodd,” and handed the book back.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯不再犹豫。他以遒劲的笔迹写下「穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线……」几个字，又笔锋不停添上「拉尔斯顿·麦克托德」，把簿子递了回去。

===Original===
“How strange,” sighed Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「多奇怪呀，」皮维小姐叹道。

===Original===
“May I look?” said Baxter, moving quickly to her side.

===Chinese===
「能看看吗？」巴克斯特说着，快步走到她身边。

===Original===
“How strange!” repeated Miss Peavey. “To think that you should have chosen that line! There are several of your more mystic passages that I meant to ask you to explain, but particularly ‘Across the pale parabola of Joy’﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「多奇怪呀！」皮维小姐又说。「你偏偏选中了这一句！您的诗里好几段玄之又玄的地方，我都想请您讲解，尤其这句『穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线』……」

===Original===
“You find it difficult to understand?”

===Chinese===
「你觉得难懂？」

===Original===
“A little, I confess.”

===Chinese===
「有一点，我不隐瞒。」

===Original===
“Well, well,” said Psmith indulgently, “perhaps I did put a bit of topspin on that one.”

===Chinese===
「好吧好吧，」皮史密斯宽容地说，「那一句，或许我确实加了点上旋。」

===Original===
“I beg your pardon?”

===Chinese===
「你说什么？」

===Original===
“I say, perhaps it is a little obscure. We must have a long chat about it﻿—later on.”

===Chinese===
「我是说，它也许有点晦涩。改日咱们长谈——长谈。」

===Original===
“Why not now?” demanded the Efficient Baxter, flashing his spectacles.

===Chinese===
「何不现在？」能干的巴克斯特追问，镜片寒光一闪。

===Original===
“I am rather tired,” said Psmith with gentle reproach, “after my journey. Fatigued. We artists﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「旅途之后，我相当疲乏，」皮史密斯轻责道。「困顿了。我们这些搞艺术的……」

===Original===
“Of course,” said Miss Peavey, with an indignant glance at the secretary. “Mr. Baxter does not understand the sensitive poetic temperament.”

===Chinese===
「当然啦，」皮维小姐说着，愤然横了秘书一眼。「巴克斯特先生不懂诗人敏感的天性。」

===Original===
“A bit unspiritual, eh?” said Psmith tolerantly. “A trifle earthy? So I thought, so I thought. One of these strong, hard men of affairs, I shouldn’t wonder.”

===Chinese===
「有点缺乏灵性，嗯？」皮史密斯宽容地说。「略带泥土气？我就说嘛，我就说嘛。大概是那种刚强干练的实务家，我猜。」

===Original===
“Shall we go and find Lord Emsworth, Mr. McTodd?” said Miss Peavey, dismissing the fermenting Baxter with a scornful look. “He wandered off just now. I suppose he is among his flowers. Flowers are very beautiful by night.”

===Chinese===
「咱们去找埃姆斯沃思勋爵好吗，麦克托德先生？」皮维小姐说着，轻蔑的一瞥把发酵中的巴克斯特打发开去。「他刚才是踱开了。我估摸他在他的花丛里。夜里的花分外美。」

===Original===
“Indeed, yes,” said Psmith. “And also by day. When I am surrounded by flowers, a sort of divine peace floods over me, and the rough, harsh world seems far away. I feel soothed, tranquil. I sometimes think, Miss Peavey, that flowers must be the souls of little children who have died in their innocence.”

===Chinese===
「正是，正是，」皮史密斯说。「白天也美。每当身处花丛，一种神圣的安宁便漫过我的全身，粗砺严酷的尘世仿佛远在天边。我感到抚慰，恬静。皮维小姐，我有时想，花朵莫不是那些天折于纯真之中的孩童的灵魂。」

===Original===
“What a beautiful thought, Mr. McTodd!” exclaimed Miss Peavey rapturously.

===Chinese===
「多美的思想啊，麦克托德先生！」皮维小姐心醉神迷地喊道。

===Original===
“Yes,” agreed Psmith. “Don’t pinch it. It’s copyright.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，」皮史密斯表示同意。「可别剽窃。有版权的。」

===Original===
The darkness swallowed them up. Lady Constance turned to the Efficient Baxter, who was brooding with furrowed brow.

===Chinese===
黑暗把他们吞没了。康斯坦丝夫人转向眉头紧锁、凝神苦思的能干的巴克斯特。

===Original===
“Charming, is he not?”

===Chinese===
「他迷人，不是吗？」

===Original===
“I beg your pardon?”

===Chinese===
「你说什么？」

===Original===
“I said I thought Mr. McTodd was charming.”

===Chinese===
「我是说麦克托德先生很迷人。」

===Original===
“Oh, quite.”

===Chinese===
「哦，相当。」

===Original===
“Completely unspoiled.”

===Chinese===
「一点没有被名声宠坏。」

===Original===
“Oh, decidedly.”

===Chinese===
「哦，的确。」

===Original===
“I am so glad that he was able to come after all. That telegram he sent this afternoon cancelling his visit seemed so curt and final.”

===Chinese===
「他终究还是能来，我真高兴。他今天下午发来的取消做客的电报，看着那么生硬、那么决绝。」

===Original===
“So I thought it.”

===Chinese===
「我也这么想。」

===Original===
“Almost as if he had taken offence at something and decided to have nothing to do with us.”

===Chinese===
「简直像他着了什么恼，打定了主意再不跟我们往来似的。」

===Original===
“Quite.”

===Chinese===
「正是。」

===Original===
Lady Constance shivered delicately. A cool breeze had sprung up. She drew her wrap more closely about her shapely shoulders, and began to walk to the house. Baxter did not accompany her. The moment she had gone he switched off the light and sat down, chin in hand. That massive brain was working hard.

===Chinese===
康斯坦丝夫人幽雅地一颤。晚风凉起来了。她把披肩往匀称的双肩上拢了拢，举步向宅子走去。巴克斯特没有陪她。她前脚一走，他后脚便关了灯，坐下来，手托着下巴。那颗硕大的脑袋开始奋力运转起来。

## VIII Confidences on the Lake / 湖上剖白

### I / I

===Original===
“Miss Halliday,” announced the Efficient Baxter, removing another letter from its envelope and submitting it to a swift, keen scrutiny, “arrives at about three today. She is catching the twelve-fifty train.”

===Chinese===
「哈利迪小姐，」能干的巴克斯特一边宣布，一边从信封里又抽出一封信，施以迅捷而犀利的检视，「今天三点左右到。她坐十二点五十的车。」

===Original===
He placed the letter on the pile beside his plate; and, having decapitated an egg, peered sharply into its interior as if hoping to surprise guilty secrets. For it was the breakfast hour, and the members of the house party, scattered up and down the long table, were fortifying their tissues against another day. An agreeable scent of bacon floated over the scene like a benediction.

===Chinese===
他把信放在餐盘旁的信堆上；随即削去一只鸡蛋的顶盖，朝蛋内锐利地窥视，仿佛指望窥出什么见不得人的秘密。此时正是早餐时分，与会的这一家人沿长桌错落而坐，正为又一日的操劳加固各自的血肉。一片令人愉快的腌肉香气浮荡在席面上空，宛如祝福。

===Original===
Lord Emsworth looked up from the seed catalogue in which he was immersed. For some time past his enjoyment of the meal had been marred by a vague sense of something missing, and now he knew what it was.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵从埋头研读的种子目录上抬起头来。一段时间以来，隐隐觉得缺了点什么，败坏着他用餐的兴致，此刻他弄明白缺的是什么了。

===Original===
“Coffee!” he said, not violently, but in the voice of a good man oppressed. “I want coffee. Why have I no coffee? Constance, my dear, I should have coffee. Why have I none?”

===Chinese===
「咖啡！」他说，声音不凶，却是好人受欺的腔调。「我要咖啡。我的咖啡呢？康斯坦丝，亲爱的，我该有咖啡的。我怎么没有？」

===Original===
“I’m sure I gave you some,” said Lady Constance, brightly presiding over the beverages at the other end of the table.

===Chinese===
「我明明给你倒过了，」康斯坦丝夫人说着，在桌子另一头精神地主持着茶饮。

===Original===
“Then where is it?” demanded his lordship clinchingly.

===Chinese===
「那它上哪儿去了？」勋爵大人一句封喉。

===Original===
Baxter﻿—almost regretfully, it seemed﻿—gave the egg a clean bill of health, and turned in his able way to cope with this domestic problem.

===Chinese===
巴克斯特——似乎还颇为惋惜地——给那只鸡蛋开了健康证明，随即以他精明干练的方式转而处理这项家政难题。

===Original===
“Your coffee is behind the catalogue you are reading, Lord Emsworth. You propped the catalogue against your cup.”

===Chinese===
「您的咖啡在您正读的那本目录后头，埃姆斯沃思勋爵。您把目录倚在杯子上了。」

===Original===
“Did I? Did I? Why, so I did! Bless my soul!” His lordship, relieved, took an invigorating sip. “What were you saying just then, my dear fellow?”

===Chinese===
「是吗？是吗？咦，可不是嘛！老天爷！」勋爵大人如释重负，提神地啜了一口。「你方才说什么来着，亲爱的伙计？」

===Original===
“I have had a letter from Miss Halliday,” said Baxter. “She writes that she is catching the twelve-fifty train at Paddington, which means that she should arrive at Market Blandings at about three.”

===Chinese===
「我收到了哈利迪小姐的信，」巴克斯特说。「她写信说坐十二点五十的帕丁顿班车，那就是三点上下到布兰丁斯集镇。」

===Original===
“Who,” asked Miss Peavey, in a low, thrilling voice, ceasing for a moment to peck at her plate of kedgeree, “is Miss Halliday?”

===Chinese===
「哪位，」皮维小姐用低沉激越的嗓音问，暂停了对那盘鸡蛋鱼饭的啄食，「是哈利迪小姐？」

===Original===
“The exact question I was about to ask myself,” said Lord Emsworth. “Baxter, my dear fellow, who is Miss Halliday?”

===Chinese===
「这正是我正要自问的问题，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵说。「巴克斯特，亲爱的伙计，哈利迪小姐是哪位？」

===Original===
Baxter, with a stifled sigh, was about to refresh his employer’s memory, when Psmith anticipated him. Psmith had been consuming toast and marmalade with his customary languid grace and up till now had firmly checked all attempts to engage him in conversation.

===Chinese===
巴克斯特强压下一声叹息，正要替雇主刷新记忆，皮史密斯抢了先。皮史密斯一直以他惯常的慵雅风度用着吐司配橘子酱，到此刻为止坚决抵制了一切攀谈的企图。

===Original===
“Miss Halliday,” he said, “is a very old and valued friend of mine. We two have, so to speak, pulled the gowans fine. I had been hoping to hear that she had been sighted on the horizon.”

===Chinese===
「哈利迪小姐，」他说，「是我的一位极老的、极可珍视的朋友。我们两个，可以说，一起采过雏菊。我正巴望着听见她出现在地平线上的消息呢。」

===Original===
The effect of these words on two of the company was somewhat remarkable. Baxter, hearing them, gave such a violent start that he spilled half the contents of his cup: and Freddie, who had been flitting like a butterfly among the dishes on the sideboard and had just decided to help himself to scrambled eggs, deposited a liberal spoonful on the carpet, where it was found and salvaged a moment later by Lady Constance’s spaniel.

===Chinese===
这番话在席上两位听者身上产生的效果颇为可观。巴克斯特一听，惊得猛一哆嗦，半杯东西泼了出去：弗雷迪本来正像蝴蝶似的在餐边柜的菜肴间翩跹，刚决计自己盛点炒蛋，一大勺失手扣在了地毯上，片刻后被康斯坦丝夫人的长毛垂耳狗发现并抢救。

===Original===
Psmith did not observe these phenomena, for he had returned to his toast and marmalade. He thus missed encountering perhaps the keenest glance that had ever come through Rupert Baxter’s spectacles. It was not a protracted glance, but while it lasted it was like the ray from an oxyacetylene blowpipe.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯没有留意这些现象，因为他已回到吐司配橘子酱上去了。他由此错过了也许是鲁珀特·巴克斯特那副镜片后射出过的最锋利的一瞥。那一瞥并不长，但在它持续期间，宛如氧乙炔吹管的射线。

===Original===
“A friend of yours?” said Lord Emsworth. “Indeed? Of course, Baxter, I remember now. Miss Halliday is the young lady who is coming to catalogue the library.”

===Chinese===
「你的朋友？」埃姆斯沃思勋爵说。「真的？当然啦，巴克斯特，我想起来了。哈利迪小姐就是来给藏书室编目的那位小姐。」

===Original===
“What a delightful task!” cooed Miss Peavey. “To live among the stored-up thoughts of dead and gone genius!”

===Chinese===
「多美妙的差事呀！」皮维小姐咕咕地说。「住在储存着逝去天才们思想宝藏的地方！」

===Original===
“You had better go down and meet her, my dear fellow,” said Lord Emsworth. “At the station, you know,” he continued, clarifying his meaning. “She will be glad to see you.”

===Chinese===
「你最好下山去接接她，亲爱的伙计，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵说。「到车站去，明白吗，」他继续阐明己意。「她会乐意见到你的。」

===Original===
“I was about to suggest it myself,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「我正想自告奋勇呢，」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
“Though why the library needs cataloguing,” said his lordship, returning to a problem which still vexed his soul when he had leisure to give a thought to it, “I can’t﻿ ﻿… However﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「不过藏书室要编什么目，」勋爵大人回到那个一有闲空便来折磨他的问题上，「我实在……然而……」

===Original===
He finished his coffee and rose from the table. A stray shaft of sunlight had fallen provocatively on his bald head, and sunshine always made him restive.

===Chinese===
他喝完咖啡，离席而起。一缕顽皮的阳光正落在他那光头上，而阳光向来叫他坐立不安。

===Original===
“Are you going to your flowers, Lord Emsworth?” asked Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「您要去您的花那儿吗，埃姆斯沃思勋爵？」皮维小姐问。

===Original===
“Eh? What? Yes. Oh, yes. Going to have a look at those lobelias.”

===Chinese===
「嗯？什么？是啊。哦，是。去看看那些半边莲。」

===Original===
“I will accompany you, if I may,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「我陪您同去，如果可以的话，」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
“Eh? Why, certainly, certainly.”

===Chinese===
「嗯？哦，当然，当然。」

===Original===
“I have always held,” said Psmith, “that there is no finer tonic than a good look at a lobelia immediately after breakfast. Doctors, I believe, recommend it.”

===Chinese===
「我一向主张，」皮史密斯说，「早餐之后好好看一眼半边莲，是天下第一补剂。我相信，医生们都这么推荐。」

===Original===
“Oh, I say,” said Freddie hastily, as he reached the door, “can I have a couple of words with you a bit later on?”

===Chinese===
「哦，我说，」弗雷迪赶到门口，匆匆说，「待会儿我能跟你说两句话吗？」

===Original===
“A thousand if you wish it,” said Psmith. “You will find me somewhere out there in the great open spaces where men are men.”

===Chinese===
「一千句也随你，」皮史密斯说。「到外面那片男人顶天立地的大旷野里找我吧。」

===Original===
He included the entire company in a benevolent smile, and left the room.

===Chinese===
他向满座众人报以慈祥的微笑，出了房门。

===Original===
“How charming he is!” sighed Miss Peavey. “Don’t you think so, Mr. Baxter?”

===Chinese===
「他多迷人呀！」皮维小姐叹道。「你不觉得吗，巴克斯特先生？」

===Original===
The Efficient Baxter seemed for a moment to find some difficulty in replying.

===Chinese===
能干的巴克斯特一时似乎有些难于作答。

===Original===
“Oh, very,” he said, but not heartily.

===Chinese===
「哦，相当，」他说，但毫无热忱。

===Original===
“And such a *soul*! It shines on that wonderful brow of his, doesn’t it?”

===Chinese===
「而且多有*灵魂*！那灵魂就在他那美妙的额头上放光，不是吗？」

===Original===
“He has a good forehead,” said Lady Constance. “But I wish he wouldn’t wear his hair so short. Somehow it makes him seem unlike a poet.”

===Chinese===
「他额头生得好，」康斯坦丝夫人说。「不过我倒希望他别把头发剪得这么短。不知怎么，这叫他不像个诗人。」

===Original===
Freddie, alarmed, swallowed a mouthful of scrambled egg.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪 alarmed 地咽下一口炒蛋。

===Original===
“Oh, he’s a poet all right,” he said hastily.

===Chinese===
「哦，他是诗人，没错的，」他急忙说。

===Original===
“Well, really, Freddie,” said Lady Constance, piqued, “I think we hardly need *you* to tell us that.”

===Chinese===
「哎呀，真是的，弗雷迪，」康斯坦丝夫人恼了，「我想这事儿还*轮不到*你来告诉我们。」

===Original===
“No, no, of course. But what I mean is, in spite of his wearing his hair short, you know.”

===Chinese===
「不不，当然。可我的意思是，尽管他头发剪得短，你要知道。」

===Original===
“I ventured to speak to him of that yesterday,” said Miss Peavey, “and he said he rather expected to be wearing it even shorter very soon.”

===Chinese===
「我昨天冒昧同他提起过这个，」皮维小姐说，「他说他料想很快就会剪得更短呢。」

===Original===
“Freddie!” cried Lady Constance with asperity. “What *are* you doing?”

===Chinese===
「弗雷迪！」康斯坦丝夫人厉声喊道。「你*到底*在干什么？」

===Original===
A brown lake of tea was filling the portion of the tablecloth immediately opposite the Hon. Frederick Threepwood. Like the Efficient Baxter a few minutes before, sudden emotion had caused him to upset his cup.

===Chinese===
一泓棕色的茶水之湖，正在弗雷德里克·思里普伍德少爷正对面的桌布上漫溢。同几分钟前的能干巴克斯特一样，骤起的情绪叫他打翻了茶杯。

### II / II

===Original===
The scrutiny of his lordship’s lobelias had palled upon Psmith at a fairly early stage in the proceedings, and he was sitting on the terrace wall enjoying a meditative cigarette when Freddie found him.

===Chinese===
对勋爵大人的半边莲的检视，皮史密斯在程序颇早的阶段便兴味索然了，此时他正坐在露台墙上，享受一支冥想的香烟，被弗雷迪寻着了。

===Original===
“Ah, Comrade Threepwood,” said Psmith, “welcome to Blandings Castle! You said something about wishing to have speech with me, if I remember rightly?”

===Chinese===
「啊，思里普伍德同志，」皮史密斯说，「欢迎来到布兰丁斯城堡！如果我没记错，你说有话要同我讲？」

===Original===
The Hon. Freddie shot a nervous glance about him, and seated himself on the wall.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪少爷神经质地四下扫了一眼，在墙上坐下。

===Original===
“I say,” he said, “I wish you wouldn’t say things like that.”

===Chinese===
「我说，」他说，「你别说那种话行不行。」

===Original===
“Like what, Comrade Threepwood?”

===Chinese===
「哪种话，思里普伍德同志？」

===Original===
“What you said to the Peavey woman.”

===Chinese===
「你跟皮维那女人说的那些。」

===Original===
“I recollect having a refreshing chat with Miss Peavey yesterday afternoon,” said Psmith, “but I cannot recall saying anything calculated to bring the blush of shame to the cheek of modesty. What observation of mine was it that meets with your censure?”

===Chinese===
「我记得昨天下午同皮维小姐有过一段提神的闲谈，」皮史密斯说，「但我不记得说过什么足以令谦逊的面颊羞红的话。我的哪一句高见蒙你指摘了？」

===Original===
“Why, that stuff about expecting to wear your hair shorter. If you’re going to go about saying that sort of thing﻿—well, dash it, you might just as well give the whole bally show away at once and have done with it.”

===Chinese===
「嗨，就是那个头发要剪得更短的鬼话。你要是打算到处说这种话——得了，见鬼，你还不如干脆把整场戏一锅端了痛快。」

===Original===
Psmith nodded gravely.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯庄重地点头。

===Original===
“Your generous heat, Comrade Threepwood, is not unjustified. It was undoubtedly an error of judgment. If I have a fault﻿—which I am not prepared to admit﻿—it is a perhaps ungentlemanly desire to pull that curious female’s leg. A stronger man than myself might well find it hard to battle against the temptation. However, now that you have called it to my notice, it shall not occur again. In future I will moderate the persiflage. Cheer up, therefore, Comrade Threepwood, and let us see that merry smile of yours, of which I hear such good reports.”

===Chinese===
「你的义愤，思里普伍德同志，不是没有道理。那无疑是一次失算。我若有什么毛病——这一点我不准备承认——便是一种或许有失绅士风度的、想捉弄那位古怪女性的欲望。比我更强的男子恐怕也难抗拒这份诱惑。不过，既然你已提请我注意，下不为例。今后我将节制打趣。所以高兴起来吧，思里普伍德同志，让我们看看你那久闻大名的莞尔一笑。」

===Original===
The appeal failed to alleviate Freddie’s gloom. He smote morosely at a fly which had settled on his furrowed brow.

===Chinese===
这番呼吁未能驱散弗雷迪的阴霾。他闷闷地拍向一只落在他皱眉上的苍蝇。

===Original===
“I’m getting as jumpy as a cat,” he said.

===Chinese===
「我最近神经衰弱得像只猫，」他说。

===Original===
“Fight against this unmanly weakness,” urged Psmith. “As far as I can see, everything is going along nicely.”

===Chinese===
「要同这有失男子气概的软弱作斗争，」皮史密斯敦促道。「依我看，一切进展顺利。」

===Original===
“I’m not so sure. I believe that blighter Baxter suspects something.”

===Chinese===
「我可不敢说。我疑心巴克斯特那小子嗅出了什么。」

===Original===
“What do you think he suspects?”

===Chinese===
「你疑心他嗅出了什么？」

===Original===
“Why, that there’s something fishy about you.”

===Chinese===
「嗐，疑心你这人有点蹊跷呗。」

===Original===
Psmith winced.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯眉头一皱。

===Original===
“I would be infinitely obliged to you, Comrade Threepwood, if you would not use that particular adjective. It awakens old memories, all very painful. But let us go more deeply into this matter, for you interest me strangely. Why do you think that cheery old Baxter, a delightful personality if ever I met one, suspects me?”

===Chinese===
「思里普伍德同志，你若能不用那个特别的形容词，我将感激不尽。它唤醒了旧日回忆，全是痛苦的。不过咱们把这桩事往深里谈谈，因为你让我产生了莫大的兴趣。你凭什么认为那位快活的老巴克斯特——要说我见过讨人喜欢的人，他算一个——疑心我？」

===Original===
“It’s the way he looks at you.”

===Chinese===
「看他瞧你的那副眼神。」

===Original===
“I know what you mean, but I attribute no importance to it. As far as I have been able to ascertain during my brief visit, he looks at everybody and everything in precisely the same way. Only last night at dinner I observed him glaring with keen mistrust at about as blameless and innocent a plate of clear soup as was ever dished up. He then proceeded to shovel it down with quite undisguised relish. So possibly you are all wrong about his motive for looking at me like that. It may be admiration.”

===Chinese===
「我知道你的意思，但我不以为意。就我这趟短期做客的观察，他瞧谁都那样，瞧什么都那样。就在昨晚的晚餐桌上，我还看见他满含猜忌地瞪着一盘清炖汤，那清汤之清白无辜，天下无双。随后他便毫不掩饰、津津有味地把它扒下了肚。所以你对那种眼神的动机，或许全然想岔了。没准是仰慕。」

===Original===
“Well, I don’t like it.”

===Chinese===
「反正我不喜欢。」

===Original===
“Nor, from an aesthetic point of view, do I. But we must bear these things manfully. We must remind ourselves that it is Baxter’s misfortune rather than his fault that he looks like a dyspeptic lizard.”

===Chinese===
「从审美角度讲，我也不喜欢。但咱们须得硬汉子似的扛住。须得提醒自己：他长相活像一条消化不良的蜥蜴，是他的不幸，而非他的过错。」

===Original===
Freddie was not to be consoled. His gloom deepened.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪无可安慰。阴霾愈重了。

===Original===
“And it isn’t only Baxter.”

===Chinese===
「还不光是巴克斯特。」

===Original===
“What else is on your mind?”

===Chinese===
「你心里还有别的事？」

===Original===
“The whole atmosphere of the place is getting rummy, if you know what I mean.” He bent towards Psmith and whispered pallidly. “I say, I believe that new housemaid is a detective!”

===Chinese===
「这地方整个的空气都透着邪性，你明白我的意思。」他凑向皮史密斯，面无人色地耳语。「我说，我疑心那个新来的女仆是个侦探！」

===Original===
Psmith eyed him patiently.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯耐心地端详他。

===Original===
“Which new housemaid, Comrade Threepwood? Brooding, as I do, pretty tensely all the time on deep and wonderful subjects, I have little leisure to keep tab on the domestic staff. *Is* there a new housemaid?”

===Chinese===
「哪个新来的女仆，思里普伍德同志？我整日价紧张地沉思着高深玄妙的主题，实在抽不出闲工夫去盯家仆花名册。*当真*有位新女仆？」

===Original===
“Yes. Susan, her name is.”

===Chinese===
「有。叫苏珊。」

===Original===
“Susan? Susan? That sounds all right. Just the name a real housemaid would have.”

===Chinese===
「苏珊？苏珊？这名字听着就正。正是一位真女仆该有的名字。」

===Original===
“Did you ever,” demanded Freddie earnestly, “see a real housemaid sweep under a bureau?”

===Chinese===
「你可曾，」弗雷迪正色质问，「见过哪位真女仆去扫写字台底下？」

===Original===
“Does she?”

===Chinese===
「她扫了？」

===Original===
“Caught her at it in my room this morning.”

===Chinese===
「今天早上在我房里逮了个正着。」

===Original===
“But isn’t it a trifle far-fetched to imagine that she is a detective? Why should she be a detective?”

===Chinese===
「可要想象她是个侦探，未免有点牵强吧？她凭什么当侦探？」

===Original===
“Well, I’ve seen such a dashed lot of films where the housemaid or the parlourmaid or whatnot were detectives. Makes a fellow uneasy.”

===Chinese===
「嗨，我看的那些电影里，女仆呀客厅女仆呀什么的当侦探的，可海了去了。看得人心里发毛。」

===Original===
“Fortunately,” said Psmith, “there is no necessity to remain in a state of doubt. I can give you an unfailing method by means of which you may discover if she is what she would have us believe her.”

===Chinese===
「所幸，」皮史密斯说，「大可不必停留在疑云之中。我可以授你一条万无一失的法子，用来查明她究竟是不是她所自称的那种人。」

===Original===
“What’s that?”

===Chinese===
「什么法子？」

===Original===
“Kiss her.”

===Chinese===
「吻她。」

===Original===
“Kiss her!”

===Chinese===
「吻她！」

===Original===
“Precisely. Go to her and say, ‘Susan, you’re a very pretty girl﻿ ﻿…’ ”

===Chinese===
「正是。走到她跟前说：『苏珊，你是个怪俊的姑娘……』」

===Original===
“But she isn’t.”

===Chinese===
「可她不俊啊。」

===Original===
“We will assume, for purposes of argument, that she is. Go to her and say, ‘Susan, you are a very pretty girl. What would you do if I were to kiss you?’ If she is a detective, she will reply, ‘How dare you, sir!’ or, possibly, more simply, ‘Sir!’ Whereas if she is the genuine housemaid I believe her to be and only sweeps under bureaux out of pure zeal, she will giggle and remark, ‘Oh, don’t be silly, sir!’ You appreciate the distinction?”

===Chinese===
「咱们且为论证起见，假定她俊。走到她跟前说：『苏珊，你是个怪俊的姑娘。要是我吻你，你会怎么样？』她若是个侦探，便会答：『你好大胆，先生！』或者，也许，更简洁些：『先生！』反之，她若是我所相信的 bona fide（如假包换的）女仆，扫写字台底下纯粹出于敬业热忱，她便会咯咯一乐，说：『哦，别傻啦，先生！』个中分别，你品出来了吧？」

===Original===
“How do you know?”

===Chinese===
「你怎么知道的？」

===Original===
“My grandmother told me, Comrade Threepwood. My advice to you, if the state of doubt you are in is affecting your enjoyment of life, is to put the matter to the test at the earliest convenient opportunity.”

===Chinese===
「我祖母告诉我的，思里普伍德同志。你若因为这团疑云败坏了生活兴致，我的忠告是：尽早觅个方便时机，付诸一试。」

===Original===
“I’ll think it over,” said Freddie dubiously.

===Chinese===
「我考虑考虑，」弗雷迪将信将疑地说。

===Original===
Silence fell upon him for a space, and Psmith was well content to have it so. He had no specific need of Freddie’s prattle to help him enjoy the pleasant sunshine and the scent of Angus McAllister’s innumerable flowers. Presently, however, his companion was off again. But now there was a different note in his voice. Alarm seemed to have given place to something which appeared to be embarrassment. He coughed several times, and his neatly-shod feet, writhing in self-conscious circles, scraped against the wall.

===Chinese===
一阵沉默落到他头上，皮史密斯乐得其所。要享受这宜人的阳光和安格斯·麦卡利斯特那数不尽的花香，原无须弗雷迪的聒噪帮忙。然而不多时，他的同伴又开腔了。只是这回嗓音里换了调门。惊惶似乎让位给了某种近似难为情的情绪。他干咳了几声，那双擦得锃亮的脚在墙边扭出自我意识的圆圈。

===Original===
“I say!”

===Chinese===
「我说！」

===Original===
“You have our ear once more, Comrade Threepwood,” said Psmith politely.

===Chinese===
「你的耳朵再次归你了，思里普伍德同志，」皮史密斯彬彬有礼地说。

===Original===
“I say, what I really came out here to talk about was something else. I say, are you really a pal of Miss Halliday’s?”

===Chinese===
「我说，我上这儿来真正想谈的是别的事。我说，你真是哈利迪小姐的伙伴？」

===Original===
“Assuredly. Why?”

===Chinese===
「当然。怎么？」

===Original===
“I say!” A rosy blush mantled the Hon. Freddie’s young cheek. “I say, I wish you would put in a word for me, then.”

===Chinese===
「我说！」一抹玫瑰色的红晕漫上弗雷迪少爷年轻的脸颊。「我说，那请你替我美言几句吧。」

===Original===
“Put in a word for you?”

===Chinese===
「替你美言几句？」

===Original===
Freddie gulped.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪一口气咽了下去。

===Original===
“I love her, dash it!”

===Chinese===
「我爱她，见鬼！」

===Original===
“A noble emotion,” said Psmith courteously. “When did you feel it coming on?”

===Chinese===
「高尚的情感，」皮史密斯谦和地说。「什么时候开始发作的？」

===Original===
“I’ve been in love with her for months. But she won’t look at me.”

===Chinese===
「我爱了她好几个月了。可她连瞧都不瞧我一眼。」

===Original===
“That, of course,” agreed Psmith, “must be a disadvantage. Yes, I should imagine that that would stick the gaff into the course of true love to no small extent.”

===Chinese===
「那当然，」皮史密斯附和道，「确属不利。是啊，我想得出，这给爱情正途设下不小的一根刺。」

===Original===
“I mean, won’t take me seriously, and all that. Laughs at me, don’t you know, when I propose. What would you do?”

===Chinese===
「我是说，她不拿我当正经人，什么的。我一求婚她就笑，你懂的。你说怎么办？」

===Original===
“I should stop proposing,” said Psmith, having given the matter thought.

===Chinese===
「我就不求了，」皮史密斯思考之后说。

===Original===
“But I can’t.”

===Chinese===
「可我办不到啊。」

===Original===
“Tut, tut!” said Psmith severely. “And, in case the expression is new to you, what I mean is ‘Pooh, pooh!’ Just say to yourself, ‘From now on I will not start proposing until after lunch.’ That done, it will be an easy step to do no proposing during the afternoon. And by degrees you will find that you can give it up altogether. Once you have conquered the impulse for the after-breakfast proposal, the rest will be easy. The first one of the day is always the hardest to drop.”

===Chinese===
「啧啧！」皮史密斯严词道。「怕你没听过这说法，我给你翻译一下，意思就是『呸呸！』你只消对自己说：『从今往后，午饭之前我绝不开始求婚。』做到这一步，下一步就容易了——下午也不求。循序渐进，你便会发现可以把这毛病整个戒掉。一旦征服了早餐后求婚的冲动，其余不在话下。一天里的头一回，永远最难戒。」

===Original===
“I believe she thinks me a mere butterfly,” said Freddie, who had not been listening to this most valuable homily.

===Chinese===
「我估摸她拿我当只花蝴蝶，」弗雷迪说，方才那段金玉良言他一个字也没听进去。

===Original===
Psmith slid down from the wall and stretched himself.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯从墙上滑下来，舒展了一下筋骨。

===Original===
“Why,” he said, “are butterflies so often described as ‘mere’? I have heard them so called a hundred times, and I cannot understand the reason.﻿ ﻿… Well, it would, no doubt, be both interesting and improving to go into the problem, but at this point, Comrade Threepwood, I leave you. I would brood.”

===Chinese===
「为什么，」他说，「蝴蝶总被形容成『只』？我听人这么叫过一百回，始终不解其故。……好吧，深入探讨这个问题，无疑既有趣又有益，可是到此为止吧，思里普伍德同志，我先失陪。我要沉思去。」

===Original===
“Yes, but, I say, will you?”

===Chinese===
「是，可是，我说，你肯不肯？」

===Original===
“Will I what?”

===Chinese===
「肯什么？」

===Original===
“Put in a word for me?”

===Chinese===
「替我美言？」

===Original===
“If,” said Psmith, “the subject crops up in the course of the chitchat, I shall be delighted to spread myself with no little vim on the theme of your fine qualities.”

===Chinese===
「若是，」皮史密斯说，「闲谈间话题恰好转到那儿，我将以十足的兴趣，把你的种种美德大大铺陈一番，不胜乐意之至。」

===Original===
He melted away into the shrubbery, just in time to avoid Miss Peavey, who broke in on Freddie’s meditations a moment later and kept him company till lunch.

===Chinese===
他悄然隐入灌木丛，正赶上躲开皮维小姐——她片刻后便闯进了弗雷迪的沉思，陪他一直坐到午饭。

### III / III

===Original===
The twelve-fifty train drew up with a grinding of brakes at the platform of Market Blandings, and Psmith, who had been whiling away the time of waiting by squandering money which he could ill afford on the slot-machine which supplied butterscotch, turned and submitted it to a grave scrutiny. Eve Halliday got out of a third-class compartment.

===Chinese===
十二点五十的火车伴着一阵刹车的锐响停靠在布兰丁斯集镇站台。皮史密斯方才一直在用他负担不起的钱币喂那台供应奶油糖的自动售货机，借以消磨候车时光，此刻转过身，对火车施以庄严的检视。伊芙·哈利迪从一节三等车厢里走了下来。

===Original===
“Welcome to our village, Miss Halliday,” said Psmith, advancing.

===Chinese===
「欢迎光临敝村，哈利迪小姐，」皮史密斯迎上前去。

===Original===
Eve regarded him with frank astonishment.

===Chinese===
伊芙坦然地愕然打量他。

===Original===
“What are you doing here?” she asked.

===Chinese===
「你在这儿干什么？」她问。

===Original===
“Lord Emsworth was kind enough to suggest that, as we were such old friends, I should come down in the car and meet you.”

===Chinese===
「埃姆斯沃思勋爵一片好意，说是咱们既是这么老的朋友，该坐汽车下来接你。」

===Original===
“Are we old friends?”

===Chinese===
「咱们是老朋友？」

===Original===
“Surely. Have you forgotten all those happy days in London?”

===Chinese===
「当然是。伦敦那些快乐的日子，你都忘了？」

===Original===
“There was only one.”

===Chinese===
「拢共就一天。」

===Original===
“True. But think how many meetings we crammed into it.”

===Chinese===
「不错。可你想想，咱们往那一天里塞了多少次见面。」

===Original===
“Are you staying at the castle?”

===Chinese===
「你在城堡住着？」

===Original===
“Yes. And what is more, I am the life and soul of the party. Have you anything in the shape of luggage?”

===Chinese===
「是啊。而且，我不但住着，还是这儿的 party 的灵魂与核心。你可有形似行李的物件？」

===Original===
“I nearly always take luggage when I am going to stay a month or so in the country. It’s at the back somewhere.”

===Chinese===
「下乡住上个把月，我照例总带行李的。在车尾哪儿的。」

===Original===
“I will look after it. You will find the car outside. If you care to go and sit in it, I will join you in a moment. And, lest the time hangs heavy on your hands, take this. Butterscotch. Delicious, and, so I understand, wholesome. I bought it specially for you.”

===Chinese===
「我去照料。车就在外头。你若乐意先去坐着，我片刻便来。免得你手上时光难熬，拿着这个。奶油糖。可口，而且，据我了解，滋补。特地为你买的。」

===Original===
A few minutes later, having arranged for the trunk to be taken to the castle, Psmith emerged from the station and found Eve drinking in the beauties of the town of Market Blandings.

===Chinese===
几分钟后，皮史密斯安排妥当衣箱运往城堡事宜，走出车站，见伊芙正在饱览布兰丁斯集镇这座小镇的风华。

===Original===
“What a delightful old place,” she said as they drove off. “I almost wish I lived here.”

===Chinese===
「多可爱的老地方，」汽车开动时她说。「我几乎想住在这儿了。」

===Original===
“During the brief period of my stay at the castle,” said Psmith, “the same thought has occurred to me. It is the sort of place where one feels that one could gladly settle down into a peaceful retirement and grow a honey-coloured beard.” He looked at her with solemn admiration. “Women are wonderful,” he said.

===Chinese===
「在城堡小住的短短期间，」皮史密斯说，「同一念头也掠过我心头。这是那种让人觉得可以欣然安居、颐养天年、留一把蜜色胡子的地方。」他以肃穆的倾慕望着她。「女人真是奇妙，」他说。

===Original===
“And why, Mr. Bones, are women wonderful?” asked Eve.

===Chinese===
「那么请问，博恩斯先生，女人为何奇妙呀？」伊芙问。

===Original===
“I was thinking at the moment of your appearance. You have just stepped off the train after a four-hour journey, and you are as fresh and blooming as﻿—if I may coin a simile﻿—a rose. How do you do it? When I arrived I was deep in alluvial deposits, and have only just managed to scrape them off.”

===Chinese===
「我此刻正想着你的容颜。四个钟头的火车坐下来，你却鲜嫩得像——恕我即兴打个比方——一朵玫瑰。你怎么做到的？我到的那天，一身冲积土没到大腿，好不容易才刮干净。」

===Original===
“When did you arrive?”

===Chinese===
「你什么时候到的？」

===Original===
“On the evening of the day on which I met you.”

===Chinese===
「遇上你的当天晚上。」

===Original===
“But it’s so extraordinary. That you should be here, I mean. I was wondering if I should ever see you again.” Eve coloured a little, and went on rather hurriedly. “I mean, it seems so strange that we should always be meeting like this.”

===Chinese===
「可这就太巧了。我是说，你居然在这儿。我还在想这辈子能不能再见到你呢。」伊芙微微一红，又急急接下去。「我是说，咱们老是这么邂逅，也太玄了。」

===Original===
“Fate, probably,” said Psmith. “I hope it isn’t going to spoil your visit?”

===Chinese===
「多半是缘分，」皮史密斯说。「但愿它别搅了你的这趟差事？」

===Original===
“Oh, no.”

===Chinese===
「哦，不会。」

===Original===
“I could have done with a trifle more emphasis on the last word,” said Psmith gently. “Forgive me for criticising your methods of voice production, but surely you can see how much better it would have sounded spoken thus: ‘Oh, *no*!’ ”

===Chinese===
「末一个字若能再多三分气力就好了，」皮史密斯温和地说。「恕我挑剔你的发声法——可你难道看不出，这么说着要好听得多么：『哦，*不*！』」

===Original===
Eve laughed.

===Chinese===
伊芙笑了。

===Original===
“Very well, then,” she said. “Oh, *no*!”

===Chinese===
「那好，」她说。「哦，*不*！」

===Original===
“Much better,” said Psmith. “Much better.”

===Chinese===
「好得多，」皮史密斯说。「好得多。」

===Original===
He began to see that it was going to be difficult to introduce a eulogy of the Hon. Freddie Threepwood into this conversation.

===Chinese===
他开始看出，要把一段对弗雷迪·思里普伍德少爷的颂词塞进这场谈话，恐怕有难度。

===Original===
“I’m very glad you’re here,” said Eve, resuming the talk after a slight pause. “Because, as a matter of fact, I’m feeling just the least bit nervous.”

===Chinese===
「你在这儿我太高兴了，」伊芙稍停之后重新开腔。「因为，实话说，我这会儿正有那么一丁点儿紧张。」

===Original===
“Nervous? Why?”

===Chinese===
「紧张？为何？」

===Original===
“This is my first visit to a place of this size.” The car had turned in at the big stone gates, and they were bowling smoothly up the winding drive. Through an avenue of trees to the right the great bulk of the castle had just appeared, grey and imposing against the sky. The afternoon sun glittered on the lake beyond it. “Is everything very stately?”

===Chinese===
「这么大的宅门，我头一回登门。」汽车已拐进大石门，正平稳地驶上蜿蜒车道。透过右首的林荫道，城堡巨大的轮廓刚刚显现，灰蒙蒙地巍然衬在天际。午后的阳光在城堡那头的湖面上闪烁。「里头是不是处处都很气派？」

===Original===
“Not at all. We are very homely folk, we of Blandings Castle. We go about, simple and unaffected, dropping gracious words all over the place. Lord Emsworth didn’t overawe you, did he?”

===Chinese===
「一点也不。我们布兰丁斯城堡都是极居家的人。我们简朴无华地四处走动，把和蔼的话撒得满地都是。埃姆斯沃思勋爵没吓着你吧？」

===Original===
“Oh, he’s a dear. And, of course, I know Freddie quite well.”

===Chinese===
「哦，他是个宝贝人。而且，弗雷迪我当然也熟得很。」

===Original===
