Psmith nodded. If she knew Freddie quite well, there was naturally no need to talk about him. He did not talk about him, therefore.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯点头。她既认识弗雷迪，自然无须再谈他。于是他便不谈他。

===Original===
“Have you known Lord Emsworth long?” asked Eve.

===Chinese===
「你认识埃姆斯沃思勋爵很久了？」伊芙问。

===Original===
“I met him for the first time the day I met you.”

===Chinese===
「认识你的那天，头一回认识他。」

===Original===
“Good gracious!” Eve stared. “And he invited you to the castle?”

===Chinese===
「天哪！」伊芙瞪大了眼。「他就这样请你进城堡了？」

===Original===
Psmith smoothed his waistcoat.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯抚了抚马甲。

===Original===
“Strange, I agree. One can only account for it, can one not, by supposing that I radiate some extraordinary attraction. Have you noticed it?”

===Chinese===
「我也说怪，你同意吧。只能这样解释，你说是不是：我周身放射着某种异乎寻常的魅力。你察觉了吗？」

===Original===
“No!”

===Chinese===
「没有！」

===Original===
“No?” said Psmith, surprised. “Ah, well,” he went on tolerantly, “no doubt it will flash upon you quite unexpectedly sooner or later. Like a thunderbolt or something.”

===Chinese===
「没有？」皮史密斯说，颇感意外。「啊，好吧，」他宽容地接着说，「想必或早或迟，它会像霹雳什么的，出其不意地照到你身上。」

===Original===
“I think you’re terribly conceited.”

===Chinese===
「我看你自负得吓人。」

===Original===
“Not at all,” said Psmith. “Conceited? No, no. Success has not spoiled me.”

===Chinese===
「哪里，」皮史密斯说。「自负？不不。成功没有宠坏我。」

===Original===
“Have you had any success?”

===Chinese===
「你有过什么成功吗？」

===Original===
“None whatever.” The car stopped. “We get down here,” said Psmith, opening the door.

===Chinese===
「一点也没有。」汽车停了。「咱们在这儿下车，」皮史密斯说着，推开车门。

===Original===
“Here? Why?”

===Chinese===
「这儿？为什么？」

===Original===
“Because, if we go up to the house, you will infallibly be pounced on and set to work by one Baxter﻿—a delightful fellow, but a whale for toil. I propose to conduct you on a tour round the grounds, and then we will go for a row on the lake. You will enjoy that.”

===Chinese===
「因为，要是上宅子那边去，你准会被一位姓巴克斯特的一把逮住、立刻开工——讨人喜欢的家伙，就是干活一头鲸鱼。我提议领你环园一游，然后咱们去湖上划船。你会喜欢的。」

===Original===
“You seem to have mapped out my future for me.”

===Chinese===
「你倒把我的前途都规划好了。」

===Original===
“I have,” said Psmith with emphasis, and in the monocled eye that met hers Eve detected so beaming a glance of esteem and admiration that she retreated warily into herself and endeavoured to be frigid.

===Chinese===
「正是，」皮史密斯加重语气说。那戴单片眼镜的眼睛里，满含着倾慕与赞赏的粲然一瞥迎上她的目光，伊芙警觉地缩回自己心里，努力摆出冷若冰霜。

===Original===
“I’m afraid I haven’t time to wander about the grounds,” she said aloofly. “I must be going and seeing Mr. Baxter.”

===Chinese===
「恐怕我没工夫逛园子，」她冷冷地说。「我得去见巴克斯特先生了。」

===Original===
“Baxter,” said Psmith, “is not one of the natural beauties of the place. Time enough to see him when you are compelled to﻿ ﻿… We are now in the southern pleasaunce or the west home-park or something. Note the refined way the deer are cropping the grass. All the ground on which we are now standing is of historic interest. Oliver Cromwell went through here in 1550. The record has since been lowered.”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特，」皮史密斯说，「不属于本地天然美景之列。等你不得不见他的时候，有的是工夫……我们现在置身的是南游乐园，或者西内园，反正诸如此类。请留意鹿群啃草的那份雅致。我们脚下所站的这片土地，处处有历史典故。奥利弗·克伦威尔于 1550 年从这里经过。这项纪录后来被打破了。」

===Original===
“I haven’t time﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我没工夫……」

===Original===
“Leaving the pleasaunce on our left, we proceed to the northern messuage. The dandelions were imported from Egypt by the ninth Earl.”

===Chinese===
「把游园留在左手边，我们前进至北宅园。这些蒲公英是第九代伯爵从埃及引进的。」

===Original===
“Well, anyhow,” said Eve mutinously, “I won’t come on the lake.”

===Chinese===
「反正，」伊芙倔强地说，「湖我不去。」

===Original===
“You will enjoy the lake,” said Psmith. “The newts are of the famous old Blandings strain. They were introduced, together with the water-beetles, in the reign of Queen Elizabeth. Lord Emsworth, of course, holds manorial rights over the mosquito-swatting.”

===Chinese===
「你会喜欢湖的，」皮史密斯说。「湖里的蝾螈出自名门，是老布兰丁斯一脉。它们与龙虱一道，入籍于伊丽莎白女王时代。至于拍蚊子权，自然归埃姆斯沃思勋爵世袭所有。」

===Original===
Eve was a girl of high and haughty spirit, and as such strongly resented being appropriated and having her movements directed by one who, in spite of his specious claims, was almost a stranger. But somehow she found her companion’s placid assumption of authority hard to resist. Almost meekly she accompanied him through meadow and shrubbery, over velvet lawns and past gleaming flowerbeds, and her indignation evaporated as her eyes absorbed the beauty of it all. She gave a little sigh. If Market Blandings had seemed a place in which one might dwell happily, Blandings Castle was a paradise.

===Chinese===
伊芙是个心高气傲的姑娘，正因如此，她对被人擅自征用、任人指挥行程一事深恶痛绝——尤其对方尽管说法天花乱坠，终究近乎生人。可不知怎的，她发现同伴这份气定神闲的专断难以抗拒。她几乎是乖乖地跟着他穿过草场与灌木，越过天鹅绒般的草坪，路过灿亮的花圃，满腔义愤在饱览这一切胜景的目光中蒸发了。她轻轻叹了口气。如果说布兰丁斯集镇已像个可以安居乐业的地方，布兰丁斯城堡便是一座天堂。

===Original===
“Before us now,” said Psmith, “lies the celebrated Yew Alley, so called from the yews which hem it in. Speaking in my capacity of guide to the estate, I may say that when we have turned this next corner you will see a most remarkable sight.”

===Chinese===
「我们眼前，」皮史密斯说，「就是有名的紫杉巷，因夹道紫杉而得名。以本产业向导的身份，我可以说，拐过下一个弯，你将目睹一幕奇观。」

===Original===
And they did. Before them, as they passed in under the boughs of an aged tree lay a green vista, faintly dappled with stray shafts of sunshine. In the middle of this vista the Hon. Frederick Threepwood was embracing a young woman in the dress of a housemaid.

===Chinese===
果然不差。他们从一株老树的枝柯下穿入，眼前展开一道绿荫长廊，零星的阳光斜洒成斑。长廊正中，弗雷德里克·思里普伍德少爷正拥抱着一位穿女仆装的年轻姑娘。

### IV / IV

===Original===
Psmith was the first of the little group to recover from the shock of this unexpected encounter, the Hon. Freddie the last. That unfortunate youth, meeting Eve’s astonished eye as he raised his head, froze where he stood and remained with his mouth open until she had disappeared, which she did a few moments later, led away by Psmith, who, as he went, directed at his young friend a look in which surprise, pain, and reproof were so nicely blended that it would have been hard to say which predominated. All that a spectator could have said with certainty was that Psmith’s finer feelings had suffered a severe blow.

===Chinese===
这场不期而遇的惊愕，这一小群人里头一个回过神来的是皮史密斯，末一个是弗雷迪少爷。那不幸的青年抬头正撞上伊芙惊愕的目光，当场僵在原地，嘴张着，直到她消失为止——片刻后她便被皮史密斯领走了；临去时，皮史密斯朝这位小朋友投去一瞥，惊、痛、责在其中调和得如此匀停，简直说不好哪一样占上风。旁观者所能确言的只有一点：皮史密斯的高尚情操遭受了沉重一击。

===Original===
“A painful scene,” he remarked to Eve, as he drew her away in the direction of the house. “But we must always strive to be charitable. He may have been taking a fly out of her eye, or teaching her jiujitsu.”

===Chinese===
「痛心的一幕，」他引着伊芙往宅子方向走时评论道。「但我们永远要力求宽厚。他也许是在替她取眼里的沙子，或者在教她柔术。」

===Original===
He looked at her searchingly.

===Chinese===
他细细端详她。

===Original===
“You seem less revolted,” he said, “than one might have expected. This argues a sweet, shall we say angelic disposition and confirms my already high opinion of you.”

===Chinese===
「你看上去，」他说，「比人们预想的要不动声色得多。这证明一副温柔的、咱们不妨说天使般的性子，也印证了我对你的高度评价。」

===Original===
“Thank you.”

===Chinese===
「谢谢。」

===Original===
“Not at all. Mark you,” said Psmith, “I don’t think that this sort of thing is a hobby of Comrade Threepwood’s. He probably has many other ways of passing his spare time. Remember that before you pass judgment upon him. Also﻿—Young Blood, and all that sort of thing.”

===Chinese===
「不客气。不过你记着，」皮史密斯说，「我并不认为这类事是思里普伍德同志的癖好。他消磨闲暇的法子想必多得很。评判他之前记着这一条。还有——青春血热，年轻人嘛，总是这样的。」

===Original===
“I haven’t any intention of passing judgment upon him. It doesn’t interest me what Mr. Threepwood does, either in his spare time or out of it.”

===Chinese===
「我没打算评判他。思里普伍德先生干什么，闲时也好忙时也好，我毫无兴趣。」

===Original===
“His interest in you, on the other hand, is vast. I forgot to tell you before, but he loves you. He asked me to mention it if the conversation happened to veer round in that direction.”

===Chinese===
「反过来，他对你的兴趣却是滔天的。我忘了先告诉你——他爱慕你。他托我捎话，倘若话头恰好转向那边。」

===Original===
“I know he does,” said Eve ruefully.

===Chinese===
「我知道，」伊芙怏怏地说。

===Original===
“And does the fact stir no chord in you?”

===Chinese===
「而这个事实，拨不动你心里的哪根弦？」

===Original===
“I think he’s a nuisance.”

===Chinese===
「我觉得他烦人。」

===Original===
“That,” said Psmith cordially, “is the right spirit. I like to see it. Very well, then, we will discard the topic of Freddie, and I will try to find others that may interest, elevate, and amuse you. We are now approaching the main buildings. I am no expert in architecture, so cannot tell you all I could wish about the façade, but you can see there *is* a façade, and in my opinion﻿—for what it is worth﻿—a jolly good one. We approach by a sweeping gravel walk.”

===Chinese===
「这才对，」皮史密斯由衷地说，「要的就是这个精神。我喜欢看到这个。很好，那咱们把弗雷迪的话题搁下，我来找点别的，兴许让你感兴趣、受教益、得消遣的。我们如今走近主建筑群了。我不是建筑行家，没法把正立面该讲的都讲给你听，但你自己看得出，正立面*是有的*，而且依我浅见——浅见不值钱——相当不坏。我们正沿着一条气派的砾石道进发。」

===Original===
“I am going in to report to Mr. Baxter,” said Eve with decision. “It’s too absurd. I mustn’t spend my time strolling about the grounds. I must see Mr. Baxter at once.”

===Chinese===
「我要进去向巴克斯特先生报到，」伊芙决然地说。「太荒唐了。我不能把时间花在逛园子上。我必须立刻见巴克斯特先生。」

===Original===
Psmith inclined his head courteously.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯彬彬有礼地一颔首。

===Original===
“Nothing easier. That big, open window there is the library. Doubtless Comrade Baxter is somewhere inside, toiling away among the archives.”

===Chinese===
「再容易不过。那边那扇敞开的大窗就是藏书室。巴克斯特同志八成正在里头某处，在故纸堆里埋头苦干呢。」

===Original===
“Yes, but I can’t announce myself by shouting to him.”

===Chinese===
「是，可我不能隔着窗喊他通报啊。」

===Original===
“Assuredly not,” said Psmith. “No need for that at all. Leave it to me.” He stooped and picked up a large flowerpot which stood under the terrace wall, and before Eve could intervene had tossed it lightly through the open window. A muffled thud, followed by a sharp exclamation from within, caused a faint smile of gratification to illumine his solemn countenance. “He *is* in. I thought he would be. Ah, Baxter,” he said graciously, as the upper half of a body surmounted by a spectacled face framed itself suddenly in the window, “a pleasant, sunny afternoon. How is everything?”

===Chinese===
「当然不能，」皮史密斯说。「压根无须如此。交给我。」他俯身从露台墙根拾起一只大花盆，不等伊芙阻拦，已经轻轻抛进了敞开的窗户。一声闷响，紧接着窗内一声急呼，引得他那庄重的面孔上浮起一丝满意的浅笑。「他*在*。我料到他在。啊，巴克斯特，」他和蔼地说，此时一具上半身连同那张戴眼镜的脸忽然嵌入了窗框，「宜人的、晴朗的下午。一切可好？」

===Original===
The Efficient Baxter struggled for utterance.

===Chinese===
能干的巴克斯特挣扎着想说出话来。

===Original===
“You look like the Blessed Damozel gazing down from the gold bar of Heaven,” said Psmith genially. “Baxter, I want to introduce you to Miss Halliday. She arrived safely after a somewhat fatiguing journey. You will like Miss Halliday. If I had a library, I could not wish for a more courteous, obliging, and capable cataloguist.”

===Chinese===
「你像极了从天堂金栏上俯瞰的蒙福的达莫泽尔，」皮史密斯亲切地说。「巴克斯特，我要给你引见哈利迪小姐。她旅途略感劳顿，安然抵达。你会喜欢哈利迪小姐的。我若有一间藏书室，再也找不到比她更谦和、更尽心、更能干的编目员了。」

===Original===
This striking and unsolicited testimonial made no appeal to the Efficient Baxter. His mind seemed occupied with other matters.

===Chinese===
这份醒目而不请自来的推荐书，对能干的巴克斯特毫无吸引力。他的心思似乎被别的事占着。

===Original===
“Did you throw that flowerpot?” he demanded coldly.

===Chinese===
「那只花盆是你扔的？」他冷冷地质问。

===Original===
“You will no doubt,” said Psmith, “wish on some later occasion to have a nice long talk with Miss Halliday in order to give her an outline of her duties. I have been showing her the grounds and am about to take her for a row on the lake. But after that she will﻿—and I know I may speak for Miss Halliday in this matter﻿—be entirely at your disposal.”

===Chinese===
「你无疑，」皮史密斯说，「愿意改日同哈利迪小姐做一次好好的长谈，把她的职责范围交代个大概。我方才领她看了园子，正要带她去湖上划船。此后她便——此事我确知可以代哈利迪小姐作答——完全听凭你安排了。」

===Original===
“Did you throw that flowerpot?”

===Chinese===
「那只花盆是你扔的？」

===Original===
“I look forward confidently to the pleasantest of associations between you and Miss Halliday. You will find her,” said Psmith warmly, “a willing assistant, a tireless worker.”

===Chinese===
「我满怀信心地期待你与哈利迪小姐之间最愉快的合作。你会发现她，」皮史密斯热忱地说，「是个甘愿出力的助手，不知疲倦的干才。」

===Original===
“Did you﻿ ﻿… ?”

===Chinese===
「是不是你……？」

===Original===
“But now,” said Psmith, “I must be tearing myself away. In order to impress Miss Halliday, I put on my best suit when I went to meet her. For a row upon the lake something simpler in pale flannel is indicated. I shall only be a few minutes,” he said to Eve. “Would you mind meeting me at the boathouse?”

===Chinese===
「不过现在，」皮史密斯说，「我必须抽身了。为了给哈利迪小姐留下印象，接站时我穿上了我最好的一套。可湖上一游，讲究的是素净的浅色法兰绒。我只要几分钟，」他对伊芙说。「你到船屋等我，可好？」

===Original===
“I am not coming on the lake with you.”

===Chinese===
「我不跟你去湖上。」

===Original===
“At the boathouse in﻿—say﻿—six and a quarter minutes,” said Psmith with a gentle smile, and pranced into the house like a long-legged mustang.

===Chinese===
「船屋见——就——六又四分之一分钟后，」皮史密斯温和一笑，随即像一匹长腿野马似的欢腾进宅去了。

===Original===
Eve remained where she stood, struggling between laughter and embarrassment. The Efficient Baxter was still leaning wrathfully out of the library window, and it began to seem a little difficult to carry on an ordinary conversation. The problem of what she was to say in order to continue the scene in an agreeable manner was solved by the arrival of Lord Emsworth, who pottered out from the bushes with a rake in his hand. He stood eyeing Eve for a moment, then memory seemed to wake. Eve’s appearance was easier to remember, possibly, than some of the things which his lordship was wont to forget. He came forward beamingly.

===Chinese===
伊芙原地站着，在好笑与窘迫之间挣扎。能干的巴克斯特还在藏书室窗口怒目外探，看来要寻常交谈是有点难了。怎样才能把这出戏体面地续下去的难题，由埃姆斯沃思勋爵的到场解决了。他手里拿着一把耙子，从灌木丛里踱出来，站在那儿端详了伊芙片刻，随后记忆似乎醒了。伊芙这张脸，大概比勋爵大人惯常忘掉的那些事要好记些。他笑吟吟地上前。

===Original===
“Ah, there you are, Miss﻿ ﻿… Dear me, I’m really afraid I have forgotten your name. My memory is excellent as a rule, but I cannot remember names﻿ ﻿… Miss Halliday! Of course, of course. Baxter, my dear fellow,” he proceeded, sighting the watcher at the window, “this is Miss Halliday.”

===Chinese===
「啊，你来了，小姐……哎呀，实在抱歉，我怕是忘了你的大名。我一向记性不坏，就是记不住名字……哈利迪小姐！对了，对了。巴克斯特，亲爱的伙计，」他望见窗上了望的人，接着说，「这位是哈利迪小姐。」

===Original===
“Mr. McTodd,” said the Efficient One sourly, “has already introduced me to Miss Halliday.”

===Chinese===
「麦克托德先生，」能干的那位没好气地说，「已经向哈利迪小姐引见过了。」

===Original===
“Has he? Deuced civil of him, deuced civil of him. But where *is* he?” inquired his lordship, scanning the surrounding scenery with a vague eye.

===Chinese===
「是吗？他可真客气，真客气。可他人*在*哪儿呢？」勋爵大人问道，两只茫然的眼把四下风景扫了一圈。

===Original===
“He went into the house. After,” said Baxter in a cold voice, “throwing a flowerpot at me.”

===Chinese===
「他进屋去了。在……」巴克斯特冷冷地说，「朝我扔了一只花盆之后。」

===Original===
“Doing what?”

===Chinese===
「干什么？」

===Original===
“He threw a flowerpot at me,” said Baxter, and vanished moodily.

===Chinese===
「他朝我扔了一只花盆，」巴克斯特说完，郁郁地消失了。

===Original===
Lord Emsworth stared at the open window, then turned to Eve for enlightenment.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵瞪着那扇敞开的窗，继而转向伊芙求个明白。

===Original===
“*Why* did Baxter throw a flowerpot at McTodd?” he said. “And,” he went on, ventilating an even deeper question, “where the deuce did he get a flowerpot? There are no flowerpots in the library.”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特*为什么*朝麦克托德扔花盆？」他说。接着他又提出了一个更深的问题：「他到底是打哪儿弄来的花盆？藏书室里可没有花盆。」

===Original===
Eve, on her side, was also seeking information.

===Chinese===
伊芙这边也在打听消息。

===Original===
“Did you say his name was McTodd, Lord Emsworth?”

===Chinese===
「您刚才说那位姓麦克托德，埃姆斯沃思勋爵？」

===Original===
“No, no. Baxter. That was Baxter, my secretary.”

===Chinese===
「不不。巴克斯特。那是巴克斯特，我的秘书。」

===Original===
“No, I mean the one who met me at the station.”

===Chinese===
「不，我是说车站接我的那一位。」

===Original===
“Baxter did not meet you at the station. The man who met you at the station,” said Lord Emsworth, speaking slowly, for women are so apt to get things muddled, “was McTodd. He’s staying here. Constance asked him, and I’m bound to say when I first heard of it I was not any too well pleased. I don’t like poets as a rule. But this fellow’s so different from the other poets I’ve met. Different altogether. And,” said Lord Emsworth with not a little heat, “I strongly object to Baxter throwing flowerpots at him. I won’t *have* Baxter throwing flowerpots at my guests,” he said firmly; for Lord Emsworth, though occasionally a little vague, was keenly alive to the ancient traditions of his family regarding hospitality.

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特没去车站接你。去车站接你的那位，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵慢条斯理地说，因为女人家最容易把事情搅浑，「是麦克托德。他在这儿做客。康斯坦丝请的他。说实话，一听说这事我心里可不怎么痛快。我素来不喜欢诗人。可这位老兄同我见过的其他诗人太不一样了。彻头彻尾地不一样。还有，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵相当激动地说，「我强烈反对巴克斯特朝他扔花盆。我*不许*巴克斯特朝我的客人扔花盆，」他斩钉截铁地说；因为埃姆斯沃思勋爵虽然偶尔糊涂一阵，对自家古老的待客之道却是分毫必较的。

===Original===
“Is Mr. McTodd a poet?” said Eve, her heart beating.

===Chinese===
「麦克托德先生是位诗人？」伊芙说，心怦怦直跳。

===Original===
“Eh? Oh yes, yes. There seems to be no doubt about that. A Canadian poet. Apparently they have poets out there. And,” demanded his lordship, ever a fair-minded man, “why not? A remarkably growing country. I was there in the year ’98. Or was it,” he added, thoughtfully passing a muddy hand over his chin and leaving a rich brown stain, “ ’99? I forget. My memory isn’t good for dates.﻿ ﻿… If you will excuse me, Miss﻿—Miss Halliday, of course﻿—if you will excuse me, I must be leaving you. I have to see McAllister, my head gardener. An obstinate man. A Scotchman. If you go into the house, my sister Constance will give you a cup of tea. I don’t know what the time is, but I suppose there will be tea soon. Never take it myself.”

===Chinese===
「嗯？哦，是的，是的。这一点看来没有疑问。加拿大的诗人。看来他们那边也出诗人。再说，」勋爵大人素来公道，此刻发问，「凭什么不出？一个热火朝天发展着的国家。我 98 年去过那儿。还是，」他若有所思地用一只沾泥的手抹了抹下巴，留下一道富饶的棕色印渍，「99 年？记不得了。我的记性对付不了年月。……失陪了，小姐——哈利迪小姐，当然是——失陪了，我得走了。我得去见麦卡利斯特，我的首席园丁。一个犟种。苏格兰人。你若进屋去，我妹妹康斯坦丝会奉你一杯茶。现在几点我不知道，不过想来快上茶了。我自己是从来不喝的。」

===Original===
“Mr. McTodd asked me to go for a row on the lake.”

===Chinese===
「麦克托德先生约我去湖上划船。」

===Original===
“On the lake, eh? On the *lake*?” said his lordship, as if this was the last place in the neighbourhood where he would have expected to hear of people proposing to row. Then he brightened. “Of course, yes, on the lake. I think you will like the lake. I take a dip there myself every morning before breakfast. I find it good for the health and appetite. I plunge in and swim perhaps fifty yards, and then return.” Lord Emsworth suspended the gossip from the training-camp in order to look at his watch. “Dear me,” he said, “I must be going. McAllister has been waiting fully ten minutes. Goodbye, then, for the present, Miss﻿—er﻿—goodbye.”

===Chinese===
「湖上，嗯？*湖*上？」勋爵大人说道，仿佛这是左近他最想不到会有人提议划船的地方。随即他眉开眼笑。「当然，是的，湖上。我想你会喜欢那湖。我自己每天早饭前都在那儿泡一泡。于健康开胃都有益。我扑通跳进去，游个五十来码，再回来。」埃姆斯沃思勋爵暂停播发训练令营的见闻，看了看表。「哎呀，」他说，「我得走了。麦卡利斯特已经干等了足足十分钟。那就回见，小姐——呃——回见。」

===Original===
And Lord Emsworth ambled off, on his face that look of tense concentration which it always wore when interviews with Angus McAllister were in prospect﻿—the look which stern warriors wear when about to meet a foeman worthy of their steel.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵蹒跚而去，脸上带着每当要与安格斯·麦卡利斯特会面便会出现的那副紧张专注的神情——猛士临敌、棋逢对手时常有的那副神情。

### V / V

===Original===
There was a cold expression in Eve’s eyes as she made her way slowly to the boathouse. The information which she had just received had come as a shock, and she was trying to adjust her mind to it. When Miss Clarkson had told her of the unhappy conclusion to her old school friend’s marriage to Ralston McTodd, she had immediately, without knowing anything of the facts, arrayed herself loyally on Cynthia’s side and condemned the unknown McTodd uncompromisingly and without hesitation. It was many years since she had seen Cynthia, and their friendship might almost have been said to have lapsed; but Eve’s affection, when she had once given it, was a durable thing, capable of surviving long separation. She had loved Cynthia at school, and she could feel nothing but animosity towards anyone who had treated her badly. She eyed the glittering water of the lake from under lowered brows, and prepared to be frigid and hostile when the villain of the piece should arrive. It was only when she heard footsteps behind her and turned to perceive Psmith hurrying up, radiant in gleaming flannel, that it occurred to her for the first time that there might have been faults on both sides. She had not known Psmith long, it was true, but already his personality had made a somewhat deep impression on her, and she was loath to believe that he could be the callous scoundrel of her imagination. She decided to suspend judgment until they should be out in mid-water and in a position to discuss the matter without interruption.

===Chinese===
伊芙慢慢走向船屋，眼里含着一丝寒意。她刚刚听来的消息不啻一记闷棍，她正努力把心思扳过来。当日克拉克森小姐告诉她，老同学辛西娅与拉尔斯顿·麦克托德的婚姻落得那样不幸的结局时，她立刻在毫不知情的情况下，忠实地站到了辛西娅一边，毫不迟疑、毫不留情地痛斥了那位素未谋面的麦克托德。她已有多年没见辛西娅，两人的友谊几乎可以说中断了；但伊芙的感情一经付出，便经久耐磨，久别亦不褪色。她在校时爱着辛西娅，对任何亏待过辛西娅的人，她只能生出敌意。她垂着睫毛望向粼粼的湖水，预备等这出戏里的恶棍一到，便以冰霜相向。直到听见身后的脚步声、回头看见皮史密斯匆匆赶来——一身闪亮的法兰绒，神采奕奕——她才头一回想到：这事儿说不定双方都有不是。诚然她认识皮史密斯不久，但他的人格已在她心上留下相当深的印迹，她不愿相信他会是她想象中那种无情的恶棍。她决定暂缓定谳，等到了湖心、可以不受打扰地细谈此事再说。

===Original===
“I am a little late,” said Psmith, as he came up. “I was detained by our young friend Freddie. He came into my room and started talking about himself at the very moment when I was tying my tie and needed every ounce of concentration for that delicate task. The recent painful episode appeared to be weighing on his mind to some extent.” He helped Eve into the boat and started to row. “I consoled him as best I could by telling him that it would probably have made you think all the more highly of him. I ventured the suggestion that girls worship the strong, rough, dashing type of man. And, after I had done my best to convince him that he was a strong, rough, dashing man, I came away. By now, of course, he may have had a relapse into despair; so, if you happen to see a body bobbing about in the water as we row along, it will probably be Freddie’s.”

===Chinese===
「我迟到了片刻，」皮史密斯赶上来说。「让咱们的小朋友弗雷迪绊住了。他闯进我屋里，正赶上我打领结、须得聚精会神对付那精细活计的当口，开始大谈他自己。方才那幕痛心的插曲，看来多少还压在他心上。」他扶伊芙上船，荡起桨来。「我尽力安慰他，说那多半反倒叫你更高看他一眼。我斗胆进言，说姑娘们崇拜的正是那种粗犷豪迈的硬汉型。等我费尽唇舌，使他确信自己正是一个粗犷豪迈的硬汉，我就来了。当然，这会儿他没准又绝望复发了呢。所以，咱们划着划着，你若碰巧看见水里浮沉着一具尸身，多半就是弗雷迪。」

===Original===
“Never mind about Freddie.”

===Chinese===
「弗雷迪不用你管。」

===Original===
“I don’t if you don’t,” said Psmith agreeably. “Very well, then, if we see a body, we will ignore it.” He rowed on a few strokes. “Correct me if I am wrong,” he said, resting on his oars and leaning forward, “but you appear to be brooding about something. If you will give me a clue, I will endeavour to assist you to grapple with any little problem which is troubling you. What is the matter?”

===Chinese===
「你不管我也就不管，」皮史密斯随和地说。「很好，看见尸身，咱们就只当没看见。」他荡了几桨。「若有说错请你指正，」他停桨前俯说，「你像是在为什么事出神。你若肯给个头绪，凡有小小难题困扰你，我都愿效力共解。怎么回事？」

===Original===
Eve, questioned thus directly, found it difficult to open the subject. She hesitated a moment, and let the water ripple through her fingers.

===Chinese===
伊芙被这么直截了当地一问，反倒难于启齿了。她迟疑片刻，任湖水从指缝间流淌。

===Original===
“I have only just found out your name, Mr. McTodd,” she said at length.

===Chinese===
「我这才知道你的名字，麦克托德先生，」她终于说。

===Original===
Psmith nodded.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯点头。

===Original===
“It is always thus,” he said. “Passing through this life, we meet a fellow-mortal, chat awhile, and part; and the last thing we think of doing is to ask him in a manly and direct way what his label is. There is something oddly furtive and shamefaced in one’s attitude towards people’s names. It is as if we shrank from probing some hideous secret. We say to ourselves ‘This pleasant stranger may be a Snooks or a Buggins. Better not inquire.’ But in my case﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「向来如此，」他说。「人生在世，遇见一位同类，聊上一阵，分手；而咱们最想不到要做的，就是以男子汉的直截了当问他姓甚名谁。人对旁人的名字，总抱着一种奇怪的鬼祟和羞怯。仿佛怕刺探什么骇人的隐私似的。我们暗自嘀咕：『这位愉快的生人，兴许姓斯努克斯，或者邦金斯。还是别打听的好。』不过至于我……」

===Original===
“It was a great shock to me.”

===Chinese===
「这对我可是一大震动。」

===Original===
“Now there,” said Psmith, “I cannot follow you. I wouldn’t call McTodd a bad name, as names go. Don’t you think there is a sort of Highland strength about it? It sounds to me like something out of ‘The Lady of the Lake’ or ‘The Lay of the Last Minstrel.’ ‘The stag at eve had drunk its fill adoon the glen beyint the hill, and welcomed with a friendly nod old Scotland’s pride, young Laird McTodd.’ You don’t think it has a sort of wild romantic ring?”

===Chinese===
「这一点，」皮史密斯说，「我跟不上你了。论名字，麦克托德不算坏名字。你不觉得它带着一股高地人的劲道？在我听来像《湖上夫人》或者《最后吟游诗人之歌》里的什么。「暮色中雄鹿饮罢甘泉，下山来穿林过岗，颔首相迎者，苏格兰之骄傲，年轻的麦克托德少爷。」你不觉得它有一种野性的浪漫音响吗？」

===Original===
“I ought to tell you, Mr. McTodd,” said Eve, “that I was at school with Cynthia.”

===Chinese===
「我应该告诉你，麦克托德先生，」伊芙说，「我和辛西娅是同学。」

===Original===
Psmith was not a young man who often found himself at a loss, but this remark gave him a bewildered feeling such as comes in dreams. It was plain to him that this delightful girl thought she had said something serious, even impressive; but for the moment it did not seem to him to make sense. He sparred warily for time.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯并非轻易语塞的青年，可这句话给了他一种梦中才有 的茫然。他明明白白看得出，这位可爱的姑娘自以为说了句要紧话，甚至是有分量的话；可眼下这话在他听来全无意义。他谨慎地虚晃一枪，争取时间。

===Original===
“Indeed? With Cynthia? That must have been jolly.”

===Chinese===
「是吗？和辛西娅？那一定很快活。」

===Original===
The harmless observation appeared to have the worst effect upon his companion. The frown came back to her face.

===Chinese===
这句无害的观察在他的同伴身上竟产生了最坏的效果。她眉间的愠色又回来了。

===Original===
“Oh, don’t speak in that flippant, sneering way,” she said. “It’s so cheap.”

===Chinese===
「哦，别用那种轻浮刻薄的腔调说话，」她说。「太廉价了。」

===Original===
Psmith, having nothing to say, remained silent, and the boat drifted on. Eve’s face was delicately pink, for she was feeling extraordinarily embarrassed. There was something in the solemn gaze of the man before her which made it difficult for her to go on. But, with the stoutheartedness which was one of her characteristics, she stuck to her task.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯无话可说，便沉默着，小船漂流着。伊芙的脸泛着淡淡的红，因为她正窘迫到了极点。对面这人那庄重的注视里有点什么，叫她难以为继。但她以她性格中特有的胆气，咬牙撑住了。

===Original===
“After all,” she said, “however you may feel about her now, you must have been fond of poor Cynthia at one time, or I don’t see why you should have married her.”

===Chinese===
「毕竟，」她说，「不管你现在对她怎么想，你总有一度是爱过可怜的辛西娅的，要不然我不明白你当初怎么会娶她。」

===Original===
Psmith, for want of conversation, had begun rowing again. The start he gave at these remarkable words caused him to skim the surface of the water with the left oar in such a manner as to send a liberal pint into Eve’s lap. He started forward with apologies.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯苦于无话，又荡起桨来。这番惊人之语吓得他左手一桨擦着水皮，把足足一品脱泼进了伊芙的裙兜。他慌忙欠身道歉。

===Original===
“Oh, never mind about that,” said Eve impatiently. “It doesn’t matter.﻿ ﻿… Mr. McTodd,” she said, and there was a note of gentleness in her voice, “I do wish you would tell me what the trouble was.”

===Chinese===
「哦，那个不用管，」伊芙不耐烦地说。「不要紧。……麦克托德先生，」她说，嗓音里带上了温柔，「我真希望你能告诉我，当初到底是怎么回事。」

===Original===
Psmith stared at the floor of the boat in silence. He was wrestling with a feeling of injury. True, he had not during their brief conversation at the Senior Conservative Club specifically inquired of Mr. McTodd whether he was a bachelor, but somehow he felt that the man should have dropped some hint as to his married state. True, again, Mr. McTodd had not asked him to impersonate him at Blandings Castle. And yet, undeniably, he felt that he had a grievance. Psmith’s was an orderly mind. He had proposed to continue the pleasant relations which had begun between Eve and himself, seeing to it that every day they became a little pleasanter, until eventually, in due season, they should reach the point where it would become possible to lay heart and hand at her feet. For there was no doubt in his mind that in a world congested to overflowing with girls Eve Halliday stood entirely alone. And now this infernal Cynthia had risen from nowhere to stand between them. Even a young man as liberally endowed with calm assurance as he was might find it awkward to conduct his wooing with such a handicap as a wife in the background.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯默默瞪着船底。他正同一股受了委屈的情绪角力。诚然，在老保守党俱乐部那番短短的谈话里，他没有专门问过麦克托德先生是否单身；可不知怎么，他觉得那人本该对他的已婚身份露点口风。再说，诚然，麦克托德先生也并没有请他来布兰丁斯城堡冒名顶替。然而，无可否认，他觉得自己有冤。皮史密斯是个有条理的头脑。他的原定计划，是把伊芙与他之间已开的这段愉快关系延续下去，并使之与日俱增地愉快，直到瓜熟蒂落，时机一到，便可以把心和手一并奉献到她的脚前。因为他心中毫无疑义：在这个姑娘拥挤得水泄不通的世界上，伊芙·哈利迪独一档。而如今这个可恶的辛西娅不知从哪里冒出来，横在他们中间。即便沉着自信如他，揣着「后台还有位太太」这样一件拖累去求爱，也难免觉得碍手碍脚。

===Original===
Eve misinterpreted his silence.

===Chinese===
伊芙误解了他的沉默。

===Original===
“I suppose you are thinking that it is no business of mine?”

===Chinese===
「我猜，你在想这不关我的事？」

===Original===
Psmith came out of his thoughts with a start.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯一惊，回过神来。

===Original===
“No, no. Not at all.”

===Chinese===
「不，不。哪里。」

===Original===
“You see, I’m devoted to Cynthia﻿—and I like you.”

===Chinese===
「你要知道，我是疼辛西娅的——而我也喜欢你。」

===Original===
She smiled for the first time. Her embarrassment was passing.

===Chinese===
她头一回笑了。她的窘迫正在过去。

===Original===
“That is the whole point,” she said. “I do like you. And I’m quite sure that if you were really the sort of man I thought you when I first heard about all this, I shouldn’t. The friend who told me about you and Cynthia made it seem as if the whole fault had been yours. I got the impression that you had been very unkind to Cynthia. I thought you must be a brute. And when Lord Emsworth told me who you were, my first impulse was to hate you. I think if you had come along just then I should have been rather horrid to you. But you were late, and that gave me time to think it over. And then I remembered how nice you had been to me and I felt somehow that﻿—that you must really be quite nice, and it occurred to me that there might be some explanation. And I thought that﻿—perhaps﻿—if you would let me interfere in your private affairs﻿—and if things hadn’t gone too far﻿—I might do something to help﻿—try to bring you together, you know.”

===Chinese===
「关键就在这儿，」她说。「我确实喜欢你。而且我十分肯定，你要真是我刚听说这事时所想的那种人，我就不会喜欢你了。那位跟我说起你和辛西娅的朋友，把事情说得好像错全在你。我得到的印象是你一直待辛西娅很坏。我想你一定是个恶棍。所以埃姆斯沃思勋爵一说你是谁，我的头一个冲动就是恨你。我想，那时你要是正好出现，我多半会狠狠地凶你一顿。可是你迟到了，我便有了把这事细想的工夫。接着我想起你待我一直多么好，不知怎么就觉得——觉得你一定实在是挺好的，又想到这中间兴许另有隐情。我又想——也许——要是你肯让我干预你的私事——要是事情还没到不可收拾的地步——我或许能出点力——想法子让你们和好，你知道的。」

===Original===
She broke off, a little confused, for now that the words were out she was conscious of a return of her former shyness. Even though she was an old friend of Cynthia’s, there did seem something insufferably officious in this meddling. And when she saw the look of pain on her companion’s face, she regretted that she had spoken. Naturally, she thought, he was offended.

===Chinese===
她戛然收住，有点心慌，话一出口，先前的羞怯又回来了。她虽是辛西娅的老友，这番多管闲事，似乎也确有几分强人所难。及至看见同伴脸上痛楚的神色，她后悔开了口。她想，他自然是着恼了。

===Original===
In supposing that Psmith was offended she was mistaken. Internally he was glowing with a renewed admiration for all those beautiful qualities in her which he had detected, before they had ever met, at several yards’ range across the street from the window of the Drones Club smoking-room. His look of pain was due to the fact that, having now had time to grapple with the problem, he had decided to dispose of this Cynthia once and for all. He proposed to eliminate her for ever from his life. And the elimination of even such a comparative stranger seemed to him to call for a pained look. So he assumed one.

===Chinese===
以为皮史密斯着恼，她可想岔了。他内心里正为一个新生的倾慕而容光焕发——为她身上那些美的品质；那些品质，在他们相识之前、隔着好几码远的街面、从蜂子俱乐部吸烟室的窗口，他早已侦察在案。他那副痛楚之色，是因为此时既已把问题掂量清楚，他决定一劳永逸地解决掉这位辛西娅。他打算把她从他的生命里永久清除出去。而清除哪怕这样一位泛泛之交，他都觉得该摆出一副痛楚的脸。于是他摆出了一副。

===Original===
“That,” he said gravely, “would, I fear, be impossible. It is like you to suggest it, and I cannot tell you how much I appreciate the kindness which has made you interest yourself in my troubles, but it is too late for any reconciliation. Cynthia and I are divorced.”

===Chinese===
「那个，」他沉重地说，「恐怕是不可能的了。你提这个，正是你的为人；你为我的烦恼分心，这份好意我说不出多么领受。可是任何和解都太迟了。辛西娅和我已经离婚了。」

===Original===
For a moment the temptation had come to him to kill the woman off with some wasting sickness, but this he resisted as tending towards possible future complications. He was resolved, however, that there should be no question of bringing them together again.

===Chinese===
有一瞬间，他曾受到诱惑，想叫那女人害一场痨病死去，但他忍住了，觉得这容易给日后平添纠葛。不过他拿定主意，绝不能再有让他们破镜重圆的事。

===Original===
He was disturbed to find Eve staring at him in amazement.

===Chinese===
他不安地发现伊芙正惊愕地瞪着他。

===Original===
“Divorced? But how can you be divorced? It’s only a few days since you and she were in London together.”

===Chinese===
「离婚了？可你怎么离得了婚？你和她前几天还一起在伦敦呢。」

===Original===
Psmith ceased to wonder that Mr. McTodd had had trouble with his wife. The woman was a perfect pest.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯不再诧异麦克托德先生同他太太闹翻了。这女人简直是个祸害。

===Original===
“I used the term in a spiritual rather than a legal sense,” he replied. “True, there has been no actual decree, but we are separated beyond hope of reunion.” He saw the distress in Eve’s eyes and hurried on. “There are things,” he said, “which it is impossible for a man to overlook, however broad-minded he may be. Love, Miss Halliday, is a delicate plant. It needs tending, nursing, assiduous fostering. This cannot be done by throwing the breakfast bacon at a husband’s head.”

===Chinese===
「我用的这个词，取其精神层面之义，而非法律层面之义，」他答道。「诚然，还没有正式判词，但我们已经分居，破镜难圆。」他看见伊芙眼里的痛楚，赶紧接着说。「有些事情，」他说，「一个男人再胸襟宽广，也无法视而不见。爱情，哈利迪小姐，是一株娇嫩的植物。需要照料，呵护，勤勉的培育。拿早餐的培根连盘子砸丈夫的脑袋，这样是培育不出来的。」

===Original===
“What!” Eve’s astonishment was such that the word came out in a startled squeak.

===Chinese===
「什么！」伊芙惊得这声「什么」出口成了尖叫。

===Original===
“*In* the dish,” said Psmith sadly.

===Chinese===
「*连盘子*，」皮史密斯凄然说。

===Original===
Eve’s blue eyes opened wide.

===Chinese===
伊芙的蓝眼睛睁得溜圆。

===Original===
“*Cynthia* did that!”

===Chinese===
「*辛西娅*干的！」

===Original===
“On more than one occasion. Her temper in the mornings was terrible. I have known her lift the cat over two chairs and a settee with a single kick. And all because there were no mushrooms.”

===Chinese===
「不止一回。她早晨的火气吓死人。我亲眼见过她一脚把猫踢过两把椅子和一张长榻。而全部起因，不过是早点里没有蘑菇。」

===Original===
“But﻿—but I can’t believe it!”

===Chinese===
「可——可我不能相信！」

===Original===
“Come over to Canada,” said Psmith, “and I will show you the cat.”

===Chinese===
「到加拿大来，」皮史密斯说，「我带你看那只猫。」

===Original===
“Cynthia did that!﻿—Cynthia﻿—why, she was always the gentlest little creature.”

===Chinese===
「辛西娅干的！——辛西娅——咦，她从前可是个顶温柔的小人儿啊。」

===Original===
“At school, you mean?”

===Chinese===
「你是说，在校的时候？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「是啊。」

===Original===
“That,” said Psmith, “would, I suppose, be before she had taken to drink.”

===Chinese===
「那，」皮史密斯说，「想必是她染上酒瘾之前的事了。」

===Original===
“Taken to drink!”

===Chinese===
「染上酒瘾！」

===Original===
Psmith was feeling happier. A passing thought did come to him that all this was perhaps a trifle rough on the absent Cynthia, but he mastered the unmanly weakness. It was necessary that Cynthia should suffer in the good cause. Already he had begun to detect in Eve’s eyes the faint dawnings of an angelic pity, and pity is recognised by all the best authorities as one of the most valuable emotions which your wooer can awaken.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯的心情快活起来。他脑中确曾掠过一念：这么编排不在场的辛西娅，或许有点儿过火。但他把这软弱压下去了。为了大计，辛西娅必须受点委屈。他已开始在伊芙眼中侦察到一丝天使般怜悯的微光，而一切最权威的学说公认，怜悯乃是求婚者所能唤起的最有价值的情感之一。

===Original===
“Drink!” Eve repeated, with a little shudder.

===Chinese===
「酒瘾！」伊芙重复道，轻轻打了个寒噤。

===Original===
“We lived in one of the dry provinces of Canada, and, as so often happens, that started the trouble. From the moment when she installed a private still her downfall was swift. I have seen her, under the influence of home-brew, rage through the house like a devastating cyclone﻿ ﻿… I hate speaking like this of one who was your friend,” said Psmith, in a low, vibrating voice. “I would not tell these things to anyone but you. The world, of course, supposes that the entire blame for the collapse of our home was mine. I took care that it should be so. The opinion of the world matters little to me. But with you it is different. I should not like you to think badly of me, Miss Halliday. I do not make friends easily﻿—I am a lonely man﻿—but somehow it has seemed to me since we met that you and I might be friends.”

===Chinese===
「我们住在加拿大一个禁酒的省，而祸根恰恰由此埋下，这种事屡见不鲜。从她装置起一座私人蒸馏器那天起，她的堕落便一日千里。我见过她灌下家酿之后，像一场毁灭性的旋风席卷整座宅子……我不愿这样谈论你的朋友，」皮史密斯低沉而颤动地说。「这些事，除了你，我谁也不会讲。世人自然把咱们家败落的罪责全算在我头上。是我特意安排成这样的。世人的舆论，于我如浮云。但你不同。我不愿你在心里把我想坏，哈利迪小姐。我不轻易交朋友——我是个孤独的人——可自相识以来，不知怎的，我总觉得你和我，或许能成为朋友。」

===Original===
Eve stretched her hand out impulsively.

===Chinese===
伊芙冲动地伸出手去。

===Original===
“Why, of course!”

===Chinese===
「那还用说！」

===Original===
Psmith took her hand and held it far longer than was strictly speaking necessary.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯握住她的手，握了远超礼节所严格必需的时间。

===Original===
“Thank you,” he said. “Thank you.”

===Chinese===
「谢谢你，」他说。「谢谢你。」

===Original===
He turned the nose of the boat to the shore, and rowed slowly back.

===Chinese===
他把船头调向岸，缓缓摇了回去。

===Original===
“I have suffered,” said Psmith gravely, as he helped her ashore. “But, if you will be my friend, I think that I may forget.”

===Chinese===
「我受过的苦，」靠岸搀她下船时，皮史密斯沉重地说，「但若你肯做我的朋友，我想我能忘掉。」

===Original===
They walked in silence up the winding path to the castle.

===Chinese===
两人默默走上通往城堡的蜿蜒小径。

### VI / VI

===Original===
To Psmith five minutes later, as he sat in his room smoking a cigarette and looking dreamily out at the distant hills, there entered the Hon. Frederick Threepwood, who, having closed the door behind him, tottered to the bed and uttered a deep and discordant groan. Psmith, his mind thus rudely wrenched from pleasant meditations, turned and regarded the gloomy youth with disfavour.

===Chinese===
五分钟后，皮史密斯正坐在自己房里抽烟，出神地遥望远山，弗雷德里克·思里普伍德少爷进来了。他反手带上门，踉跄到床边，发出一声深沉而走调的呻吟。皮史密斯的好梦被这样粗暴地拧断，回过头，不以为然地打量这位愁云满面的青年。

===Original===
“At any other time, Comrade Threepwood,” he said politely but with firmness, “certainly. But not now. I am not in the vein.”

===Chinese===
「换个任何别的时刻，思里普伍德同志，」他客气而坚决地说，「都欢迎。但现在不行。我没有那个兴致。」

===Original===
“What?” said the Hon. Freddie vacantly.

===Chinese===
「什么？」弗雷迪少爷茫然地说。

===Original===
“I say that at any other time I shall be delighted to listen to your farmyard imitations, but not now. At the moment I am deep in thoughts of my own, and I may say frankly that I regard you as more or less of an excrescence. I want solitude, solitude. I am in a beautiful reverie, and your presence jars upon me somewhat profoundly.”

===Chinese===
「我是说，别的任何时候，听你学农场牲口叫，我都乐意之至，但现在不行。眼下我沉浸在自己的思绪里，而且恕我直言，我把你多少看成一样赘物。我要孤独，孤独。我在做一场美妙的幻梦，你的出现相当深刻地硌着我。」

===Original===
The Hon. Freddie ruined the symmetry of his hair by passing his fingers feverishly through it.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪少爷的手指发烧似的插进头发，毁掉了它对称的格局。

===Original===
“Don’t *talk* so much! I never met a fellow like you for talking.” Having rumpled his hair to the left, he went through it again and rumpled it to the right. “I say, do you know what? You’ve jolly well got to clear out of here quick!” He got up from the bed, and approached the window. Having done which, he bent towards Psmith and whispered in his ear. “The game’s up!”

===Chinese===
「别*说*这么多话！我从来没见过你这么能说的。」把头发左边揉乱之后，他又倒回去把右边揉乱。「我说，你知不知道？你他妈的得赶紧卷铺盖走人了！」他从床上爬起来，走到窗前。做完了这一套，他俯身凑近皮史密斯，对着他耳朵悄声说。「全穿帮了！」

===Original===
Psmith withdrew his ear with a touch of hauteur, but he looked at his companion with a little more interest. He had feared, when he saw Freddie stagger in with such melodramatic despair and emit so hollow a groan, that the topic on which he wished to converse was the already exhausted one of his broken heart. It now began to appear that weightier matters were on his mind.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯带几分傲慢地收回耳朵，但看同伴的眼光里添了几分兴味。方才见弗雷迪带着那副情节剧式的绝望踉跄而入、发出那声空洞的呻吟，他还怕他要谈的又是他心碎那件早已榨干的话题。现在看来，压在他心头的多半是更重的事。

===Original===
“I fail to understand you, Comrade Threepwood,” he said. “The last time I had the privilege of conversing with you, you informed me that Susan, or whatever her name is, merely giggled and told you not to be silly when you embraced her. In other words, she is *not* a detective. What has happened since then to get you all worked up?”

===Chinese===
「我听不懂你，思里普伍德同志，」他说。「上次有幸与你交谈，你告诉我，苏珊，或者不管她姓甚名谁，在你拥抱她时只是咯咯一乐，叫你别犯傻。换言之，她*不是*侦探。那以后又出了什么事，叫你激动成这样？」

===Original===
“Baxter!”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特！」

===Original===
“What has Baxter been doing?”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特干什么了？」

===Original===
“Only giving the whole bally show away to me, that’s all,” said Freddie feverishly. He clutched Psmith’s arm violently, causing that exquisite to utter a slight moan and smooth out the wrinkles thus created in his sleeve. “Listen! I’ve just been talking to the blighter. I was passing the library just now, when he popped out of the door and hauled me in. And, dash it, he hadn’t been talking two seconds before I realised that he has seen through the whole dam’ thing practically from the moment you got here. Though he doesn’t seem to know that I’ve anything to do with it, thank goodness.”

===Chinese===
「他把整台戏都给我抖搂了，就这么回事，」弗雷迪狂热地说。他一把死死抓住皮史密斯的胳膊，疼得那位雅人轻哼一声，抚平袖子上由此添的褶子。「听着！我刚跟那小子谈过。我正打藏书室门口过，他猛地从门里蹿出来，把我拽了进去。见鬼，他话没说两句我就明白了：打你进门那一刻起，他把这该死的把戏从头到尾都看穿了。不过他好像不知道这事有我一份，谢天谢地。」

===Original===
“I should imagine not, if he makes you his confidant. Why did he do that, by the way? What made him select you as the recipient of his secrets?”

===Chinese===
「我料想没有，既然他拿你当心腹。话说回来，他为何如此？他凭什么挑中你当他的机密收藏人？」

===Original===
“As far as I can make out, his idea was to form a gang, if you know what I mean. He said a lot of stuff about him and me being the only two able-bodied young men in the place, and we ought to be prepared to tackle you if you started anything.”

===Chinese===
「据我琢磨，他的意思是拉个帮派，你懂我意思吧。他说了一大套，什么这地方能打能蹦的青年就属我们俩，你要是闹出什么动静，咱们得准备好一块儿扑你。」

===Original===
“I see. And now tell me how our delightful friend ever happened to begin suspecting that I was not all I seemed to be. I had been flattering myself that I had put the little deception over with complete success.”

===Chinese===
「明白了。那么再告诉我，咱们这位可爱的朋友，当初究竟怎么会起疑，疑心我不是表里如一的？我还一直自诩，这小小的骗局天衣无缝呢。」

===Original===
“Well, in the first place, dash it, that dam’ fellow McTodd﻿—the real one, you know﻿—sent a telegram saying that he wasn’t coming. So it seemed rummy to Baxter bang from the start when you blew in all merry and bright.”

===Chinese===
「首先，嗨，就是麦克托德那小子——真麦克托德，你知道——拍了电报说不来了。所以你欢蹦乱跳地一进门，巴克斯特打头一眼就觉得邪门。」

===Original===
“Ah! That was what they all meant by saying they were glad I had come ‘after all.’ A phrase which at the moment, I confess, rather mystified me.”

===Chinese===
「啊！怪不得他们个个都说什么『终究』还是来了，我来了很高兴。这话当时着实把我弄糊涂了一阵。」

===Original===
“And then you went and wrote in the Peavey female’s autograph-book.”

===Chinese===
「后来你又跑到皮维那女人的签名簿上写字。」

===Original===
“In what way was that a false move?”

===Chinese===
「那一手错在何处？」

===Original===
“Why, that was the biggest bloomer on record, as it has turned out,” said Freddie vehemently. “Baxter apparently keeps every letter that comes to the place on a file, and he’d skewered McTodd’s original letter with the rest. I mean, the one he wrote accepting the invitation to come here. And Baxter compared his handwriting with what you wrote in the Peavey’s album, and, of course, they weren’t a dam’ bit alike. And that put the lid on it.”

===Chinese===
「嗐，事后看来，那是有史以来最大的昏招，」弗雷迪激愤地说。巴克斯特那家伙显然把这地方来的每一封信都存档，真麦克托德那封原信也跟他钉在一堆里。我是说，他答复邀请要来做客的那封。巴克斯特把他的笔迹跟你在皮维簿子上写的一对，当然，一丁点也不像。这下就盖棺定论了。」

===Original===
Psmith lit another cigarette and drew at it thoughtfully. He realised that he had made a tactical error in underestimating the antagonism of the Efficient One.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯点燃另一支烟，若有所思地吸着。他意识到，自己低估了能干的那一位的敌意，犯了个战术错误。

===Original===
“Does he seem to have any idea why I have come to the castle?” he asked.

===Chinese===
「他对我来城堡的缘由，可有什么想法？」他问。

===Original===
“Any idea? Why, dash it, the very first thing he said to me was that you must have come to sneak Aunt Connie’s necklace.”

===Chinese===
「想法？嗐，他跟我说的头一句就是，你必是来偷康妮姑妈的项链的。」

===Original===
“In that case, why has he made no move till today? I should have supposed that he would long since have denounced me before as large an audience as he could assemble. Why this reticence on the part of genial old Baxter?”

===Chinese===
「既然如此，他何以按兵不动直到今天？我本以为他早就该召集尽可能大的听众群，当众揭发我了。这位快活的老巴克斯特，为何如此讳莫如深？」

===Original===
A crimson flush of chivalrous indignation spread itself over Freddie’s face.

===Chinese===
侠义的义愤在弗雷迪脸上漫开一片绯红。

===Original===
“He told me that, too.”

===Chinese===
「那个他也跟我说了。」

===Original===
“There seems to have been no reserves between Comrade Baxter and yourself. And very healthy, too, this spirit of confidence. What was his reason for abstaining from loosing the bomb?”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特同志和你之间看来毫无保留。这种知无不言的精神，也很健康。他不投炸弹的理由是什么？」

===Original===
“He said he was pretty sure you wouldn’t try to do anything on your own. He thought you would wait till your accomplice arrived. And, damn him,” cried Freddie heatedly, “do you know who he’s got the infernal gall to think is your accomplice? Miss Halliday! Dash him!”

===Chinese===
「他说他断定你不会单枪匹马动手。他想你会等你的同伙到齐。而且，这该死的，」弗雷迪怒气冲冲地喊道，「你知道他吃了熊心豹子胆，疑心你的同伙是谁吗？哈利迪小姐！这个挨千刀的！」

===Original===
Psmith smoked in thoughtful silence.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯在沉思中吸着烟。

===Original===
“Well, of course, now that this has happened,” said Freddie, “I suppose it’s no good thinking of going on with the thing. You’d better pop off, what? If I were you, I’d leg it today and have your luggage sent on after you.”

===Chinese===
「唉，当然了，事已至此，」弗雷迪说，「我想这事再想办下去也是白搭。你最好快溜，嗯？我要是你，今天就撒腿，行李随后寄去。」

===Original===
Psmith threw away his cigarette and stretched himself. During the last few moments he had been thinking with some tenseness.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯抛掉香烟，伸展了一下筋骨。方才几分钟里，他一直在相当紧张地思考。

===Original===
“Comrade Threepwood,” he said reprovingly, “you suggest a cowardly and weak-minded action. I admit that the outlook would be distinctly rosier if no such person as Baxter were on the premises, but nevertheless the thing must be seen through to a finish. At least we have this advantage over our spectacled friend, that we know he suspects me and he doesn’t know we know. I think that with a little resource and ingenuity we may yet win through.” He turned to the window and looked out. “Sad,” he sighed, “that these idyllic surroundings should have become oppressed with a cloud of sinister menace. One thinks one sees a faun popping about in the undergrowth, and on looking more closely perceives that it is in reality a detective with a notebook. What one fancied was the piping of Pan turns out to be a police-whistle, summoning assistance. Still, we must bear these things without wincing. They are our cross. What you have told me will render me, if possible, warier and more snakelike than ever, but my purpose remains firm. The cry goes round the castle battlements ‘Psmith intends to keep the old flag flying!’ So charge off and soothe your quivering ganglions with a couple of aspirins, Comrade Threepwood, and leave me to my thoughts. All will doubtless come right in the future.”

===Chinese===
「思里普伍德同志，」他责备说，「你这提议懦弱而没脑子。我承认，只要庄里没有巴克斯特这么一号人物，前景无疑会光明得多。然而此事必须善始善终。至少我们比这位戴眼镜的朋友占着一项便宜：我们知道他疑心我，而他不知道我们知道。我想，略施机变与巧思，我们未尝不能笑到最后。」他转向窗口，望了出去。「可叹，」他叹道，「这般田园胜境，竟罩上了阴森杀机的乌云。恍惚以为灌丛里蹦跳的是头羊角小仙，定睛一看，原是个揣小本本的侦探。原以为是潘神的芦笛声，细听竟是召援的警笛。不过，这些我们都得咬牙扛住。这是咱们的十字架。你带来的消息，只会叫我比从前更谨慎、更蛇形，但我的决心分毫不动。城堡雉堞间响彻这样的呐喊——『皮史密斯誓让旌旗高扬不倒！』所以跑去服两片阿司匹林，安抚安抚你颤抖的神经节吧，思里普伍德同志，把我留给我的思绪。将来一切自会好转，殆无疑义。」

## IX Psmith Engages a Valet / 皮史密斯雇贴身男仆

### I / I

===Original===
From out of the scented shade of the big cedar on the lawn in front of the castle Psmith looked at the flowerbeds, jaunty and gleaming in the afternoon sun; then he looked back at Eve, incredulity in every feature.

===Chinese===
从城堡前草坪上那株大雪树芳香的浓荫里，皮史密斯望着午后天光下明艳照人的花圃；然后他回望伊芙，满脸的不敢置信。

===Original===
“I must have misunderstood you. Surely,” he said in a voice vibrant with reproach, “you do not seriously intend to *work* in weather like this?”

===Chinese===
「我一定是听岔了。 surely，」他用饱含责备的震颤嗓音说，「你总不至于当真打算在这种天气里*干活*吧？」

===Original===
“I must. I’ve got a conscience. They aren’t paying me a handsome salary﻿—a fairly handsome salary﻿—to sit about in deck-chairs.”

===Chinese===
「我必须干。我有良心。人家付我一份可观的薪水——相当可观的薪水——可不是让我坐躺椅的。」

===Original===
“But you only came yesterday.”

===Chinese===
「可你昨天才到啊。」

===Original===
“Well, I ought to have worked yesterday.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，我昨天就该干活来着。」

===Original===
“It seems to me,” said Psmith, “the nearest thing to slavery that I have ever struck. I had hoped, seeing that everybody had gone off and left us alone, that we were going to spend a happy and instructive afternoon together under the shade of this noble tree, talking of this and that. Is it not to be?”

===Chinese===
「依我看，」皮史密斯说，「这是我平生仅见的最接近蓄奴的东西。我本指望，既然人人都出门去了，只撇下你我，咱们可以在这株大树的浓荫下，共度一个快活而有教益的下午，谈天说地。难道不成吗？」

===Original===
“No, it is not. It’s lucky you’re not the one who’s supposed to be cataloguing this library. It would never get finished.”

===Chinese===
「不成。幸亏要给藏书室编目的不是你。要不编到下辈子也编不完。」

===Original===
“And why, as your employer would say, should it? He has expressed the opinion several times in my hearing that the library has jogged along quite comfortably for a great number of years without being catalogued. Why shouldn’t it go on like that indefinitely?”

===Chinese===
「而且，用你雇主的话说，为什么要编完？他当着我的面说过好几回了，那间藏书室没编目也舒舒服服过了这么多年。凭什么不能一直这么过下去？」

===Original===
“It’s no good trying to tempt me. There’s nothing I should like better than to loaf here for hours and hours, but what would Mr. Baxter say when he got back and found out?”

===Chinese===
「诱不了我。再没有比在这儿赖上几个钟头更叫我乐意的了，可巴克斯特先生回来发现了，会怎么说？」

===Original===
“It is becoming increasingly clear to me each day that I stay in this place,” said Psmith moodily, “that Comrade Baxter is little short of a blister on the community. Tell me, how do you get on with him?”

===Chinese===
「我住在这地方的每一天都越来越看清，」皮史密斯郁郁地说，「巴克斯特同志简直就是地方一害。说说看，你跟他处得如何？」

===Original===
“I don’t like him much.”

===Chinese===
「我不大喜欢他。」

===Original===
“Nor do I. It is on these communities of taste that lifelong attachments are built. Sit down and let us exchange confidences on the subject of Baxter.”

===Chinese===
「我也不喜欢。终身情谊正是建立在这样的共同品位上。坐下来，咱们就巴克斯特这个题目互通一下款曲。」

===Original===
Eve laughed.

===Chinese===
伊芙笑了。

===Original===
“I won’t. You’re simply trying to lure me into staying out here and neglecting my duty. I really must be off now. You have no idea what a lot of work there is to be done.”

===Chinese===
「我才不呢。你分明是想诱我留在这儿荒废职责。我真得走了。你想象不出等着干的活有多少。」

===Original===
“You are entirely spoiling my afternoon.”

===Chinese===
「你这是把我一下午全毁了。」

===Original===
“No, I’m not. You’ve got a book. What is it?”

===Chinese===
「才没有。你不是有书嘛。什么书？」

===Original===
Psmith picked up the brightly-jacketed volume and glanced at it.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯拾起那本封面鲜亮的书，瞟了一眼。

===Original===
“*The Man with the Missing Toe*. Comrade Threepwood lent it to me. He has a vast store of this type of narrative. I expect he will be wanting you to catalogue his library next.”

===Chinese===
「《缺趾人》。思里普伍德同志借我的。这类叙事作品他有一大宗收藏。我估摸他下回就要请你给他自己的藏书编目了。」

===Original===
“Well, it looks interesting.”

===Chinese===
「哟，看着挺有意思。」

===Original===
“Ah, but what does it *teach*? How long do you propose to shut yourself up in that evil-smelling library?”

===Chinese===
「啊，可它能*教人*什么？你打算把自己关在那间怪味藏书室里多久？」

===Original===
“An hour or so.”

===Chinese===
「一个钟头上下。」

===Original===
“Then I shall rely on your society at the end of that period. We might go for another saunter on the lake.”

===Chinese===
「那我就指望一个钟头之后与你做伴了。咱们不妨再去湖上荡一回。」

===Original===
“All right. I’ll come and find you when I’ve finished.”

===Chinese===
「行。我干完了来找你。」

===Original===
Psmith watched her disappear into the house, then seated himself once more in the long chair under the cedar. A sense of loneliness oppressed him. He gave one look at *The Man with the Missing Toe*, and, having rejected the entertainment it offered, gave himself up to meditation.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯目送她消失在宅子里，然后在雪松下的长椅上重新落座。一阵孤独感压上心头。他瞥了一眼《缺趾人》，谢绝了它奉献的娱乐，沉入冥想。

===Original===
Blandings Castle dozed in the midsummer heat like a Palace of Sleep. There had been an exodus of its inmates shortly after lunch, when Lord Emsworth, Lady Constance, Mr. Keeble, Miss Peavey, and the Efficient Baxter had left for the neighbouring town of Bridgeford in the big car, with the Hon. Freddie puffing in its wake in a natty two-seater. Psmith, who had been invited to accompany them, had declined on the plea that he wished to write a poem. He felt but a tepid interest in the afternoon’s programme, which was to consist of the unveiling by his lordship of the recently completed memorial to the late Hartley Reddish, Esq., J.P., for so many years Member of Parliament for the Bridgeford and Shifley Division of Shropshire. Not even the prospect of hearing Lord Emsworth﻿—clad, not without vain protest and weak grumbling, in a silk hat, morning coat, and sponge-bag trousers﻿—deliver a speech, had been sufficient to lure him from the castle grounds.

===Chinese===
布兰丁斯城堡在仲夏的暑热里打着盹，活像一座睡宫。午饭刚过，宅中人便倾巢而出：埃姆斯沃思勋爵、康斯坦丝夫人、基布尔先生、皮维小姐，还有能干的巴克斯特，坐大汽车前往邻镇布里奇福德，弗雷迪少爷开着一辆时髦的双座小车，喘着白烟缀在后面。皮史密斯也在被邀之列，却推辞了，理由是要写一首诗。他对下午的节目兴味寥寥：无非是勋爵大人为新近落成的纪念碑揭幕，纪念已故的哈特利·雷迪什绅士，治安官，多年来担任什罗普郡布里奇福德与希夫利选区的下院议员。就连听埃姆斯沃思勋爵演讲的前景——他将头戴大礼帽、身穿晨礼服、足登轻薄裤， protests 与嘟囔均告无效——也不足以把他诱出城堡园子。

===Original===
But at the moment when he had uttered his refusal, thereby incurring the ill-concealed envy both of Lord Emsworth and his son Freddie, the latter also an unwilling celebrant, he had supposed that his solitude would be shared by Eve. This deplorable conscientiousness of hers, this morbid craving for work, had left him at a loose end. The time and the place were both above criticism, but, as so often happens in this life of ours, he had been let down by the girl.

===Chinese===
然而，在他婉言谢绝、招来埃姆斯沃思勋爵父子二人毫不掩饰的艳羡的那一刻（做儿子的也是一名不情愿的典礼出席者），他原以为他的清静会有伊芙分享。她这份要命的责任心，这种病态的工作瘾，把他撂了个空。光阴与去处都无可挑剔，可是，正如人生中屡见不鲜的那样，他被姑娘放了鸽子。

===Original===
But, though he chafed for awhile, it was not long before the dreamy peace of the afternoon began to exercise a soothing effect upon him. With the exception of the bees that worked with their usual misguided energy among the flowers and an occasional butterfly which flitted past in the sunshine, all nature seemed to be taking a siesta. Somewhere out of sight a lawn-mower had begun to emphasise the stillness with its musical whir. A telegraph-boy on a red bicycle passed up the drive to the front door, and seemed to have some difficulty in establishing communication with the domestic staff﻿—from which Psmith deduced that Beach, the butler, like a good opportunist, was taking advantage of the absence of authority to enjoy a nap in some distant lair of his own. Eventually a parlourmaid appeared, accepted the telegram and (apparently) a rebuke from the boy, and the bicycle passed out of sight, leaving silence and peace once more.

===Chinese===
不过，他烦闷了一阵之后，午后那梦幻般的安宁很快便对他起了抚慰之效。除了照旧以其误导的干劲在花间劳作的蜜蜂，和偶尔翩然掠过阳光的一只蝴蝶，整个自然界似乎都在午睡。看不见的什么地方，一台割草机开始用它乐音般的嗡嗡声衬托寂静。一个电报童骑着红色自行车沿车道上前门来，似乎和家仆们建立联络颇费了些周折——皮史密斯由此推断，管家比奇这位良机主义者，正趁上头不在，在自己哪处遥远的巢穴里享受午睡。终于出来一位客厅女仆，收下电报，并且（看上去）挨了电报童一顿数落，自行车驶出视野，寂静与安宁重又君临一切。

===Original===
The noblest minds are not proof against atmospheric conditions of this kind. Psmith’s eyes closed, opened, closed again. And presently his regular breathing, varied by an occasional snore, was added to the rest of the small sounds of the summer afternoon.

===Chinese===
再高贵的心灵也挡不住这路天气氛围的侵蚀。皮史密斯的双眼合上，睁开，又合上。不多时，他那匀停的呼吸，间或夹一声鼾，加入了夏日午后细碎声响的大合唱。

===Original===
The shadow of the cedar was appreciably longer when he awoke with that sudden start which generally terminates sleep in a garden-chair. A glance at his watch told him that it was close on five o’clock, a fact which was confirmed a moment later by the arrival of the parlourmaid who had answered the summons of the telegraph-boy. She appeared to be the sole survivor of the little world that had its centre in the servants’ hall. A sort of female Casabianca.

===Chinese===
待他在花园椅上照例猛地一惊而醒时，雪松的影子已明显变长了。看一眼表，快五点了；片刻后这一事实得到确认——那位应过电报童召唤的客厅女仆来了。她似乎是用人食堂那个小天地里唯一的幸存者。一位女版卡萨比安卡。

===Original===
“I have put your tea in the hall, sir.”

===Chinese===
「我把您的茶放在大厅里了，先生。」

===Original===
“You could have performed no nobler or more charitable task,” Psmith assured her; and, having corrected a certain stiffness of limb by means of massage, went in. It occurred to him that Eve, assiduous worker though she was, might have knocked off in order to keep him company.

===Chinese===
「再没有比这更高尚、更仁慈的差事了，」皮史密斯向她保证；随即按摩了一番以解除肢体的某种僵硬，走了进去。他想到，伊芙虽然勤勉，说不定已经收工来陪他了。

===Original===
The hope proved vain. A single cup stood bleakly on the tray. Either Eve was superior to the feminine passion for tea or she was having hers up in the library. Filled with something of the sadness which he had felt at the sight of the toiling bees, Psmith embarked on his solitary meal, wondering sorrowfully at the perverseness which made girls work when there was no one to watch them.

===Chinese===
这指望落了空。茶盘上孤零零立着一只茶杯。要么是伊芙超脱了女性对茶的普遍热情，要么是她在楼上藏书室里喝自己的。怀着几分见了劳作蜜蜂时的那股惆怅，皮史密斯开始独自用茶，为姑娘们无人监督时偏要工作的乖张而黯然神伤。

===Original===
It was very agreeable here in the coolness of the hall. The great door of the castle was open, and through it he had a view of lawns bathed in a thirst-provoking sunlight. Through the green-baize door to his left, which led to the servants’ quarters, an occasional sharp giggle gave evidence of the presence of humanity, but apart from that he might have been alone in the world. Once again he fell into a dreamy meditation, and there is little reason to doubt that he would shortly have disgraced himself by falling asleep for the second time in a single afternoon, when he was restored to alertness by the sudden appearance of a foreign body in the open doorway. Against the background of golden light a black figure had abruptly manifested itself.

===Chinese===
大厅里凉爽宜人。城堡的大敞门开着，门外是一派晒得人直想喝水的阳光草坪。透过左手那扇通往仆役区域的绿呢门，偶尔一声尖细的咯咯笑证明着人烟的存在；除此之外，他大可以算是孑然一身于天地间。他再度坠入冥想，而且几乎无可怀疑，要不了多久他就要在同一个下午里第二次丢人地睡去——这时，敞开的门口突然出现一个不速之客，把他惊回了机警。金色的光幕背景上，一条黑影蓦地现了形。

===Original===
The sharp pang of apprehension which ran through Psmith’s consciousness like an electric shock, causing him to stiffen like some wild creature surprised in the woods, was due to the momentary belief that the newcomer was the local vicar, of whose conversational powers he had had experience on the second day of his visit. Another glance showed him that he had been too pessimistic. This was not the vicar. It was someone whom he had never seen before﻿—a slim and graceful young man with a dark, intelligent face, who stood blinking in the subdued light of the hall with eyes not yet accustomed to the absence of strong sunshine. Greatly relieved, Psmith rose and approached him.

===Chinese===
那一阵像电击般穿过皮史密斯意识的尖锐惊惶、那使他像林中受惊的野物般僵直的瞬间反应，是因为他一时以为来者是本地牧师——来做客的第二天他就领教过此公的谈锋。再看一眼，他知道自己过于悲观了。这不是牧师。这是一位他从未见过的人——一个瘦削优雅的青年，一张黝黑而机警的脸，正站在大厅幽暗的光线里眨眼，双眼还不惯于失去强烈的阳光。皮史密斯大放宽心，起身迎上去。

===Original===
“Hallo!” said the newcomer. “I didn’t see you. It’s quite dark in here after outside.”

===Chinese===
「你好！」来客说。「刚才没瞧见你。外头进来，这里头可够黑的。」

===Original===
“The light is pleasantly dim,” agreed Psmith.

===Chinese===
「这光线幽暗得宜人，」皮史密斯附和道。

===Original===
“Is Lord Emsworth anywhere about?”

===Chinese===
「埃姆斯沃思勋爵在吗？」

===Original===
“I fear not. He has legged it, accompanied by the entire household, to superintend the unveiling of a memorial at Bridgeford to﻿—if my memory serves me rightly﻿—the late Hartley Reddish, Esq., J.P., M.P. Is there anything I can do?”

===Chinese===
「恐怕不在。他撒腿跑了，阖宅倾随，去布里奇福德主持一座纪念碑的揭幕——如果我没记错，纪念的是已故的哈特利·雷迪什绅士，治安官，下院议员。有什么我能效劳的吗？」

===Original===
“Well, I’ve come to stay, you know.”

===Chinese===
「这个，我是来做客的，你知道。」

===Original===
“Indeed?”

===Chinese===
「是吗？」

===Original===
“Lady Constance invited me to pay a visit as soon as I reached England.”

===Chinese===
「康斯坦丝夫人邀我一到英国就来小住。」

===Original===
“Ah! Then you have come from foreign parts?”

===Chinese===
「啊！那您是打海外来的？」

===Original===
“Canada.”

===Chinese===
「加拿大。」

===Original===
Psmith started slightly. This, he perceived, was going to complicate matters. The last thing he desired was the addition to the Blandings circle of one familiar with Canada. Nothing would militate against his peace of mind more than the society of a man who would want to exchange with him views on that growing country.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯微微一惊。他察觉，这要坏事。他最不想要的，就是布兰丁斯圈子里添一位熟悉加拿大的人。再也没有比一位想同他交流那个蒸蒸日上国度之观感的人，更能搅扰他的清静了。

===Original===
“Oh, Canada?” he said.

===Chinese===
「哦，加拿大？」他说。

===Original===
“I wired,” proceeded the other, “but I suppose it came after everybody had left. Ah, that must be my telegram on that table over there. I walked up from the station.” He was rambling idly about the hall after the fashion of one breaking new ground. He paused at an occasional table, the one where, when taking after-dinner coffee, Miss Peavey was wont to sit. He picked up a book, and uttered a gratified laugh. “One of my little things,” he said.

===Chinese===
「我拍了电报，」对方接着说，「不过估计送到时人已经都走了。啊，那边桌上那份想必就是我的电报。我从车站一路走来的。」他正像初来乍到者那样，在大厅里信步闲逛。他在一张独脚小圆桌前停下——正是皮维小姐用晚餐后咖啡时常坐的那张。他拾起一本书，发出一声满足的笑。「我的一点小东西，」他说。

===Original===
“One of what?” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「什么的一点？」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
“This book. *Songs of Squalor*. I wrote it.”

===Chinese===
「这本书。《阴郁之歌》。我写的。」

===Original===
“You wrote it!”

===Chinese===
「你写的！」

===Original===
“Yes. My name’s McTodd. Ralston McTodd. I expect you have heard them speak of me?”

===Chinese===
「是啊。我叫麦克托德。拉尔斯顿·麦克托德。想必你听他们提起过我？」

### II / II

===Original===
The mind of a man who has undertaken a mission as delicate as Psmith’s at Blandings Castle is necessarily alert. Ever since he had stepped into the five o’clock train at Paddington, when his adventure might have been said formally to have started, Psmith had walked warily, like one in a jungle on whom sudden and unexpected things might pounce out at any moment. This calm announcement from the slim young man, therefore, though it undoubtedly startled him, did not deprive him of his faculties. On the contrary, it quickened them. His first action was to step nimbly to the table on which the telegram lay awaiting the return of Lord Emsworth, his second was to slip the envelope into his pocket. It was imperative that telegrams signed McTodd should not lie about loose while he was enjoying the hospitality of the castle.

===Chinese===
一个承担着皮史密斯在布兰丁斯城堡这样微妙使命的人，头脑必然时刻警觉。打从他在帕丁顿跨上五点钟的火车、他的冒险正式开场那一刻起，皮史密斯就走得步步为营，像穿行丛林、随时会有意外扑面而来的那人。因此，瘦削青年这句不动声色的通告，虽着实叫他心头一震，却没有夺去他的神智。相反，他的神智更锐了。他的第一个动作，是轻捷地跨到那张搁着电报、专候埃姆斯沃思勋爵归来的桌前；第二个动作，是把信封滑进自己口袋。凡署名麦克托德的电报，绝不能在他叨扰城堡期间散置在外。

===Original===
This done, he confronted the young man.

===Chinese===
办妥这两件，他迎向那青年。

===Original===
“Come, come!” he said with quiet severity.

===Chinese===
「岂有此理！」他以平静的严厉说道。

===Original===
He was extremely grateful to a kindly Providence which had arranged that this interview should take place at a time when nobody but himself was in the house.

===Chinese===
他深深感激仁慈的上苍，把这场会面安排在了宅中只他一人的时刻。

===Original===
“You say that you are Ralston McTodd, the author of these poems?”

===Chinese===
「你说你是拉尔斯顿·麦克托德，这些诗的作者？」

===Original===
“Yes, I do.”

===Chinese===
「是啊。」

===Original===
“Then what,” said Psmith incisively, “is a pale parabola of Joy?”

===Chinese===
「那么，」皮史密斯刀锋似的说，「欢乐的苍白抛物线，是什么？」

===Original===
“Er﻿—what?” said the newcomer in an enfeebled voice. There was manifest in his demeanour now a marked nervousness.

===Chinese===
「呃——什么？」来客的声音弱了下去。他的举止里此刻透着一股明显的心虚。

===Original===
“And here is another,” said Psmith. “ ‘The﻿—’ Wait a minute, I’ll get it soon. Yes. ‘The sibilant, scented silence that shimmered where we sat.’ Could you oblige me with a diagram of that one?”

===Chinese===
「还有这句，」皮史密斯说。「『那——』等一下，我马上想起来。有了。『那咝咝作响、芬芳的寂静，在我们落座之处微微闪光。』可否请你给这一句配张图解？」

===Original===
“I﻿—I﻿—﻿— What are you talking about?”

===Chinese===
「我——我——你在说什么呀？」

===Original===
Psmith stretched out a long arm and patted him almost affectionately on the shoulder.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯伸出长臂，近乎慈爱地拍了拍他的肩膀。

===Original===
“It’s lucky you met me before you had to face the others,” he said. “I fear that you undertook this little venture without thoroughly equipping yourself. They would have detected your imposture in the first minute.”

===Chinese===
「幸亏你先撞见的是我，还没轮到去见其他人，」他说。「恐怕你这趟小小的远征，装备不齐就上路了。他们不出一分钟就会识破你的冒名顶替。」

===Original===
“What do you mean﻿—imposture? I don’t know what you’re talking about.”

===Chinese===
「你说什么——冒名顶替？我不明白你在讲什么。」

===Original===
Psmith waggled his forefinger at him reproachfully.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯伸出食指，责备地朝他摇了摇。

===Original===
“My dear Comrade, I may as well tell you at once that the genuine McTodd is an old and dear friend of mine. I had a long and entertaining conversation with him only a few days ago. So that, I think we may confidently assert, is that. Or am I wrong?”

===Chinese===
「亲爱的同志，我不妨现在就告诉你：正牌的麦克托德是我的一位极老、极亲爱的朋友。就在几天前，我同他还进行过一次长而有趣的谈话。所以，我想咱们可以放心断言，就这么回事了。还是说，我错了？」

===Original===
“Oh, hell!” said the young man. And, flopping bonelessly into a chair, he mopped his forehead in undisguised and abject collapse.

===Chinese===
「哦，见鬼！」青年说完，骨头散架似的瘫进椅子，毫不掩饰地、彻底认栽地擦起额头。

===Original===
Silence reigned for awhile.

===Chinese===
一阵沉默。

===Original===
“What,” inquired the visitor, raising a damp face that shone pallidly in the dim light, “are you going to do about it?”

===Chinese===
「那么，」来客抬起一张潮湿的脸，在大厅幽光里泛着苍白，「你打算怎么办？」

===Original===
“Nothing, Comrade﻿—by the way, what is your name?”

===Chinese===
「不怎么办，同志——对了，你叫什么名字？」

===Original===
“Cootes.”

===Chinese===
「库茨。」

===Original===
“Nothing, Comrade Cootes. Nothing whatever. You are free to leg it hence whenever you feel disposed. In fact, the sooner you do so, the better I shall be pleased.”

===Chinese===
「不怎么办，库茨同志。 absolutely 什么都不办。你想什么时候开溜，随时请便。实话说，你越早走，我越高兴。」

===Original===
“Say! That’s darned good of you.”

===Chinese===
「喂！你可真够意思。」

===Original===
“Not at all, not at all.”

===Chinese===
「哪里哪里。」

===Original===
“You’re an ace﻿—”

===Chinese===
「你真是个好人——」

===Original===
“Oh, hush!” interrupted Psmith modestly. “But before you go tell me one or two things. I take it that your object in coming here was to have a pop at Lady Constance’s necklace?”

===Chinese===
「哦，快别说了！」皮史密斯谦逊地打断道。「不过临走之前，告诉我一两件事。我猜你来这儿的目的，是冲康斯坦丝夫人的项链下手的吧？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「是啊。」

===Original===
“I thought as much. And what made you suppose that the real McTodd would not be here when you arrived?”

===Chinese===
「我料到如此。你又凭什么认定，你到的时候正牌麦克托德不会在场呢？」

===Original===
“Oh, that was all right. I travelled over with that guy McTodd on the boat, and saw a good deal of him when we got to London. He was full of how he’d been invited here, and I got it out of him that no one here knew him by sight. And then one afternoon I met him in the Strand, all worked up. Madder than a hornet. Said he’d been insulted and wouldn’t come down to this place if they came and begged him on their bended knees. I couldn’t make out what it was all about, but apparently he had met Lord Emsworth and hadn’t been treated right. He told me he was going straight off to Paris.”

===Chinese===
「哦，那个稳当得很。我坐船跟那家伙麦克托德一路过来的，到了伦敦又常跟他泡在一块儿。他满嘴都是受邀来这儿做客的事，我套出了口风：这儿没人见过他的长相。后来有天下午我在河滨街碰上他，气得炸了锅。比马蜂还马蜂。说什么受了侮辱，就算他们跪着来求，也不上这地方来了。我摸不清怎么回事，反正他是见了埃姆斯沃思勋爵，没受着好待遇。他告诉我他直奔巴黎去了。」

===Original===
“And did he?”

===Chinese===
「他真去了？」

===Original===
“Sure. I saw him off myself at Charing Cross. That’s why it seemed such a cinch coming here instead of him. It’s just my darned luck that the first man I run into is a friend of his. How was I to know that he had any friends this side? He told me he’d never been in England before.”

===Chinese===
「那可不。查灵十字车站我亲眼送的他。所以我寻思顶他的名上这儿来，还不是手拿把攥。倒霉就倒霉在，头一个撞见的就是他朋友。我哪知道他在这边还有朋友？他自己说的，他从前压根没来过英国。」

===Original===
“In this life, Comrade Cootes,” said Psmith, “we must always distinguish between the Unlikely and the Impossible. It was unlikely, as you say, that you would meet any friend of McTodd’s in this out-of-the-way spot; and you rashly ordered your movements on the assumption that it was impossible. With what result? The cry goes round the Underworld, ‘Poor old Cootes has made a bloomer!’ ”

===Chinese===
「人生在世，库茨同志，」皮史密斯说，「须得分清『不大可能』与『绝无可能』。诚如你所言，在这天涯海角撞见麦克托德的朋友，不大可能；而你轻率地按『绝无可能』部署了行动。结果如何？黑道里喊声四起：『可怜的老库茨栽了个大跟头！』」

===Original===
“You needn’t rub it in.”

===Chinese===
「你就别往伤口撒盐了。」

===Original===
“I am only doing so for your good. It is my earnest hope that you will lay this lesson to heart and profit by it. Who knows that it may not be the turning-point in your career? Years hence, when you are a white-haired and opulent man of leisure, having retired from the crook business with a comfortable fortune, you may look back on your experience of today and realise that it was the means of starting you on the road to Success. You will lay stress on it when you are interviewed for the *Weekly Burglar* on ‘How I Began’﻿ ﻿… But, talking of starting on roads, I think that perhaps it would be as well if you now had a dash at the one leading to the railway-station. The household may be returning at any moment now.”

===Chinese===
「我这是为你好。我诚挚地希望你把这一课记在心里，引为鉴戒。谁知这不是不是你事业的转折点呢？多年以后，当你成了个白头发、有闲有钱的大款，揣着诈骗行当里挣下的一份舒适家当金盆洗手，回顾今天的遭遇，或许会意识到，正是它引你踏上了成功之路。到《每周窃贼》杂志来采访你谈『我是怎么起家的』时，你要着重讲这段……不过，说起踏上路程，我看眼下你最好还是朝火车站那条路冲刺一把。阖宅的人随时可能回来。」

===Original===
“That’s right,” agreed the visitor.

===Chinese===
「说得对，」来客表示赞同。

===Original===
“I think so,” said Psmith. “I think so. You will be happier when you are away from here. Once outside the castle precincts, a great weight will roll off your mind. A little fresh air will put the roses in your cheeks. You know your way out?”

===Chinese===
「我也这么看，」皮史密斯说。「我是这么看的。你离了这儿就快活了。一出城堡地界，一块大石头就从你心上滚下来了。来点新鲜空气，脸颊就红润了。出去的路认识吧？」

===Original===
He shepherded the young man to the door and with a cordial push started him on his way. Then with long strides he ran upstairs to the library to find Eve.

===Chinese===
他把那位青年牧送到门口，热情一送，送上了路。随即他大步流星跑上楼，去藏书室找伊芙。

===Original===
---

===Chinese===
---

===Original===
At about the same moment, on the platform of Market Blandings station, Miss Aileen Peavey was alighting from the train which had left Bridgeford some half an hour earlier. A headache, the fruit of standing about in the hot sun, had caused her to forgo the pleasure of hearing Lord Emsworth deliver his speech: and she had slipped back on a convenient train with the intention of lying down and resting. Finding, on reaching Market Blandings, that her head was much better, and the heat of the afternoon being now over, she started to walk to the castle, greatly refreshed by a cool breeze which had sprung up from the west. She left the town at almost the exact time when the disconsolate Mr. Cootes was passing out of the big gates at the end of the castle drive.

===Chinese===
几乎在同一时刻，在布兰丁斯集镇站的站台上，艾琳·皮维小姐正从半小时前发自布里奇福德的火车上下来。站在烈日下晒出的头痛，使她放弃了聆听埃姆斯沃思勋爵演讲的荣幸：她悄悄搭上一班方便的车回来，打算躺下歇歇。到了布兰丁斯集镇，发现头已大好，午后的暑气也已过去，她便起步行走回城堡，西边刮起的一阵凉风使她精神焕发。她出镇的时刻，几乎恰是郁郁寡欢的库茨先生走出城堡车道尽头大门的那一刻。

### III / III

===Original===
The grey melancholy which accompanied Mr. Cootes like a diligent spectre as he began his walk back to the town of Market Blandings, and which not even the delightful evening could dispel, was due primarily, of course, to that sickening sense of defeat which afflicts a man whose high hopes have been wrecked at the very instant when success has seemed in sight. Once or twice in the life of every man there falls to his lot something which can only be described as a soft snap, and it had seemed to Mr. Cootes that this venture of his to Blandings Castle came into that category. He had, like most members of his profession, had his ups and downs in the past, but at last, he told himself, the goddess Fortune had handed him something on a plate with watercress round it. Once established in the castle, there would have been a hundred opportunities of achieving the capture of Lady Constance’s necklace: and it had looked as though all he had to do was to walk in, announce himself, and be treated as the honoured guest. As he slouched moodily between the dusty hedges that fringed the road to Market Blandings, Edward Cootes tasted the bitterness that only those know whose plans have been upset by the hundredth chance.

===Chinese===
伴随着库茨先生开始步行返回布兰丁斯集镇的，是那份像尽职的幽灵一样寸步不离的灰色忧郁，连可爱的傍晚也驱不散它。这忧郁，首先自然是出于那种令人作呕的挫败感——眼看成功在望，宏图毁于顷刻的人才尝得到这种滋味。人生在世，偶尔会撞上一两桩只能用「美差」来形容的运气，而在库茨先生看来，他这趟布兰丁斯城堡之行正属此类。他这一行当的大多数成员一样，有过起落浮沉，可这回，他对自己说，命运女神总算端着盘子、围着水芹，把好运给他捧上门来了。一旦在城堡里站稳，偷取康斯坦丝夫人的项链有的是一百样机会；而他原以为，自己只需走进去，报上名号，便会被奉为上宾。当他沿着通往布兰丁斯集镇那条覆尘的树篱路垂头丧气地蹭行时，爱德华·库茨品尝着只有计划败于百分之一侥幸的人才识得的那份苦涩。

===Original===
But this was not all. In addition to the sadness of frustrated hope, he was also experiencing the anguish of troubled memories. Not only was the Present torturing him, but the Past had come to life and jumped out and bitten him. A sorrow’s crown of sorrow is remembering happier things, and this was what Edward Cootes was doing now. It is at moments like this that a man needs a woman’s tender care, and Mr. Cootes had lost the only woman in whom he could have confided his grief, the only woman who would have understood and sympathised.

===Chinese===
但这还不是全部。除了希望受挫的悲哀，他还经受着记忆翻腾的绞痛。不仅现在折磨着他，过去也还了魂，跳出来咬了他一口。悲哀的极致是追忆更快乐的时光，爱德华·库茨此刻干的正是这个。人在这种时刻最需要女人温柔的抚慰，而库茨先生恰恰失去了唯一可以倾诉忧伤的女人，唯一会理解他、体恤他的女人。

===Original===
We have been introduced to Mr. Cootes at a point in his career when he was practising upon dry land; but that was not his chosen environment. Until a few months back his business had lain upon deep waters. The salt scent of the sea was in his blood. To put it more exactly, he had been by profession a cardsharper on the Atlantic liners; and it was during this period that he had loved and lost. For three years and more he had worked in perfect harmony with the lady who, though she adopted a variety of names for purposes of travel, was known to her immediate circle as Smooth Lizzie. He had been the practitioner, she the decoy, and theirs had been one of those ideal business partnerships which one so seldom meets with in a world of cynicism and mistrust. Comradeship had ripened into something deeper and more sacred, and it was all settled between them that when they next touched New York, Mr. Cootes, if still at liberty, should proceed to the City Hall for a marriage-licence; when they had quarrelled﻿—quarrelled irrevocably over one of those trifling points over which lovers do quarrel. Some absurd dispute as to the proper division of the quite meagre sum obtained from a cattle millionaire on their last voyage had marred their golden dreams. One word had led to another. The lady, after woman’s habit, had the last of the series, and even Mr. Cootes was forced to admit that it was a pippin. She had spoken it on the pier at New York, and then passed out of his life. And with her had gone all his luck. It was as if her going had brought a curse upon him. On the very next trip he had had an unfortunate misunderstanding with an irritable gentleman from the Middle West, who, piqued at what he considered﻿—not unreasonably﻿—the undue proportion of kings and aces in the hands which Mr. Cootes had been dealing himself, expressed his displeasure by biting off the first joint of the other’s right index finger﻿—thus putting an abrupt end to a brilliant career. For it was on this finger that Mr. Cootes principally relied for the almost magical effects which he was wont to produce with a pack of cards after a little quiet shuffling.

===Chinese===
我们结识库茨先生的当口，他正在旱地上营生；但那并非他选定的用武之地。直到几个月前，他的买卖还开在深水之上。海水的咸香在他血液里。说得再确切些，他本行是大西洋邮轮上的纸牌老千；正是在那段岁月里，他恋爱了，又失去了。三年多来，他同那位女士合作得天衣无缝——女士出行时化名繁多，圈内人只叫她滑头莉齐。他管做局，她管诱敌，他俩是那个玩世不恭、互相提防的世界上罕有的那种理想生意搭档。同志情谊渐渐酿熟为更深、更神圣的东西，两人都说定了：下回船到纽约，库茨先生若还是自由身，就去市政厅领结婚证；不料他们吵翻了——为恋人们常吵的那种鸡毛蒜皮，吵得不可挽回。上次航程从一位养牛大亨身上分到的那笔区区之数该怎么分，这么一点荒唐争执，毁了他们的黄金美梦。一言不合，话赶着话。女士照女人的惯例，撂下了那串话里的最后一句，连库茨先生也不得不承认，那真是句绝的。她是在纽约码头上说的，说完便走出了他的生活。随之而去的是他的全部运气。她的离去仿佛给他带来了诅咒。紧接着的下一个航次，他同一位中西部来的火气先生闹了点不幸的误会——对方见库茨先生分到自己手里的牌里国王和尖儿比例过高，颇为光火（不能说毫无道理），一口咬掉了后者右手食指的第一节——一段辉煌事业就此戛然而止。要知道，库茨先生发牌前略施安静的手法便能变出近乎魔术的效果，靠的正是这根手指。

===Original===
With an aching sense of what might have been he thought now of his lost Lizzie. Regretfully he admitted to himself that she had always been the brains of the firm. A certain manual dexterity he had no doubt possessed, but it was ever Lizzie who had been responsible for the finer work. If they had still been partners, he really believed that she could have discovered some way of getting round the obstacles which had reared themselves now between himself and the necklace of Lady Constance Keeble. It was in a humble and contrite spirit that Edward Cootes proceeded on his way to Market Blandings.

===Chinese===
他此刻怀着对「本来可以怎样」的隐痛，想着他失去的莉齐。他黯然承认，她一向是这家商号的头脑。手上功夫他自信不缺，但细活儿从来出自莉齐。倘若两人还是搭档，他真心相信，她准能想出法子绕开眼下横在他与康斯坦丝·基布尔夫人项链之间的这些障碍。爱德华·库茨就是怀着这般谦卑与悔罪的心情，一步一步走往布兰丁斯集镇的。

===Original===
---

===Chinese===
---

===Original===
Miss Peavey, meanwhile, who, it will be remembered, was moving slowly along the road from the Market Blandings end, was finding her walk both restful and enjoyable. There were moments, it has to be recorded, when the society of her hostess and her hostess’s relations was something of a strain to Miss Peavey; and she was glad to be alone. Her headache had disappeared, and she revelled in the quiet evening hush. About now, if she had not had the sense to detach herself from the castle platoon, she would, she reflected, be listening to Lord Emsworth’s speech on the subject of the late Hartley Reddish, J.P., M.P.: a topic which even the noblest of orators might have failed to render really gripping. And what she knew of her host gave her little confidence in his powers of oratory.

===Chinese===
与此同时，前文交代过的皮维小姐，正从布兰丁斯集镇那一头沿路缓行，越走越觉这趟散步舒坦惬意。必须记录在案：皮维小姐也有觉得女主人及其亲眷的陪伴颇为吃力的时刻；她乐得独处。她的头痛消失了，她尽情享受傍晚的静谧。她想，若不是她有急智脱开了城堡纵队，这会儿就得听埃姆斯沃思勋爵大讲已故治安官、下院议员哈特利·雷迪什了——这个题目，只怕最高明的演说家也难讲出真正的抓人劲儿来。而她对自己东道的了解，更叫她对勋爵的口才不敢恭维。

===Original===
Yes, she was well out of it. The gentle breeze played soothingly upon her face. Her delicately modelled nostrils drank in gratefully the scent from the hedgerows. Somewhere out of sight a thrush was singing. And so moved was Miss Peavey by the peace and sweetness of it all that she, too, began to sing.

===Chinese===
是啊，躲开得值。微风拂面，她那双造型精致秀气的鼻孔，感恩地啜饮着树篱间的芬芳。看不见的什么地方，一只画眉在歌唱。这万般的安宁甜美如此打动皮维小姐，于是她也唱了起来。

===Original===
Had those who enjoyed the privilege of her acquaintance at Blandings Castle been informed that Miss Peavey was about to sing, they would doubtless have considered themselves on firm ground if called upon to make a conjecture as to the type of song which she would select. Something quaint, dreamy, a little wistful﻿ ﻿… that would have been the universal guess﻿ ﻿… some old-world ballad, possibly﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
布兰丁斯城堡里那些有幸与她相熟的人，若被告知皮维小姐要唱歌，而被要求猜一猜她会选哪一类歌，多半都自觉稳操胜券。有点古雅、有点梦意、有点怅惘的……这会是众口一词的猜测……兴许是支旧日谣曲什么的……

===Original===
What Miss Peavey actually sang﻿—in a soft, meditative voice like that of a linnet waking to greet a new dawn﻿—was that curious composition known as “The Beale Street Blues.”

===Chinese===
而皮维小姐实际唱的——用一种轻柔的、沉思的嗓音，像一只红雀醒来迎接新曙光——是那支人称《比尔街蓝调》的古怪作品。

===Original===
As she reached the last line, she broke off abruptly. She was, she perceived, no longer alone. Down the road toward her, walking pensively like one with a secret sorrow, a man was approaching; and for an instant, as she turned the corner, something in his appearance seemed to catch her by the throat and her breath came sharply.

===Chinese===
刚唱到末一行，她猛地收了声。她发觉，自己不再是孤身一人了。沿路朝她走来一个男人，步态怅惘，像揣着一段隐痛；她转过一个弯的一瞬间，他外形上的什么东西似乎攥住了她的喉咙，她的呼吸急促起来。

===Original===
“Gee!” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「哎呀！」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
She was herself again the next moment. A chance resemblance had misled her. She could not see the man’s face, for his head was bent, but how was it possible﻿ ﻿…

===Chinese===
下一刻她已经恢复了常态。只是机缘巧合的相像迷了她的眼。男人的脸看不见，他低着头；可是，怎么可能……

===Original===
And then, when he was quite close, he raised his head, and the county of Shropshire, as far as it was visible to her amazed eyes, executed a sudden and eccentric dance. Trees bobbed up and down, hedgerows shimmied like a Broadway chorus; and from out of the midst of the whirling countryside a voice spoke.

===Chinese===
接着，等他走得极近了，他抬起头来，什罗普郡——凡她惊愕的双眼所及——猛地跳了一支颠三倒四的舞。树木上下颠动，树篱像百老汇的歌舞队一样扭摆；而从天旋地转的乡野正中，一个声音开了口。

===Original===
“Liz!”

===Chinese===
「莉兹！」

===Original===
“Eddie!” ejaculated Miss Peavey faintly, and sat down in a heap on a grassy bank.

===Chinese===
「埃迪！」皮维小姐轻轻地喊了一声，一屁股坐在了草坡上。

### IV / IV

===Original===
“Well, for goodness’ sake!” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「哎哟，我的天哪！」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
