Shropshire had become static once more. She stared at him, wide-eyed.

===Chinese===
什罗普郡重归静止。她瞪着他，眼睛睁得溜圆。

===Original===
“Can you tie it!” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「你能信吗！」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
She ran her gaze over him once again from head to foot.

===Chinese===
她的目光把他从头到脚又扫了一遍。

===Original===
“Well, if this ain’t the cat’s whiskers!” said Miss Peavey. And with this final pronouncement she rose from her bank, somewhat restored, and addressed herself to the task of picking up old threads.

===Chinese===
「哎呀，这可真是猫胡子了！」皮维小姐说。发完这最后的感言，她从坡上站起身，神色大体复原，着手捡起旧日的话头。

===Original===
“Wherever,” she inquired, “did you spring from, Ed?”

===Chinese===
「你到底，」她问，「是打哪儿冒出来的，埃德？」

===Original===
There was nothing but affection in her voice. Her gaze was that of a mother contemplating her long-lost child. The past was past and a new era had begun. In the past she had been compelled to describe this man as a hunk of cheese and to express the opinion that his crookedness was such as to enable him to hide at will behind a spiral staircase; but now, in the joy of this unexpected reunion, all these harsh views were forgotten. This was Eddie Cootes, her old sidekick, come back to her after many days, and only now was it borne in upon her what a gap in her life his going had made. She flung herself into his arms with a glad cry.

===Chinese===
她嗓音里只剩柔情。她的目光，是母亲端详失散多年的孩子。过去的事过去了，新纪元开始了。过去她曾不得不把这个男人形容成一块干酪，说他那身歪骨头邪门到能随意藏进旋转楼梯后头；可如今，在这场意外重逢的欢乐里，那些刻薄话全忘了。这是埃迪·库茨，她的老搭档，多日之后回到她身边来了；直到此刻她才明白，他的离去在她生活里留下多大一个窟窿。她欢叫一声，扑进他的怀抱。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes, who had not been expecting this demonstration of esteem, staggered a trifle at the impact, but recovered himself sufficiently to return the embrace with something of his ancient warmth. He was delighted at this cordiality, but also surprised. The memory of the lady’s parting words on the occasion of their last meeting was still green, and he had not realised how quickly women forget and forgive, and how a sensitive girl, stirred by some fancied injury, may address a man as a pie-faced plugugly and yet retain in her inmost heart all the old love and affection. He kissed Miss Peavey fondly.

===Chinese===
库茨先生没料到有这份敬意的表达，被撞得踉跄了一下，但也稳住了，以几分旧日的热度还了这个拥抱。这份热诚叫他高兴，也叫他诧异。上次见面时女士临别赠言的记忆还青着呢；他不曾意识到女人忘得这样快、恕得这样快，不曾意识到一个敏感的姑娘被臆想的委屈一激，可以骂一个男人是饼脸恶棍，心底里却原封不动留着全部旧情旧爱。他深情地吻了皮维小姐。

===Original===
“Liz,” he said with fervour, “you’re prettier than ever.”

===Chinese===
「莉兹，」他热烈地说，「你比从前更好看了。」

===Original===
“Now you behave,” responded Miss Peavey coyly.

===Chinese===
「你可放规矩点，」皮维小姐娇嗔道。

===Original===
The arrival of a baaing flock of sheep, escorted by a priggish dog and followed by a couple of the local peasantry, caused an intermission in these tender exchanges; and by the time the procession had moved off down the road they were in a more suitable frame of mind to converse quietly and in a practical spirit, to compare notes, and to fill up the blanks.

===Chinese===
一群咩咩叫的羊的到来——由一条一本正经的狗押队，尾随两三个本地乡民——使这些缠绵的交流告一段落；等这一列队伍沿路走远，两人的心境已比较适合安安静静、脚踏实地交谈，交换情况，填补空白了。

===Original===
“Wherever,” inquired Miss Peavey again, “did you spring from, Ed? You could of knocked me down with a feather when I saw you coming along the road. I couldn’t have believed it was you, this far from the ocean. What are you doing inland like this? Taking a vacation, or aren’t you working the boats any more?”

===Chinese===
「你到底，」皮维小姐又问，「是打哪儿冒出来的，埃德？看见你沿路走过来，我下巴都惊掉了。离海这么老远，我做梦也想不到是你。你跑到内陆来干什么？度假呢，还是不跑船了？」

===Original===
“No, Liz,” said Mr. Cootes sadly. “I’ve had to give that up.”

===Chinese===
「不跑了，莉兹，」库茨先生悲戚地说。「只好洗手不干了。」

===Original===
And he exhibited the hiatus where an important section of his finger had been and told his painful tale. His companion’s sympathy was balm to his wounded soul.

===Chinese===
他亮出手指上缺了要紧一截的豁口，讲了那段惨痛的往事。同伴的同情如香膏，敷在他受伤的灵魂上。

===Original===
“The risks of the profession, of course,” said Mr. Cootes moodily, removing the exhibit in order to place his arm about her slender waist. “Still, it’s done me in. I tried once or twice, but I couldn’t seem to make the cards behave no more, so I quit. Ah, Liz,” said Mr. Cootes with feeling, “you can take it from me that I’ve had no luck since you left me. Regular hoodoo there’s been on me. If I’d walked under a ladder on a Friday to smash a mirror over the dome of a black cat I couldn’t have had it tougher.”

===Chinese===
「这一行的风险嘛，自然，」库茨先生郁郁地说，一边撤下展品好把胳膊搂上她纤细的腰。「可这下把我废了。我试过一两回，牌就是不听使唤了，索性撂挑子。啊，莉兹，」库茨先生动情地说，「你听我的，自打你离开我，我就没走过运。邪门到家了。就是我礼拜五从梯子底下走过去、拿镜子砸了黑猫的脑门，也就背到这份上了。」

===Original===
“You poor boy!”

===Chinese===
「你这可怜的孩子！」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes nodded sombrely.

===Chinese===
库茨先生黯然点头。

===Original===
“Tough,” he agreed, “but there it is. Only this afternoon my jinx gummed the game for me and threw a spanner into the prettiest little scenario you ever thought of﻿ ﻿… But let’s not talk about my troubles. What are you doing now, Liz?”

===Chinese===
「背，」他认账，「可背就背了。就在今天下午，我这霉运还坏了我的事，往你平生所见最漂亮的小剧本里扔了根撬棍……不过别提我的倒霉事了。你如今在干什么，莉兹？」

===Original===
“Me? Oh, I’m living near here.”

===Chinese===
「我？哦，我就住在这附近。」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes started.

===Chinese===
库茨先生一惊。

===Original===
“Not married?” he exclaimed in alarm.

===Chinese===
「没嫁人吧？」他 惊慌地问。

===Original===
“No!” cried Miss Peavey with vehemence, and shot a tender glance up at his face. “And I guess you know why, Ed.”

===Chinese===
「没有！」皮维小姐激烈地喊，朝他的脸投去温柔的一瞥。「缘故你该知道，埃德。」

===Original===
“You don’t mean﻿ ﻿… you hadn’t forgotten me?”

===Chinese===
「你莫非是说……你没忘了我？」

===Original===
“As if I could ever forget you, Eddie! There’s only one tintype on *my* mantelpiece.”

===Chinese===
「我哪能忘了你呢，埃迪！*我*那壁炉架上，可只摆着一个人的相片。」

===Original===
“But it struck me﻿ ﻿… it sort of occurred to me as a passing thought that, when we saw each other last, you were a mite peeved with your Eddie﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「可我总觉得……不知怎么灵光一闪地想到，咱们上次见面那会儿，你对你这埃迪好像有三分不痛快……」

===Original===
It was the first allusion either of them had made to the past unpleasantness, and it caused a faint blush to dye Miss Peavey’s soft cheek.

===Chinese===
这是两人头一回影射那段旧日的不愉快，它引得一层淡淡的红晕染上皮维小姐柔软的脸颊。

===Original===
“Oh, shucks!” she said. “I’d forgotten all about that next day. I was good and mad at the time, I’ll allow, but if only you’d called me up next morning, Ed﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「嗨，瞧你说的！」她说。「第二天我就全忘了。当时我气得不轻，这个我认。可你第二天早上哪怕打个电话来呢，埃德……」

===Original===
There was a silence, as they mused on what might have been.

===Chinese===
一阵沉默，两人各自想着本可以怎样。

===Original===
“What are you doing, living here?” asked Mr. Cootes after a pregnant pause. “Have you retired?”

===Chinese===
「你住在这儿干什么？」意味深长的停顿之后，库茨先生问。「退休了？」

===Original===
“No, *sir*. I’m sitting in at a game with real worthwhile stakes. But, darn it,” said Miss Peavey regretfully, “I’m wondering if it isn’t too big for me to put through alone. Oh, Eddie, if only there was some way you and me could work it together like in the old days.”

===Chinese===
「才不呢，*先生*。我正坐在一局真有大注的牌上。只是，见鬼，」皮维小姐惋惜地说，「我琢磨着，这局大得我一个人怕吃不下来。哦，埃迪，要是有法子让你我搭伙，像从前那样一起做，该多好。」

===Original===
“What is it?”

===Chinese===
「什么局？」

===Original===
“Diamonds, Eddie. A necklace. I’ve only had one look at it so far, but that was enough. Some of the best ice I’ve saw in years, Ed. Worth every cent of a hundred thousand berries.”

===Chinese===
「钻石，埃迪。一条项链。我到今儿为止只见过一眼，可一眼就够了。好些年没见过那么好的冰了，埃德。实实在在值十万个大洋。」

===Original===
The coincidence drew from Mr. Cootes a sharp exclamation.

===Chinese===
这巧合逼出库茨先生一声尖呼。

===Original===
“A necklace!”

===Chinese===
「项链！」

===Original===
“Listen, Ed, while I slip you the low-down. And, say, if you knew the relief it was to me talking good United States again! Like taking off a pair of tight shoes. I’m doing the high-toned stuff for the moment. Soulful. *You* remember, like I used to pull once or twice in the old days. Just after you and me had that little spat of ours I thought I’d take another trip in the old *Atlantic*﻿—force of habit or something, I guess. Anyway, I sailed, and we weren’t two days out from New York when I made the biggest kind of a hit with the dame this necklace belongs to. Seemed to take a shine to me right away﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「听着，埃德，我给你交个底。还有，喂，你知道说一口地道美国话对我多解乏吗？跟脱了双挤脚的鞋一个样。我眼下在演高雅路子。深情款款那一套。你记得的，从前我也偶尔来一两手。跟你我拌过嘴之后，我想不如再坐一回那条老『大西洋』——习惯成自然吧，大概。总之我上了船，出纽约没两天，我就跟项链的那位太太打得火热。她好像一眼就相中了我……」

===Original===
“I don’t blame her!” murmured Mr. Cootes devotedly.

===Chinese===
「这我不怪她！」库茨先生忠贞地喃喃道。

===Original===
“Now don’t you interrupt,” said Miss Peavey, administering a gratified slap. “Where was I? Oh yes. This here now Lady Constance Keeble I’m telling you about﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「不许打岔，」皮维小姐说着，赏了他一记心满意足的拍打。「我说到哪儿了？哦对。我跟你说起的这位康斯坦丝·基布尔夫人……」

===Original===
“What!”

===Chinese===
「什么！」

===Original===
“What’s the matter now?”

===Chinese===
「又怎么了？」

===Original===
“Lady Constance Keeble?”

===Chinese===
「康斯坦丝·基布尔夫人？」

===Original===
“That’s the name. She’s Lord Emsworth’s sister, who lives at a big place up the road. Blandings Castle it’s called. She didn’t seem like she was able to let me out of her sight, and I’ve been with her off and on ever since we landed. I’m visiting at the castle now.”

===Chinese===
「就是这名儿。她是埃姆斯沃思勋爵的妹妹，住在大路那头上的一座大宅子里。叫布兰丁斯城堡。她简直舍不得让我离开她的视线，打上了岸我就断断续续跟着她。这会儿正在堡里做客呢。」

===Original===
A deep sigh, like the groan of some great spirit in travail, forced itself from between Mr. Cootes’s lips.

===Chinese===
一声长叹，像某位伟大精灵临盆的呻吟，从库茨先生唇间挤了出来。

===Original===
“Well, wouldn’t that jar you!” he demanded of circumambient space. “Of all the lucky ones! getting into the place like that, with the band playing and a red carpet laid down for you to walk on! Gee, if you fell down a well, Liz, you’d come up with the bucket. You’re a human horseshoe, that’s what you are. Say, listen. Lemme-tell-ya-sumf’n. Do you know what *I’ve* been doing this afternoon? Only trying to edge into the dam’ place myself and getting the air two minutes after I was past the front door.”

===Chinese===
「哎呀，这还不把你乐死！」他冲着四面八方的空间质问。「也不看看谁有这福气！这么进得门去，还有乐队吹打，还铺了红毯子请你走！哎呀莉兹，你就是掉进井里，也能抓着水桶爬上来。你这个人形马蹄铁，就是这么回事。喂，听着。我给你讲个事儿。你知道*我*今天下午干什么来着？想混进那个鬼地方，结果前脚刚过大门两分钟就给轰出来了。」

===Original===
“What! *You*, Ed?”

===Chinese===
「什么！*你*，埃德？」

===Original===
“Sure. You’re not the only one that’s heard of that collection of ice.”

===Chinese===
「可不。听说那批冰的，又不止你一个。」

===Original===
“Oh, Ed!” Bitter disappointment rang in Miss Peavey’s voice. “If only you could have worked it! Me and you partners again! It hurts to think of it. What was the stuff you pulled to get you in?”

===Chinese===
「哦，埃德！」皮维小姐的声音里响着痛惜。「你要是成了多好！你我重新搭伙！想想都心疼。你当时使的什么招想混进去？」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes so far forgot himself in his agony of spirit as to expectorate disgustedly at a passing frog. And even in this trivial enterprise failure dogged him. He missed the frog, which withdrew into the grass with a cold look of disapproval.

===Chinese===
库茨先生在灵魂的绞痛中一时忘形，朝一只路过的青蛙厌恶地啐了一口。连这桩小小的事业里，失败也缠着他不放。他没啐中，青蛙带着冷冷的非难缩回了草丛。

===Original===
“Me?” said Mr. Cootes. “I thought I’d got it smooth. I’d chummed up with a fellow who had been invited down to the place and had thought it over and decided not to go, so I said to myself what’s the matter with going there instead of him. A gink called McTodd this was, a poet, and none of the folks had ever set eyes on him, except the old man, who’s too shortsighted to see anyone, so﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我？」库茨先生说。「本以为万无一失。我结交了一个受邀请来这地方、后来想了想决定不来的人，我就寻思，顶他的名来有什么不妥。是个叫麦克托德的家伙，诗人，那边没人见过他长什么样，就老爷子见过——可老爷子近视得谁都看不清，所以……」

===Original===
Miss Peavey interrupted.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐打断了他。

===Original===
“You don’t mean to tell me, Ed Cootes, that you thought you could get into the castle by pretending to be Ralston McTodd?”

===Chinese===
「你不会是想说，埃德·库茨，你以为装成拉尔斯顿·麦克托德就能进城堡吧？」

===Original===
“Sure I did. Why not? It didn’t seem like there was anything to it. A cinch, that’s what it looked like. And the first guy I meet in the joint is a mutt who knows this McTodd well. We had a couple of words, and I beat it. I know when I’m not wanted.”

===Chinese===
「可不。有什么不成？看着压根没有难度。手拿把攥，瞅着就这么回事。结果在那宅子里碰见的头一个家伙，就是认识这麦克托德的傻蛋。咱俩说了两句，我就撤了。我还不识趣吗。」

===Original===
“But, Ed! Ed! What do you mean? Ralston McTodd is at the castle now, this very moment.”

===Chinese===
「可是，埃德！埃德！你这说的是什么呀？拉尔斯顿·麦克托德这会儿就在城堡里，此时此刻就在。」

===Original===
“How’s that?”

===Chinese===
「哪有的事！」

===Original===
“Sure. Been there coupla days and more. Long, thin bird with an eyeglass.”

===Chinese===
「真的。都来两三天了。瘦高个儿，戴单片眼镜。」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes’s mind was in a whirl. He could make nothing of this matter.

===Chinese===
库茨先生的脑子转成了漩涡。这事他怎么也理不出头绪。

===Original===
“Nothing like it! McTodd’s not so darned tall or so thin, if it comes to that. And he didn’t wear no eyeglass all the time I was with him. This﻿ ﻿…” He broke off sharply. “My gosh! I wonder!” he cried. “Liz! How many men are there in the joint right now?”

===Chinese===
「不对吧！麦克托德没那么老高，也没那么瘦，真要论起来。而且我跟他一块儿那阵儿，他压根不戴什么单片眼镜。这……」他猛地顿住。「我的天！我明白了！」他喊道。「莉兹！那宅子里现在拢共几个男的？」

===Original===
“Only four besides Lord Emsworth. There’s a big party coming down for the County Ball, but that’s all there is at present. There’s Lord Emsworth’s son, Freddie﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「除了埃姆斯沃思勋爵就四个。郡舞会要来一大拨客人，可眼下就这几个。有埃姆斯沃思勋爵的儿子，弗雷迪……」

===Original===
“What does he look like?”

===Chinese===
「他长什么样？」

===Original===
“Sort of a dude with blond hair slicked back. Then there’s Mr. Keeble. He’s short with a red face.”

===Chinese===
「油头粉面的一个公子哥，金头发梳得溜光。还有基布尔先生。矮个儿，红脸膛。」

===Original===
“And?”

===Chinese===
「还有呢？」

===Original===
“And Baxter. He’s Lord Emsworth’s secretary. Wears spectacles.”

===Chinese===
「还有巴克斯特。埃姆斯沃思勋爵的秘书。戴眼镜。」

===Original===
“And that’s the lot?”

===Chinese===
「就这些？」

===Original===
“That’s all there is, not counting this here McTodd and the help.”

===Chinese===
「就这些了，不算这位麦克托德，再就是底下人。」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes brought his hand down with a resounding report on his leg. The mildly pleasant look which had been a feature of his appearance during his interview with Psmith had vanished now, its place taken by one of an extremely sinister malevolence.

===Chinese===
库茨先生一巴掌拍在大腿上，拍出一声脆响。先前与皮史密斯会面时他脸上那副温吞和气已经荡然无存，取而代之的是一种极其阴森的恶毒。

===Original===
“And I let him shoo me out as if I was a stray pup!” he muttered through clenched teeth. “Of all the bunk games!”

===Chinese===
「我还让他像轰野狗似的把我轰出来了！」他从牙缝里挤出这句。「这算什么事儿！」

===Original===
“What *are* you talking about, Ed?”

===Chinese===
「你*到底*在说什么呀，埃德？」

===Original===
“And I thanked him! *Thanked* him!” moaned Edward Cootes, writhing at the memory. “I thanked him for letting me go!”

===Chinese===
「我还谢了他！*谢*了他！」爱德华·库茨哀叹道，想起这事就浑身扭动。「我谢谢他放我走！」

===Original===
“Eddie Cootes, whatever are you﻿ ﻿… ?”

===Chinese===
「埃迪·库茨，你究竟……？」

===Original===
“Listen, Liz.” Mr. Cootes mastered his emotion with a strong effort. “I blew into that joint and met this fellow with the eyeglass, and he told me he knew McTodd well and that I wasn’t him. And, from what you tell me, this must be the very guy that’s passing himself off as McTodd! Don’t you see? This baby must have started working on the same lines I did. Got to know McTodd, found he wasn’t coming to the castle, and came down instead of him, same as me. Only he got there first, damn him! Wouldn’t that give you a pain in the neck!”

===Chinese===
「听着，莉兹。」库茨先生强压住情绪。「我闯进那宅子，碰上这个戴单片眼镜的家伙，他说他跟麦克托德很熟，说我不是他。照你刚才讲的，这不就是那个正顶着麦克托德名头招摇的家伙！你明白没？这小子八成跟我一条道儿起的家。认识了麦克托德，发觉他不来城堡了，就顶名来了，跟我一个路数。可他先到了一步，该死的！你说气不气人！」

===Original===
Amazement held Miss Peavey dumb for an instant. Then she spoke.

===Chinese===
惊愕让皮维小姐一时哑然。随即她开了口。

===Original===
“The big stiff!” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「大笨蛋！」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes, regardless of a lady’s presence, went even further in his censure.

===Chinese===
库茨先生全不顾有女士在场，把谴责又推进了一步。

===Original===
“I had a feeling from the first that there was something not on the level about that guy!” said Miss Peavey. “Gee! He must be after that necklace too.”

===Chinese===
「我打头一眼就觉得那家伙不对路！」皮维小姐说。「哎呀！他八成也盯着那条项链呢。」

===Original===
“Sure he’s after the necklace,” said Mr. Cootes impatiently. “What did you think he’d come down for? A change of air?”

===Chinese===
「他盯项链还用说，」库茨先生不耐烦地说。「你以为他下来干什么来了？换空气？」

===Original===
“But, Ed! Say! Are you going to let him get away with it?”

===Chinese===
「可是，埃德！喂！你打算就这么让他得手？」

===Original===
“Am *I* going to let him get away with it!” said Mr. Cootes, annoyed by the foolish question. “Wake me up in the night and ask me!”

===Chinese===
「*我*打算让他得手？」库茨先生说，被这愚蠢的问题惹恼了。「半夜里叫醒我问问我！」

===Original===
“But what are you going to do?”

===Chinese===
「那你打算怎么办？」

===Original===
“Do!” said Mr. Cootes. “Do! I’ll tell you what I’m going to﻿ ﻿…” He paused, and the stern resolve that shone in his face seemed to flicker. “Say, what the hell *am* I going to do?” he went on somewhat weakly.

===Chinese===
「办！」库茨先生说。「办！我告诉你我要怎……」他顿住，脸上那股严峻的决心似乎闪烁了一下。「喂，我到底打算怎办来着？」他有点软弱地接下去。

===Original===
“You won’t get anything by putting the folks wise that he’s a fake. That would be the finish of him, but it wouldn’t get *you* anywhere.”

===Chinese===
「你去给主人家揭穿他是冒牌的，捞不着好处。那样他是完了，可*你*也一寸挪不动窝。」

===Original===
“No,” said Mr. Cootes.

===Chinese===
「是不动，」库茨先生说。

===Original===
“Wait a minute while I think,” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「等等，让我想想，」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
There was a pause. Miss Peavey sat with knit brows.

===Chinese===
一阵停顿。皮维小姐皱眉坐着。

===Original===
“How would it be﻿ ﻿… ?” ventured Mr. Cootes.

===Chinese===
「要是这样……如何？」库茨先生试探道。

===Original===
“Cheese it!” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「住口！」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes cheesed it. The minutes ticked on.

===Chinese===
库茨先生住了口。分钟嘀嗒走着。

===Original===
“I’ve got it,” said Miss Peavey. “This guy’s ace-high with Lady Constance. You’ve got to get him alone right away and tell him he’s got to get you invited to the place as a friend of his.”

===Chinese===
「有了，」皮维小姐说。「这家伙在康斯坦丝夫人那儿红得发紫。你得马上把他单独弄到手，叫他想法子把你当他的朋友请进宅子。」

===Original===
“I knew you’d think of something, Liz,” said Mr. Cootes, almost humbly. “You always were a wonder like that. How am I to get him alone?”

===Chinese===
「我就知道你有主意，莉兹，」库茨先生说，几乎带着谦卑。「这种事你向来是一绝。我怎么把他单独弄到手？」

===Original===
“I can fix that. I’ll ask him to come for a stroll with me. He’s not what you’d call crazy about me, but he can’t very well duck if I keep after him. We’ll go down the drive. You’ll be in the bushes﻿—I’ll show you the place. Then I’ll send him to fetch me a wrap or something, and while I walk on he’ll come back past where you’re hiding, and you jump out at him.”

===Chinese===
「这我来办。我约他跟我遛弯儿。他算不上怎么稀罕我，可我黏住他，他跑不脱。咱们顺车道往下走。你埋伏在树丛里——地方我指给你。然后我差他去给我拿披肩什么的，我接着往前走，他回头必得路过你藏身的地方，你就跳出来堵他。」

===Original===
“Liz,” said Mr. Cootes, lost in admiration, “when it comes to doping out a scheme, you’re the snake’s eyebrows!”

===Chinese===
「莉兹，」库茨先生叹服得五体投地，「论憋坏水儿，你可真是蛇眉毛！」

===Original===
“But what are you going to do if he just turns you down?”

===Chinese===
「可他要是不容分说把你拒了呢？」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes uttered a bleak laugh, and from the recesses of his costume produced a neat little revolver.

===Chinese===
库茨先生发出一声凄冷的笑，从行头深处摸出一把精致的小左轮。

===Original===
“*He* won’t turn me down!” he said.

===Chinese===
「*他*敢不容分说！」他说。

### V / V

===Original===
“Fancy!” said Miss Peavey. “If I had not had a headache and come back early, we should never have had this little chat!”

===Chinese===
「真想不到！」皮维小姐说。「要不是我头疼早回来了，咱们哪有这场小聚！」

===Original===
She gazed up at Psmith in her gentle, wistful way as they started together down the broad gravel drive. A timid, soulful little thing she looked.

===Chinese===
两人并肩踏上宽阔的砾石车道时，她以她那温柔怅惘的方式仰望着皮史密斯。一副怯生生、情深深的可怜样儿。

===Original===
“No,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「是啊，」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
It was not a gushing reply, but he was not feeling at his sunniest. The idea that Miss Peavey might return from Bridgeford in advance of the main body had not occurred to him. As he would have said himself, he had confused the Unlikely with the Impossible. And the result had been that she had caught him beyond hope of retreat as he sat in his garden-chair and thought of Eve Halliday, who on their return from the lake had been seized with a fresh spasm of conscience and had gone back to the library to put in another hour’s work before dinner. To decline Miss Peavey’s invitation to accompany her down the drive in order to see if there were any signs of those who had been doing honour to the late Hartley Reddish, M.P., had been out of the question. But Psmith, though he went, went without pleasure. Every moment he spent in her society tended to confirm him more and more in the opinion that Miss Peavey was the curse of the species.

===Chinese===
这答话毫无热情，因为他此刻心绪不佳。皮维小姐可能先于大部队从布里奇福德折返——这一点他全然不曾虑及。用他自己的话说，他把「不大可能」混同于「绝无可能」了。结果便是：他坐在花园椅里思念伊芙·哈利迪（两人游湖归来，她又发了一阵良心痉挛，回藏书室赶晚饭前再干一个钟头去了），被她堵了个插翅难逃。皮维小姐邀他同下车道，看看给已故下院议员哈特利·雷迪什增光的各位可有回府的迹象——这邀请无法推辞。但皮史密斯虽去了，去得毫无乐趣。与她相处的每一刻，都愈发坐实他那个判断：皮维小姐是这一物种的祸害。

===Original===
“And I have been so longing,” continued his companion, “to have a nice, long talk. All these days I have felt that I haven’t been able to get as *near* you as I should wish.”

===Chinese===
「而且我一直这么盼着，」他的同伴接着说，「能同你好好的长谈一次。这些天来，我总觉得没法像我希望的那样*贴近*你。」

===Original===
“Well, of course, with the others always about﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「这个自然，旁人老在跟前……」

===Original===
“I meant in a spiritual sense, of course.”

===Chinese===
「我指的是心灵意义上的贴近，当然。」

===Original===
“I see.”

===Chinese===
「明白。」

===Original===
“I wanted so much to discuss your wonderful poetry with you. You haven’t so much as *mentioned* your work since you came here. *Have* you!”

===Chinese===
「我多想同你讨论讨论你那些美妙的诗。你来了以后，连自己的作品都*绝口*不提呢。*是不是*！」

===Original===
“Ah, but, you see, I am trying to keep my mind off it.”

===Chinese===
「啊，这个，你瞧，我正尽力不去想它。」

===Original===
“Really? Why?”

===Chinese===
「真的吗？为什么？」

===Original===
“My medical adviser warned me that I had been concentrating a trifle too much. He offered me the choice, in fact, between a complete rest and the loony-bin.”

===Chinese===
「我的医学顾问警告我，我近来用脑有点过甚。事实上，他给了我两个选择：彻底静养，或者疯人院。」

===Original===
“The *what*, Mr. McTodd?”

===Chinese===
「*什么*，麦克托德先生？」

===Original===
“The lunatic asylum, he meant. These medical men express themselves oddly.”

===Chinese===
「他指的是精神病院。这些学医的表达起来怪里怪气。」

===Original===
“But surely, then, you ought not to *dream* of trying to compose if it is as bad as that? And you told Lord Emsworth that you wished to stay at home this afternoon to write a poem.”

===Chinese===
「可要是这么严重，你*万万*不该想着创作呀？你今天下午还跟埃姆斯沃思勋爵说，要留在家里写诗呢。」

===Original===
Her glance showed nothing but tender solicitude, but inwardly Miss Peavey was telling herself that *that* would hold him for awhile.

===Chinese===
她的目光里只有温柔的关切，可皮维小姐心里正暗想，这一下够他支吾一阵了。

===Original===
“True,” said Psmith, “true. But you know what Art is. An inexorable mistress. The inspiration came, and I felt that I must take the risk. But it has left me weak, weak.”

===Chinese===
「是啊，」皮史密斯说，「是啊。可艺术是什么，你是知道的。一位说一不二的女主人。灵感来了，我觉得就得冒这个险。可它把我弄虚了，虚了。」

===Original===
“You **big stiff**!” said Miss Peavey. But not aloud.

===Chinese===
「你这个**大笨蛋**！」皮维小姐说。不过没出声。

===Original===
They walked on a few steps.

===Chinese===
两人走了几步。

===Original===
“In fact,” said Psmith, with another inspiration, “I’m not sure I ought not to be going back and resting now.”

===Chinese===
「实际上，」皮史密斯灵机又一动，「我都拿不准这会儿是不是该回去歇着了。」

===Original===
Miss Peavey eyed a clump of bushes some dozen yards farther down the drive. They were quivering slightly, as though they sheltered some alien body; and Miss Peavey, whose temper was apt to be impatient, registered a resolve to tell Edward Cootes that, if he couldn’t hide behind a bush without dancing about like a cat on hot bricks, he had better give up his profession and take to selling jellied eels. In which, it may be mentioned, she wronged her old friend. He had been as still as a statue until a moment before, when a large and excitable beetle had fallen down the space between his collar and his neck, an experience which might well have tried the subtlest woodsman.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐的眼角扫了下车道前方十几码外的一丛灌木。那丛灌木微微发颤，仿佛掩着什么异物；皮维小姐脾气素来缺乏耐心，当下拿定主意，回头要告诉爱德华·库茨：要是他连蹲灌木丛都要像热砖上的猫那样手舞足蹈，趁早改行卖冻鳗鱼去。这里得替她的老友说句公道话，她冤枉他了。直到片刻之前，他还一直纹丝不动如雕像；就在刚才，一只肥大而暴躁的甲虫掉进了他领子与脖颈之间的空当——这种遭遇，再老练的林中人也要经受考验。

===Original===
“Oh, please don’t go in yet,” said Miss Peavey. “It is such a lovely evening. Hark to the music of the breeze in the treetops. So soothing. Like a faraway harp. I wonder if it is whispering secrets to the birds.”

===Chinese===
「哦，先别进去嘛，」皮维小姐说。「多好的傍晚。你听树梢上微风的乐声。多熨帖。像一具遥远的竖琴。我在想，它是不是正把秘密说给鸟儿听。」

===Original===
Psmith forbore to follow her into this region of speculation, and they walked past the bushes in silence.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯忍住了没跟她踏进这片玄思的疆域，两人默然走过那丛灌木。

===Original===
Some little distance farther on, however, Miss Peavey seemed to relent.

===Chinese===
再往前走了一小段，皮维小姐似乎心软了。

===Original===
“You *are* looking tired, Mr. McTodd,” she said anxiously. “I am afraid you really have been overtaxing your strength. Perhaps after all you had better go back and lie down.”

===Chinese===
「你*真是*一脸倦容，麦克托德先生，」她焦灼地说。「我怕你当真是把力气使过了头。说到底，你还是回去躺躺的好。」

===Original===
“You think so?”

===Chinese===
「你这么看？」

===Original===
“I am sure of it. I will just stroll on to the gates and see if the car is in sight.”

===Chinese===
「我拿得准。我就自己溜达到大门口，看看汽车进没进视线。」

===Original===
“I feel that I am deserting you.”

===Chinese===
「这我就等于抛下你了。」

===Original===
“Oh, please!” said Miss Peavey deprecatingly.

===Chinese===
「哦，千万别这么说！」皮维小姐谦逊地摆手。

===Original===
With something of the feelings of a long-sentence convict unexpectedly released immediately on his arrival in jail, Psmith retraced his steps. Glancing over his shoulder, he saw that Miss Peavey had disappeared round a bend in the drive; and he paused to light a cigarette. He had just thrown away the match and was walking on, well content with life, when a voice behind him said “Hey!” and the well-remembered form of Mr. Edward Cootes stepped out of the bushes.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯转身往回走，那心情，像一名长刑期囚犯刚进监狱便意外获释。回头一瞥，皮维小姐已消失在车道拐弯处；他停下来点烟。刚甩掉火柴，正心怀满足地往前走，身后一个声音说「喂！」，爱德华·库茨先生那记忆犹新的身形从灌木丛里跨了出来。

===Original===
“See this?” said Mr. Cootes, exhibiting his revolver.

===Chinese===
「瞧见这个没？」库茨先生亮出他的左轮。

===Original===
“I do indeed, Comrade Cootes,” replied Psmith. “And, if it is not an untimely question, what is the idea?”

===Chinese===
「瞧见了， indeed，库茨同志，」皮史密斯答道。「若不算不合时宜的话——有何见教？」

===Original===
“That,” said Mr. Cootes, “is just in case you try any funny business.” And, replacing the weapon in a handy pocket, he proceeded to slap vigorously at the region between his shoulder blades. He also wriggled with not a little animation.

===Chinese===
「这个嘛，」库茨先生说，「就是防你来什么滑稽戏法的。」说着把凶器揣回一只称手的口袋，随即在两侧肩胛骨之间的区域啪啪猛拍起来。他还相当活泛地扭动着。

===Original===
Psmith watched these manoeuvres gravely.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯庄重地注视着这套动作。

===Original===
“You did not stop me at the pistol’s point merely to watch you go through your Swedish exercises?” he said.

===Chinese===
「你把我拦在枪口下，总不会就为看你表演瑞典体操吧？」他说。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes paused for an instant.

===Chinese===
库茨先生略一停顿。

===Original===
“Got a beetle or something down my back,” he explained curtly.

===Chinese===
「背上掉进去个甲虫什么的，」他简短地解释。

===Original===
“Ah? Then, as you will naturally wish to be alone in such a sad moment, I will be bidding you a cordial good evening and strolling on.”

===Chinese===
「啊？那，这种伤心的时刻你想必愿意独处，我就热忱地道个晚安，接着遛我的弯儿了。」

===Original===
“No, you don’t!”

===Chinese===
「不行，你走不了！」

===Original===
“Don’t I?” said Psmith resignedly. “Perhaps you are right, perhaps you are right.” Mr. Cootes replaced the revolver once more. “I take it, then, Comrade Cootes, that you would have speech with me. Carry on, old friend, and get it off your diaphragm. What seems to be on your mind?”

===Chinese===
「是吗？」皮史密斯听天由命地说。「也许你是对的，也许你是对的。」库茨先生又把左轮收了起来。「那么我理解，库茨同志，你有话要对我说。接着讲吧，老朋友，把心头的块垒吐出来。你心里搁着什么事？」

===Original===
A lucky blow appeared to have stunned Mr. Cootes’s beetle, and he was able to give his full attention to the matter in hand. He stared at Psmith with considerable distaste.

===Chinese===
看来幸运的一击已把库茨先生的甲虫打懵了，他得以把全部注意力放在手头的事情上。他十分嫌恶地瞪着皮史密斯。

===Original===
“I’m on to you, Bill!” he said.

===Chinese===
「我看透你了，比尔！」他说。

===Original===
“My name is not Bill,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「我不叫比尔，」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
“No,” snapped Mr. Cootes, his annoyance by this time very manifest. “And it’s not McTodd.”

===Chinese===
「对，」库茨先生厉声道，这时他的恼怒已经全写在脸上，「也不叫麦克托德。」

===Original===
Psmith looked at his companion thoughtfully. This was an unforeseen complication, and for the moment he would readily have admitted that he saw no way of overcoming it. That the other was in no genial frame of mind towards him the expression on his face would have showed, even if his actions had not been sufficient indication of the fact. Mr. Cootes, having disposed of his beetle and being now at leisure to concentrate his whole attention on Psmith, was eyeing that immaculate young man with a dislike which he did not attempt to conceal.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯若有所思地看着同伴。这是个未预料到的节外生枝，此刻他很愿意承认自己看不出破解之法。对方对他绝无善意，这一点从脸色上便看得出，纵然其行动尚不足以说明问题。库茨先生料理完他的甲虫，得以把全副注意力集中于皮史密斯，正以不加掩饰的厌恶打量着这位一尘不染的青年。

===Original===
“Shall we be strolling on?” suggested Psmith. “Walking may assist thought. At the moment I am free to confess that you have opened up a subject which causes me some perplexity. I think, Comrade Cootes, having given the position of affairs a careful examination, that we may say that the next move is with you. What do you propose to do about it?”

===Chinese===
「咱们边走边谈如何？」皮史密斯提议。「走路也许有助于思考。此刻我坦白承认，你抛出的这个题目让我颇费思量。我想，库茨同志，把局势仔细掂量过之后，咱们可以说：下一步棋在你。你打算怎么办？」

===Original===
“I’d like,” said Mr. Cootes with asperity, “to beat your block off.”

===Chinese===
「我恨不得，」库茨先生恶狠狠地说，「把你那颗脑袋揍扁。」

===Original===
“No doubt. But﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「毫无疑问。可是……」

===Original===
“I’d like to knock you for a goal!”

===Chinese===
「我恨不得把你打进球门！」

===Original===
Psmith discouraged these Utopian dreams with a deprecating wave of the hand.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯一摆手，劝阻了这些乌托邦梦想。

===Original===
“I can readily understand it,” he said courteously. “But, to keep within the sphere of practical politics, what is the actual move which you contemplate? You could expose me, no doubt, to my host, but I cannot see how that would profit you.”

===Chinese===
「我完全可以理解，」他谦和地说。「不过，咱们还是回到现实政治的范围内，你盘算中的实际行动是什么？你无疑可以向我的东道揭发我，但我不明白这对你有何好处。」

===Original===
“I know that. But you can remember I’ve got that up my sleeve in case you try any funny business.”

===Chinese===
「这我知道。可你记住，这张牌我揣在袖子里，就防你来什么滑稽戏法。」

===Original===
“You persist in harping on that possibility, Comrade Cootes. The idea seems to be an obsession with you. I can assure you that I contemplate no such thing. What, to return to the point, do you intend to do?”

===Chinese===
「你总揪着这个可能性不放，库茨同志。这念头都快成你的心魔了。我向你保证，我没打算来什么滑稽戏法。回到正题：你到底意欲何为？」

===Original===
They had reached the broad expanse opposite the front door, where the drive, from being a river, spread out into a lake of gravel. Psmith stopped.

===Chinese===
两人已走到正门前那片开阔地，车道在这里由一条河漫成一片砾石湖。皮史密斯站住。

===Original===
“You’ve got to get me into this joint,” said Mr. Cootes.

===Chinese===
「你得设法把我弄进这宅子，」库茨先生说。

===Original===
“I feared that that was what you were about to suggest. In my peculiar position I have naturally no choice but to endeavour to carry out your wishes. Any attempt not to do so would, I imagine, infallibly strike so keen a critic as yourself as ‘funny business.’ But how can I get you into what you breezily describe as ‘this joint’?”

===Chinese===
「我怕的正是你要提这个。处在我这微妙的地位，我自然别无选择，只能尽力满足你的愿望。我想，任何不从的企图，都逃不过你这样一位敏锐批评家的法眼，必被鉴定为『滑稽戏法』。可是，怎么才能把你弄进你轻快地称之为『这宅子』的地方呢？」

===Original===
“You can say I’m a friend of yours and ask them to invite me.”

===Chinese===
「你可以说我是你朋友，请他们邀我来。」

===Original===
Psmith shook his head gently.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯轻轻摇头。

===Original===
“Not one of your brightest suggestions, Comrade Cootes. Tactfully refraining from stressing the point that an instant lowering of my prestige would inevitably ensue should it be supposed that you were a friend of mine, I will merely mention that, being myself merely a guest in this stately home of England, I can hardly go about inviting my chums here for indefinite visits. No, we must find another way.﻿ ﻿… You’re sure you want to stay? Quite so, quite so, I merely asked.﻿ ﻿… Now, let us think.”

===Chinese===
「这可不算你最聪明的主意，库茨同志。姑且得体地不提这一层——一旦被认定你是我的朋友，我的威望势必即刻一落千丈——我只消指出：我自己在这座英国庄严古宅里也不过一介宾客，总不能四处发帖，请我的哥们儿来无限期小住。不行，得另想办法。……你拿准了想留下？是了，是了，我不过问问。……现在，咱们来想想。」

===Original===
Through the belt of rhododendrons which jutted out from one side of the castle a portly form at this point made itself visible, moving high and disposedly in the direction of the back premises. It was Beach, the butler, returning from the pleasant ramble in which he had indulged himself on the departure of his employer and the rest of the party. Revived by some gracious hours in the open air, Beach was returning to duty. And with the sight of him there came to Psmith a neat solution of the problem confronting him.

===Chinese===
正此时，一条富态的身影从城堡一侧探出的杜鹃花带里现了出来，高高大大、从容不迫地朝后院方向移动。是管家比奇，东家暨一行人马出门后，他尽兴遛了一大圈，如今归来。在露天待了几个称心的钟头，比奇精神焕发地回岗了。一看见他，皮史密斯灵光一闪，眼下这个难题的干净解法有了。

===Original===
“Oh, Beach,” he called.

===Chinese===
「哦，比奇，」他唤道。

===Original===
“Sir?” responded a fruity voice. There was a brief pause while the butler navigated into the open. He removed the straw hat which he had donned for his excursion, and enfolded Psmith in a pop-eyed but not unkindly gaze. A thoughtful critic of country-house humanity, he had long since decided that he approved of Psmith. Since Lady Constance had first begun to offer the hospitality of the castle to the literary and artistic world, he had been profoundly shocked by some of the rare and curious specimens who had nodded their disordered locks and flaunted their ill-cut evening clothes at the dinner-table over which he presided; and Psmith had come as a pleasant surprise.

===Chinese===
「先生？」一个醇厚的嗓音应道。管家侧身挪进空地，略停了停。他摘下遛弯专用的草帽，把皮史密斯罩进一双暴凸却并不恶意的凝视里。作为乡下宅邸人类的一位深思的批评家，他早就断定自己欣赏皮史密斯。打康斯坦丝夫人头一回把城堡的门面向文艺界敞开以来，一些珍稀古怪的标本曾在他主理的餐桌上摇着蓬乱的发绺、炫着剪坏了的晚礼服，令他深受震动；皮史密斯的到来是一份惊喜。

===Original===
“Sorry to trouble you, Beach.”

===Chinese===
「打扰了，比奇。」

===Original===
“Not at all, sir.”

===Chinese===
「别客气，先生。」

===Original===
“This,” said Psmith, indicating Mr. Cootes, who was viewing the scene with a wary and suspicious eye, an eye obviously alert for any signs of funny business, “is my man. My valet, you know. He has just arrived from town. I had to leave him behind to attend the bedside of a sick aunt. Your aunt was better when you came away, Cootes?” he inquired graciously.

===Chinese===
「这位，」皮史密斯指着库茨先生说——库茨先生正以警觉狐疑的目光注视着这一幕，那目光显然随时提防着滑稽戏法的苗头，「是我的人。我的贴身男仆，你知道。他刚从城里到。我先前不得不把他留下，照料一位卧病在床的姑妈。你动身时你姑妈好些了吧，库茨？」他和颜悦色地垂询。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes correctly interpreted this question as a feeler with regard to his views on this new development, and decided to accept the situation. True, he had hoped to enter the castle in a slightly higher capacity than that of a gentleman’s personal gentleman, but he was an old campaigner. Once in, as he put it to himself with admirable common sense, he would be in.

===Chinese===
库茨先生准确地解读出，这句问话是在探他对这一新局面的态度，便决定接受现状。诚然，他原指望以比绅士的私侍高一点的身份进城堡，可他是员老将。用他事后富于常识的自勉来说：一旦进去，人就在里头了。

===Original===
“Yes, sir,” he replied.

===Chinese===
「好些了，先生。」他答道。

===Original===
“Capital,” said Psmith. “Capital. Then will you look after Cootes, Beach.”

===Chinese===
「妙极，」皮史密斯说。「妙极。那就请你照应一下库茨，比奇。」

===Original===
“Very good, sir,” said the butler in a voice of cordial approval. The only point he had found to cavil at in Psmith had been removed; for it had hitherto pained him a little that a gentleman with so nice a taste in clothes as that dignified guest should have embarked on a visit to such a place as Blandings Castle without a personal attendant. Now all was explained and, as far as Beach was concerned, forgiven. He proceeded to escort Mr. Cootes to the rear. They disappeared behind the rhododendrons.

===Chinese===
「遵命，先生，」管家以热忱嘉许的口吻说。他在皮史密斯身上挑出的唯一毛病，就此消灭；因为此前颇令他痛心的一点是：一位衣着品位如此高雅的贵客，居然不带私人随从就跑到布兰丁斯城堡这种地方来做客。如今一切皆有了解释，就比奇而言，一切皆获了宽恕。他随即押送库茨先生前往后院。两人在杜鹃花丛后面消失了。

===Original===
They had hardly gone when a sudden thought came to Psmith as he sat once more in the coolness of the hall. He pressed the bell. Strange, he reflected, how one overlooked these obvious things. That was how generals lost battles.

===Chinese===
他们前脚刚走，重新坐进大厅清凉里的皮史密斯后脚就想到一件事。他按了铃。奇怪，他寻思，人怎么会漏掉这些明摆着的事。将军们输掉战役，正是这么输的。

===Original===
“Sir?” said Beach, appearing through the green baize door.

===Chinese===
「先生？」比奇应声从绿呢门后出现。

===Original===
“Sorry to trouble you again, Beach.”

===Chinese===
「再次打扰，抱歉，比奇。」

===Original===
“Not at all, sir.”

===Chinese===
「别客气，先生。」

===Original===
“I hope you will make Cootes comfortable. I think you will like him. His, when you get to know him, is a very winning personality.”

===Chinese===
「希望你让库茨住得舒服。我想你会喜欢他的。处久了就知道，他那人为人很有魅力。」

===Original===
“He seems a nice young fellow, sir.”

===Chinese===
「看着是个不错的小伙子，先生。」

===Original===
“Oh, by the way, Beach. You might ask him if he brought my revolver from town with him.”

===Chinese===
「哦，对了，比奇。你不妨问问，他从城里来时带没带我的左轮枪。」

===Original===
“Yes, sir,” said Beach, who would have scorned to betray emotion if it had been a Lewis gun.

===Chinese===
「是，先生，」比奇应道，就算那是挺刘易斯机枪，他也会不屑于动声色。

===Original===
“I think I saw it sticking out of his pocket. You might bring it to me, will you?”

===Chinese===
「我记得瞥见它从他口袋里支棱出来。麻烦你给我取来，可好？」

===Original===
“Very good, sir.”

===Chinese===
「遵命，先生。」

===Original===
Beach retired, to return a moment later. On the silver salver which he carried the lethal weapon was duly reposing.

===Chinese===
比奇退下，片刻即回。他托的银盘里，那件致命武器正安然躺着。

===Original===
“Your revolver, sir,” said Beach.

===Chinese===
「您的左轮，先生，」比奇说。

===Original===
“Thank you,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「谢谢，」皮史密斯说。

### VI / VI

===Original===
For some moments after the butler had withdrawn in his stately pigeon-toed way through the green baize door, Psmith lay back in his chair with the feeling that something attempted, something done, had earned a night’s repose. He was not so sanguine as to suppose that he had actually checkmated an adversary of Mr. Cootes’s strenuousness by the simple act of removing a revolver from his possession; but there was no denying the fact that the feel of the thing in his pocket engendered a certain cosy satisfaction. The little he had seen of Mr. Cootes had been enough to convince him that the other was a man who was far better off without an automatic pistol. There was an impulsiveness about his character which did not go well with the possession of firearms.

===Chinese===
管家以他那庄严的内八字步穿过绿呢门退下之后，皮史密斯在椅中靠了片刻，体会着一种「有志者事竟成，可安枕一夜」的感觉。他还不至于乐观到以为，仅仅没收一把左轮，就将军掉了库茨先生这样一位劲敌；但无可否认，那玩意儿揣在口袋里的手感，滋生出一种暖洋洋的满足。他与库茨先生打的那点交道，已足以令他信服：此人没有自动手枪时，境况要好得多。他的性子里有一股冲动劲儿，与持有火器不大相宜。

===Original===
Psmith’s meditations had taken him thus far when they were interrupted by an imperative voice.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯的冥想至此，被一声不容分说的嗓音打断。

===Original===
“Hey!”

===Chinese===
「喂！」

===Original===
Only one person of Psmith’s acquaintance was in the habit of opening his remarks in this manner. It was consequently no surprise to him to find Mr. Edward Cootes standing at his elbow.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯相熟的人里，只有一位惯用这种方式起头。因此毫不意外，他看见爱德华·库茨先生立在自己肘边。

===Original===
“Hey!”

===Chinese===
「喂！」

===Original===
“All right, Comrade Cootes,” said Psmith with a touch of austerity, “I heard you the first time. And may I remind you that this habit of yours of popping out from unexpected places and saying ‘Hey!’ is one which should be overcome. Valets are supposed to wait till rung for. At least, I think so. I must confess that until this moment I have never had a valet.”

===Chinese===
「行了，库茨同志，」皮史密斯带几分严峻地说，「你头一声我就听见了。容我提醒你，你这种从意想不到的地方蹦出来说『喂！』的习惯，应该改掉。贴身男仆照理该摇了铃再来。至少，我是这么想的。坦白说，此刻之前我从来没雇过贴身男仆。」

===Original===
“And you wouldn’t have one now if I could help it,” responded Mr. Cootes.

===Chinese===
「要是我能做主，你这会儿也雇不上，」库茨先生回敬道。

===Original===
Psmith raised his eyebrows.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯挑了挑眉。

===Original===
“Why,” he inquired, surprised, “this peevishness? Don’t you like being a valet?”

===Chinese===
「咦，」他讶异地问，「哪来这股怨气？当贴身男仆不好吗？」

===Original===
“No, I don’t.”

===Chinese===
「不好。」

===Original===
“You astonish me. I should have thought you would have gone singing about the house. Have you considered that the tenancy of such a position throws you into the constant society of Comrade Beach, than whom it would be difficult to imagine a more delightful companion?”

===Chinese===
「你可真让我吃惊。我原以为你会在宅子里引吭高歌呢。你想过没有，这个职位的在任，使你得以与比奇同志朝夕相处——再难想象出比他更可人的伙伴了。」

===Original===
“Old stiff!” said Mr. Cootes sourly. “If there’s one thing that makes me tired, it’s a guy that talks about his darned stomach all the time.”

===Chinese===
「老古板！」库茨先生酸溜溜地说。「要说有什么让人腻烦的，就是那种整天价念叨他那宝贝胃口的哥们儿。」

===Original===
“I beg your pardon?”

===Chinese===
「你说什么？」

===Original===
“The Beach gook,” explained Mr. Cootes, “has got something wrong with the lining of his stomach, and if I hadn’t made my getaway he’d be talking about it yet.”

===Chinese===
「比奇这个家伙，」库茨先生解释道，「胃的里子上出了毛病，我要不是脚底抹油，他这会儿还在讲呢。」

===Original===
“If you fail to find entertainment and uplift in firsthand information about Comrade Beach’s stomach, you must indeed be hard to please. I am to take it, then, that you came snorting out here, interrupting my daydreams, merely in order to seek my sympathy?”

===Chinese===
「连比奇同志胃部的第一手资料都寻不着乐趣和教益，你可真是难伺候。那么我是否可以理解为，你冲到这儿来、打断我的白日梦，专为讨我的同情来了？」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes gazed upon him with a smouldering eye.

===Chinese===
库茨先生用一只阴燃的眼瞪着他。

===Original===
“I came to tell you I suppose you think you’re darned smart.”

===Chinese===
「我来告诉你，你八成觉得自己精得冒尖儿吧。」

===Original===
“And very nice of you, too,” said Psmith, touched. “A pretty compliment, for which I am not ungrateful.”

===Chinese===
「多谢夸奖，」皮史密斯感动地说。「一句漂亮的恭维，我心领了。」

===Original===
“You got that gun away from me mighty smoothly, didn’t you?”

===Chinese===
「你那把枪从我这儿缴得溜光水滑，是吧？」

===Original===
“Since you mention it, yes.”

===Chinese===
「既然你提起来了——是。」

===Original===
“And now I suppose you think you’re going to slip in ahead of me and get away with that necklace? Well, say, listen, lemme tell you it’ll take someone better than a half-baked string-bean like you to put one over on me.”

===Chinese===
「这会儿你八成盘算着抢到我头里，把项链卷走吧？好啊，你听着，我告诉你：想算计我，得换个比你这种半生不熟的豆芽菜强点的货色。」

===Original===
“I seem,” said Psmith, pained, “to detect a certain animus creeping into your tone. Surely we can be trade rivals without this spirit of hostility. My attitude towards you is one of kindly tolerance.”

===Chinese===
「我似乎，」皮史密斯痛心地说，「从你的腔调里察觉出一点敌意。同行相争，何必结仇。我对你的态度，是宽厚的容忍。」

===Original===
“Even if you get it, where do you think you’re going to hide it? And, believe me, it’ll take some hiding. Say, lemme tell you something. I’m your valet, ain’t I? Well, then, I can come into your room and be tidying up whenever I darn please, can’t I? Sure I can. I’ll tell the world I can do just that little thing. And you take it from me, Bill﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「就算你偷到手，你往哪儿藏？信我的，那可够藏的。喂，我给你讲个事儿。我是你的贴身男仆，对不？那么，我乐意什么时候进你屋子收拾收拾，就什么时候收拾，对不？当然对。这我拿得准。你听好了，比尔……」

===Original===
“You persist in the delusion that my name is William﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「你固执地抱有错觉，以为我叫威廉……」

===Original===
“You take it from me, Bill, that if ever that necklace disappears and it isn’t me that’s done the disappearing, you’ll find me tidying up in a way that’ll make you dizzy. I’ll go through that room of yours with a fine-tooth comb. So chew on that, will you?”

===Chinese===
「你听好了，比尔：那条项链几时没了影儿，又不是本人我没了影儿，你就瞧好吧，我会把你的屋子收拾得叫你眼晕。我拿篦子把你那间屋从头篦到尾。这话你给我嚼烂了咽下去，成不成？」

===Original===
And Edward Cootes, moving sombrely across the hall, made a sinister exit. The mood of cool reflection was still to come, when he would realise that, in his desire to administer what he would have described as a hot one, he had acted a little rashly in putting his enemy on his guard. All he was thinking now was that his brief sketch of the position of affairs would have the effect of diminishing Psmith’s complacency a trifle. He had, he flattered himself, slipped over something that could be classed as a jolt.

===Chinese===
爱德华·库茨说完，阴沉沉横穿大厅，退了一个阴森的场。冷静反省的时刻尚未到来——到那时他会意识到，自己一心要给对方吃一记他所谓的「热乎的」，却把敌人惊入了戒备，未免行事孟浪。此刻他满脑子只有一桩：他刚才对局势的简短勾勒，多少能挫一挫皮史密斯的自满。他自我恭维地想，方才那番话，够格算一记闷棍。

===Original===
Nor was he unjustified in this view. The aspect of the matter on which he had touched was one that had not previously presented itself to Psmith: and, musing on it as he resettled himself in his chair, he could see that it afforded food for thought. As regarded the disposal of the necklace, should it ever come into his possession, he had formed no definite plan. He had assumed that he would conceal it somewhere until the first excitement of the chase slackened, and it was only now that he realised the difficulty of finding a suitable hiding-place outside his bedroom. Yes, it was certainly a matter on which, as Mr. Cootes had suggested, he would do well to chew. For ten minutes, accordingly, he did so. And﻿—it being practically impossible to keep a good man down﻿—at the end of that period he was rewarded with an idea. He rose from his chair and pressed the bell.

===Chinese===
这么看倒也没冤枉他。他点到的那层，皮史密斯先前不曾虑及：在椅中重新坐定、细细咀嚼之后，他看出这确是一道思考题。至于项链到手之后如何处置，他原无成算。他本打算先藏到风头过去再说，直到此刻才意识到，要在自己卧室之外觅一处妥当的藏所，谈何容易。是啊，诚如库茨先生所言，这确实是值得好好嚼一嚼的题目。于是他嚼了十分钟。而且——好人是压不垮的——十分钟期满，他收获了一个主意。他从椅中起身，按铃。

===Original===
“Ah, Beach,” he said affably, as the green baize door swung open, “I must apologise once more for troubling you. I keep ringing, don’t I?”

===Chinese===
「啊，比奇，」绿呢门旋开时，他和蔼地说，「我得再一次为打扰你赔罪。我老按铃，是吧？」

===Original===
“No trouble at all, sir,” responded the butler paternally. “But if you were ringing to summon your personal attendant, I fear he is not immediately available. He left me somewhat abruptly a few moments ago. I was not aware that you would be requiring his services until the dressing-gong sounded, or I would have detained him.”

===Chinese===
「一点也不麻烦，先生，」管家慈父般地应道。「不过您若是摇铃召唤您的私人侍从，恐怕他一时半会儿不在。他方才颇突然地离我而去。我原不知道换衣锣响之前您会需要他侍候，不然就留住他了。」

===Original===
“Never mind. It was you I wished to see. Beach,” said Psmith, “I am concerned about you. I learn from my man that the lining of your stomach is not all it should be.”

===Chinese===
「不妨事。我要找的正是你。比奇，」皮史密斯说，「我在为你操心。听我那位手下说，你胃的里子不太像个样子。」

===Original===
“That is true, sir,” replied Beach, an excited gleam coming into his dull eyes. He shivered slightly, as might a warhorse at the sound of the bugle. “I do have trouble with the lining of my stomach.”

===Chinese===
「确实如此，先生，」比奇答道，呆滞的双眼里迸出一点兴奋的光。他微微一颤，像战马听见了号角。「我胃的里子确实常给我找麻烦。」

===Original===
“Every stomach has a silver lining.”

===Chinese===
「每只胃都有一层银里子。」

===Original===
“Sir?”

===Chinese===
「先生？」

===Original===
“I said, tell me all about it.”

===Chinese===
「我说，从头讲给我听。」

===Original===
“Well, really, sir﻿ ﻿…” said Beach wistfully.

===Chinese===
「这个，当真，先生……」比奇渴慕地说。

===Original===
“To please me,” urged Psmith.

===Chinese===
「给我个面子，」皮史密斯敦促道。

===Original===
“Well, sir, it is extremely kind of you to take an interest. It generally starts with a dull shooting pain on the right side of the abdomen from twenty minutes to half an hour after the conclusion of a meal. The symptoms﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「好吧，先生，您肯垂顾，实在抬举我了。通常是从一餐饭后二十分钟到半个钟头起，腹部右侧发起一阵钝钝的窜痛。这病症……」

===Original===
There was nothing but courteous sympathy in Psmith’s gaze as he listened to what sounded like an eyewitness’s account of the San Francisco earthquake, but inwardly he was wishing that his companion could see his way to making it a bit briefer and snappier. However, all things come to an end. Even the weariest river winds somewhere to the sea. With a moving period, the butler finally concluded his narrative.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯望着他，目光里只有礼貌的同情，耳朵里灌着像旧金山大地震目击实录的东西；可他心里正盼着同伴能把这段叙述弄得简短些、利落些。然而，万事皆有尽头。再累的河也终要入海。管家终于以一个感人的句子收束了他的叙事。

===Original===
“Parks’ Pepsinine,” said Psmith promptly.

===Chinese===
「帕克斯胃蛋白酶，」皮史密斯应声说。

===Original===
“Sir?”

===Chinese===
「先生？」

===Original===
“That’s what you want. Parks’ Pepsinine. It would set you right in no time.”

===Chinese===
「你要的就是这个。帕克斯胃蛋白酶。保管药到病除。」

===Original===
“I will make a note of the name, sir. The specific has not come to my notice until now. And, if I may say so,” added Beach, with a glassy but adoring look at his benefactor, “I should like to express my gratitude for your kindness.”

===Chinese===
「我记下这名字，先生。此药此前不曾入过我的耳目。而且恕我冒昧，」比奇补充道，用一种发直却崇拜的目光望着他的恩人，「请容我向您的好意致谢。」

===Original===
“Not at all, Beach, not at all. Oh, Beach,” he said, as the other started to manoeuvre towards the door, “I’ve just remembered. There was something else I wanted to talk to you about.”

===Chinese===
「哪里的话，比奇，哪里的话。哦，比奇，」对方刚要朝门口挪动，他又说，「我刚想起来。还有件事想同你商量。」

===Original===
“Yes, sir?”

===Chinese===
「先生？」

===Original===
“I thought it might be as well to speak to you about it before approaching Lady Constance. The fact is, Beach, I am feeling cramped.”

===Chinese===
「我想还是先跟你通气，再去见康斯坦丝夫人为好。实情是，比奇，我觉得憋闷。」

===Original===
“Indeed, sir? I forgot to mention that one of the symptoms from which I suffer is a sharp cramp.”

===Chinese===
「当真，先生？忘了说了，我犯的病症之一正是一种厉害的闷痛。」

===Original===
“Too bad. But let us, if you do not mind, shelve for the moment the subject of your interior organism and its ailments. When I say I am feeling cramped, I mean spiritually. Have you ever written poetry, Beach?”

===Chinese===
「可惜。不过，若不见怪，咱们暂且把你体内的器官及其疾患搁一搁。我说憋闷，指的是灵性上的。你写过诗吗，比奇？」

===Original===
“No, sir.”

===Chinese===
「没有，先生。」

===Original===
“Ah! Then it may be a little difficult for you to understand my feelings. My trouble is this. Out in Canada, Beach, I grew accustomed to doing my work in the most solitary surroundings. You remember that passage in my *Songs of Squalor* which begins ‘Across the pale parabola of Joy﻿ ﻿…’?”

===Chinese===
「啊！那你恐怕有点难于体会我的心情。我的难处是这样的。在加拿大那边，比奇，我习惯了在四顾无人的环境里创作。还记得我那本《阴郁之歌》里以『穿越欢乐那苍白的抛物线……』开头的那段吗？」

===Original===
“I fear, sir﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「恐怕，先生……」

===Original===
“You missed it? Tough luck. Try to get hold of it some time. It’s a bird. Well, that passage was written in a lonely hut on the banks of the Saskatchewan, miles away from human habitation. I am like that, Beach. I need the stimulus of the great open spaces. When I am surrounded by my fellows, inspiration slackens and dies. You know how it is when there are people about. Just as you are starting in to write a nifty, someone comes and sits down on the desk and begins talking about himself. Every time you get going nicely, in barges some alien influence and the Muse goes blooey. You see what I mean?”

===Chinese===
「漏读了？可惜。改天找来读读。是只好鸟。嗯，那段文字，就是在萨斯喀彻温河岸上一间孤零零的小屋里写成的，方圆几里没人烟。我就是这样的人，比奇。我需要大旷野的刺激。一被同类包围，灵感就委顿，就断气。人多的地方什么样，你是知道的。你刚要动笔写点俏皮东西，就有人过来往你桌上一坐，大谈起他自己来。每回你刚进入状态，就有什么不相干的影响闯进来，缪斯就完蛋。你懂我的意思吧？」

===Original===
“Yes, sir,” said Beach, gaping slightly.

===Chinese===
「懂，先生，」比奇说着，嘴微张。

===Original===
“Well, that is why for a man like me existence in Blandings Castle has its drawbacks. I have got to get a place where I can be alone, Beach﻿—alone with my dreams and visions. Some little eyrie perched on the cliffs of Time.﻿ ﻿… In other words, do you know of an empty cottage somewhere on the estate where I could betake myself when in the mood and swing a nib without any possibility of being interrupted?”

===Chinese===
「所以，对像我这样的人来说，布兰丁斯城堡的日子有它的难处。我必须弄到一个能独处的地方，比奇——独自对着我的梦与幻。一个蹲踞在时间之崖上的小鹰巢。……换句话说，庄园里有没有一间空着的小屋，能让我兴之所至时前去，挥一挥笔尖而绝无被打搅之虞？你可知道这么一间？」

===Original===
“A little cottage, sir?”

===Chinese===
「一间小屋，先生？」

===Original===
“A little cottage. With honeysuckle over the door, and Old Mister Moon climbing up above the trees. A cottage, Beach, where I can meditate, where I can turn the key in the door and bid the world go by. Now that the castle is going to be full of all these people who are coming for the County Ball, it is imperative that I wangle such a haven. Otherwise, a considerable slab of priceless poetry will be lost to humanity for ever.”

===Chinese===
「一间小屋。门上爬着忍冬，老月亮先生爬上树梢。一间小屋，比奇，容我沉思，容我锁上门，请世界从门前走它的路。眼下城堡马上要住满来赴郡舞会的客人，我必须设法挖到这样一个洞天。否则，人类将永久损失一大块无价的诗。」

===Original===
“You desire,” said Beach, feeling his way cautiously, “a small cottage where you can write poetry, sir?”

===Chinese===
「您想要的，」比奇小心翼翼地摸着词儿，「是一间可以写诗的小屋，先生？」

===Original===
“You follow me like a leopard. Do you know of such a one?”

===Chinese===
「你跟得我像头豹子。知道有这么一间吗？」

===Original===
“There is an unoccupied gamekeeper’s cottage in the west wood, sir, but it is an extremely humble place.”

===Chinese===
「西林子里有间空着的猎场看守小屋，先生，只是那地方寒酸得很。」

===Original===
“Be it never so humble, it will do for me. Do you think Lady Constance would be offended if I were to ask for the loan of it for a few days?”

===Chinese===
「再寒酸，于我也足够。你若认为我去借住几天，康斯坦丝夫人会见怪吗？」

===Original===
“I fancy that her ladyship would receive the request with equanimity, sir. She is used to﻿ ﻿… She is not unaccustomed﻿ ﻿… Well, I can only say, sir, that there was a literary gentleman visiting the castle last summer who expressed a desire to take sun-baths in the garden each morning before breakfast. In the nood, sir. And, beyond instructing me to warn the maids, her ladyship placed no obstacle in the way of the fulfilment of his wishes. So﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我想夫人对这请求会处之泰然，先生。她习惯了……她并非不惯于……这么说吧先生，去年夏天有位来堡里做客的文人，表示想每天早饭前在花园里做日光浴。光着身子，先生。夫人除了吩咐我预先知会女仆们，对他愿望的实现未设任何障碍。所以……」

===Original===
“So a modest request like mine isn’t likely to cause a heart-attack? Admirable! You don’t know what it means to me to feel that I shall soon have a little refuge of my own, to which I can retreat and be in solitude.”

===Chinese===
「所以像我这样一点本分的请求，大概不至于引发心脏病？好极了！你不知道，想到不久就能有一方自己的小小避难所，可以退进去独处，对我意味着什么。」

===Original===
“I can imagine that it must be extremely gratifying, sir.”

===Chinese===
「我想那必定极为称心，先生。」

===Original===
“Then I will put the motion before the Board directly Lady Constance returns.”

===Chinese===
「那我就等康斯坦丝夫人一回来，立即向董事会提出动议。」

===Original===
“Very good, sir.”

===Chinese===
「很好，先生。」

===Original===
“I should like to splash it on the record once more, Beach, that I am much obliged to you for your sympathy and advice in this matter. I knew you would not fail me.”

===Chinese===
「我想再往会议记录上大书一笔，比奇：此事承你同情指教，我不胜感激。我早知道你不会让我失望。」

===Original===
“Not at all, sir. I am only too glad to have been able to be of assistance.”

===Chinese===
「别客气，先生。能稍效绵薄，我高兴还来不及。」

===Original===
“Oh, and, Beach﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「哦，还有，比奇……」

===Original===
“Sir?”

===Chinese===
「先生？」

===Original===
“Just one other thing. Will you be seeing Cootes, my valet, again shortly?”

===Chinese===
「就一件小事。你很快会再见到我的贴身男仆库茨吧？」

===Original===
“Quite shortly, sir, I should imagine.”

===Chinese===
「我想很快就会，先生。」

===Original===
“Then would you mind just prodding him smartly in the lower ribs﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「那么可否请你，往他下肋上干脆利落地捅上一记……」

===Original===
“Sir?” cried Beach, startled out of his butlerian calm. He swallowed a little convulsively. For eighteen months and more, ever since Lady Constance Keeble had first begun to cast her fly and hook over the murky water of the artistic world and jerk its denizens on to the pile carpets of Blandings Castle, Beach had had his fill of eccentricity. But until this moment he had hoped that Psmith was going to prove an agreeable change from the stream of literary lunatics which had been coming and going all that weary time. And lo! Psmith’s name led all the rest. Even the man who had come for a week in April and had wanted to eat jam with his fish paled in comparison.

===Chinese===
「先生？！」比奇喊出声来，被惊出了管家式的镇定。他痉挛似的咽了下口水。打康斯坦丝·基布尔夫人头一回把钓钩甩进艺术界的浑水、把水族们一条条拽上布兰丁斯城堡的长毛地毯，一年零八个月以来，比奇的怪癖早已吃撑了。可直到此刻，他一直指望皮史密斯能给那串来来去去、络绎不绝的文坛疯子来一个宜人的变化。谁承想！皮史密斯的大名力压群雄。就是四月里来住了一星期、要拿果酱就鱼吃的那位，相形之下也黯然失色。

===Original===
“Prod him in the ribs, sir?” he quavered.

===Chinese===
「往肋上捅，先生？」他颤声问。

===Original===
“Prod him in the ribs,” said Psmith firmly. “And at the same time whisper in his ear the word ‘Aha!’ ” Beach licked his dry lips.

===Chinese===
「往肋上捅，」皮史密斯语气坚定。「与此同时，附在他耳边低语一个字：『啊哈！』」比奇舔了舔发干的嘴唇。

===Original===
“Aha, sir?”

===Chinese===
「啊哈，先生？」

===Original===
“Aha! And say it came from me.”

===Chinese===
「啊哈！就说，是我说的。」

===Original===
“Very good, sir. The matter shall be attended to,” said Beach. And with a muffled sound that was half a sigh, half a death-rattle, he tottered through the green-baize door.

===Chinese===
「遵命，先生。此事定当办妥，」比奇说。随即，伴着半声叹息半声临终喉音的闷响，他踉跄着穿过绿呢门去了。

## X Sensational Occurrence at a Poetry Reading / 诗歌朗诵会上的惊变

### I / I

===Original===
Breakfast was over, and the guests of Blandings had scattered to their morning occupations. Some were writing letters, some were in the billiard-room: some had gone to the stables, some to the links: Lady Constance was interviewing the housekeeper, Lord Emsworth harrying head-gardener McAllister among the flowerbeds: and in the Yew Alley, the dappled sunlight falling upon her graceful head, Miss Peavey walked pensively up and down.

===Chinese===
早餐已毕，布兰丁斯的宾客们散往各自的晨课。有的写信，有的进了台球房：有的去了马厩，有的上了高尔夫球场：康斯坦丝夫人正约见管家妇，埃姆斯沃思勋爵在花圃间围剿首席园丁麦卡利斯特：而在紫杉巷里，斑驳的日影洒在她优美的头顶上，皮维小姐怅惘地踱来踱去。

===Original===
She was alone. It is a sad but indisputable fact that in this imperfect world Genius is too often condemned to walk alone﻿—if the earthier members of the community see it coming and have time to duck. Not one of the horde of visitors who had arrived overnight for the County Ball had shown any disposition whatever to court Miss Peavey’s society.

===Chinese===
她独自一人。世间一件可悲而不容置辩的事实是：在这个不完美的世界上，天才往往注定独行——只要尘俗的众生来得及看见她过来、来得及闪避。连夜涌来赴郡舞会的那一大群客人里，没有一个表现出丝毫想亲近皮维小姐的意思。

===Original===
One regrets this. Except for that slight bias towards dishonesty which led her to steal everything she could lay her hands on that was not nailed down, Aileen Peavey’s was an admirable character; and, oddly enough, it was the noble side of her nature to which these coarse-fibred critics objected. Of Miss Peavey, the purloiner of other people’s goods, they knew nothing; the woman they were dodging was Miss Peavey, the poetess. And it may be mentioned that, however much she might unbend in the presence of a congenial friend like Mr. Edward Cootes, she was a perfectly genuine poetess. Those six volumes under her name in the British Museum catalogue were her own genuine and unaided work: and, though she had been compelled to pay for the production of the first of the series, the other five had been brought out at her publisher’s own risk, and had even made a little money.

===Chinese===
这令人遗憾。除了那一点点偏于不老实、见到没钉牢的东西就忍不住顺手的毛病，艾琳·皮维的品格堪称可敬；而且说来奇怪，恰恰是她天性中高贵的那一面，遭到这些粗神经批评家的抵制。皮维小姐，那个摸走旁人财物的小偷，他们一无所知；他们躲着的这位，是皮维小姐，女诗人。这里不妨一提：无论她在爱德华·库茨先生这般投契的朋友面前多么放得开，她都是一位如假包换的女诗人。大英博物馆书目里挂着她名字的那六卷，全是她本人货真价实、无人代劳的手笔：虽说头一卷是自费印的，后五卷却都由出版商自担风险推出，甚至还小有赚头。

===Original===
Miss Peavey, however, was not sorry to be alone: for she had that on her mind which called for solitary thinking. The matter engaging her attention was the problem of what on earth had happened to Mr. Edward Cootes. Two days had passed since he had left her to go and force Psmith at the pistol’s point to introduce him into the castle: and since that moment he had vanished completely. Miss Peavey could not understand it.

===Chinese===
不过，皮维小姐倒不嫌独处：因为她心头正压着一桩需要独自盘算的事。占据她注意力的，是爱德华·库茨先生究竟出了什么事这个难题。撇下她去用枪逼皮史密斯把自己弄进城堡，已经过去两天了：自那一刻起，他无影无踪。皮维小姐百思不解。

===Original===
His nonappearance was all the more galling in that her superb brain had just completed in every detail a scheme for the seizure of Lady Constance Keeble’s diamond necklace; and to the success of this plot his aid was an indispensable adjunct. She was in the position of a general who comes from his tent with a plan of battle all mapped out, and finds that his army has strolled off somewhere and left him. Little wonder that, as she paced the Yew Alley, there was a frown on Miss Peavey’s fair forehead.

===Chinese===
他的不露面尤其令人窝火，因为她那颗高超的头脑刚刚完成了一项天衣无缝的方略，用于夺取康斯坦丝·基布尔夫人的钻石项链；而这一密谋的成功，他的协助是不可或缺的一环。她的处境，好比一位将军携着绘制停当的作战计划步出帐外，却发现他的大军溜达去了别处，把他一个人撂在那里。难怪皮维小姐在紫杉巷里踱步时，姣好的额头上凝着一团愠色。

===Original===
The Yew Alley, as Lord Emsworth had indicated in his extremely interesting lecture to Mr. Ralston McTodd at the Senior Conservative Club, contained among other noteworthy features certain yews which rose in solid blocks with rounded roof and stemless mushroom finials, the majority possessing arched recesses, forming arbors. As Miss Peavey was passing one of these, a voice suddenly addressed her.

===Chinese===
正如埃姆斯沃思勋爵在老保守党俱乐部对拉尔斯顿·麦克托德先生那场极其有趣的讲座中指出的，紫杉巷在诸多可观之处中，还有这么几株紫杉：长成敦实的一坨，圆顶无干，顶上蘑菇似的收个穹隆，多数树身上还开了拱形的凹龛，形成一个个树凉亭。皮维小姐正打其中一处经过，一个声音突然对她说话。

===Original===
“Hey!”

===Chinese===
「喂！」

===Original===
Miss Peavey started violently.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐猛吃一惊。

===Original===
“Anyone about?”

===Chinese===
「附近有人没？」

===Original===
A damp face with twigs sticking to it was protruding from a nearby yew. It rolled its eyes in an ineffectual effort to see round the corner.

===Chinese===
一张沾着碎枝叶的湿漉漉的脸，从近旁一株紫杉里探了出来。它转动眼珠，徒劳地想看到拐角后头去。

===Original===
Miss Peavey drew nearer, breathing heavily. The question as to the whereabouts of her wandering boy was solved; but the abruptness of his return had caused her to bite her tongue; and joy, as she confronted him, was blended with other emotions.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐凑近了些，呼吸粗重。她那流浪儿行踪的问题有了答案；可他这一回来得太突兀，害她咬了舌头；所以面对他时，欢喜里搀着别的情绪。

===Original===
“You dish-faced gazooni!” she exclaimed heatedly, her voice trembling with a sense of ill-usage, “where do you get that stuff, hiding in trees, and barking a girl’s head off?”

===Chinese===
「你这个饼脸蠢货！」她气冲冲地嚷道，嗓音因受了亏待而发颤，「你搞什么名堂，藏树里头，嚷嚷得把姑娘家魂儿都吓飞了？」

===Original===
“Sorry, Liz. I﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「对不住，莉兹。我……」

===Original===
“And where,” proceeded Miss Peavey, ventilating another grievance, “have you been all this darned time? Gosh-dingit, you leave me a coupla days back saying you’re going to stick up this bozo that calls himself McTodd with a gat and make him get you into the house, and that’s the last I see of you. What’s the big idea?”

===Chinese===
「还有，」皮维小姐又翻出一桩委屈，「这老半天你死哪儿去了？我的天，你前两天撂下我，说要去拿家伙顶住那个自称麦克托德的家伙，逼他把你弄进宅子，从此就再没照面。你什么意思？」

===Original===
“It’s all right, Liz. He did get me into the house. I’m his valet. That’s why I couldn’t get at you before. The way the help has to keep itself to itself in this joint, we might as well have been in different counties. If I hadn’t happened to see you snooping off by yourself this morning﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「没事了，莉兹。他真把我弄进宅子了。我当了他的贴身男仆。所以我一直没法来找你。这宅子里下人归下人、主人归主人，咱们跟隔着几个县似的。要不是我今儿早上碰巧看见你一个人溜出来……」

===Original===
Miss Peavey’s keen mind grasped the position of affairs.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐敏锐的头脑立时把握了全局。

===Original===
“All right, all right,” she interrupted, ever impatient of long speeches from others. “I understand. Well, this is good, Ed. It couldn’t have worked out better. I’ve got a scheme all doped out, and now you’re here we can get busy.”

===Chinese===
「行了行了，」她打断道，她素来不耐烦听旁人长篇大论。「我明白了。哎，这可太好了，埃德。再顺不过了。我方略全憋好了，这会儿你到了，咱们就能开工。」

===Original===
“A scheme?”

===Chinese===
「什么方略？」

===Original===
“A pippin,” assented Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「一绝，」皮维小姐应道。

===Original===
“It’ll need to be,” said Mr. Cootes, on whom the events of the last few days had caused pessimism to set its seal. “I tell you that McTodd gook is smooth. He somehow,” said Mr. Cootes prudently, for he feared harsh criticisms from his ladylove should he reveal the whole truth, “he somehow got wise to the notion that, as I was his valet, I could go and snoop round in his room, where he’d be wanting to hide the stuff if he ever got it, and now he’s gone and got them to let him have a kind of shack in the woods.”

===Chinese===
「最好是，」库茨先生说，近几日的事变已在他身上盖下了悲观的印。「我跟你说，麦克托德那家伙滑得很。他不知怎么，」库茨先生谨慎地说，因为他怕和盘托出会招致爱人的严词批判，「他不知怎么起了疑，觉得我既当他的贴身男仆，就会去他屋里翻腾——他真弄到手的话，东西准藏那儿——这不，他算计上了，让他们给了他林子里一间什么棚子。」

===Original===
“H’m!” said Miss Peavey. “Well,” she resumed after a thoughtful pause, “I’m not worrying about him. Let him go and roost in the woods all he wants to. I’ve got a scheme all ready, and it’s gilt-edged. And, unless you ball up your end of it, Ed, it can’t fail to drag home the gravy.”

===Chinese===
「哼！」皮维小姐说。「好啊，」她若有所思地顿了顿，接着说，「我不替他操心。随他爱去林子里蹲多久蹲多久。我的方略现成，金边证券。只要你不把你那一头搞砸，埃德，这趟油水稳吃。」

===Original===
“Am I in it?”

===Chinese===
「有我一份？」

===Original===
“You bet you’re in it. I can’t work it without you. That’s what’s been making me so darned mad when you didn’t show up all this time.”

===Chinese===
「你笃定有份。没你我办不成。你这些天不露面，把我气得呀。」

===Original===
“Spill it, Liz,” said Mr. Cootes humbly. As always in the presence of this dynamic woman, he was suffering from an inferiority complex. From the very start of their combined activities she had been the brains of the firm, he merely the instrument to carry into effect the plans she dictated.

===Chinese===
「倒出来吧，莉兹，」库茨先生谦卑地说。一在这位精力充沛的女人面前，他就害着自卑情结。打合伙那天起，商号的头脑一向是她，他不过是把她口授的计划付诸实施的一件家伙。

===Original===
Miss Peavey glanced swiftly up and down the Yew Alley. It was still the same peaceful, lonely spot. She turned to Mr. Cootes again, and spoke with brisk decision.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐飞快地朝紫杉巷两头各扫了一眼。这里依旧是那处安宁僻静的所在。她转回身，对着库茨先生，干脆利落地说起来。

===Original===
“Now, listen, Ed, and get this straight, because maybe I shan’t have another chance of talking to you.”

===Chinese===
「现在听着，埃德，给我记牢了，没准儿往后没工夫再跟你细说。」

===Original===
“I’m listening,” said Mr. Cootes obsequiously.

===Chinese===
「听着呢，」库茨先生谄媚地说。

===Original===
“Well, to begin with, now that the house is full, Her Nibs is wearing that necklace every night. And you can take it from me, Ed, that you want to put on your smoked glasses before you look at it. It’s a lalapaloosa.”

===Chinese===
「首先，如今宅子住满了，那位夫人大人天天晚上把项链挂在脖子上。你信我的，埃德，看它之前你先戴上墨镜。绝了，那玩意儿。」

===Original===
“As good as that?”

===Chinese===
「有那么好？」

===Original===
“Ask me! You don’t know the half of it.”

===Chinese===
「那还用问！你连一半都不知道。」

===Original===
“Where does she keep it, Liz? Have you found that out?” asked Mr. Cootes, a gleam of optimism playing across his sad face for an instant.

===Chinese===
「她平时搁哪儿，莉兹？这个你摸清了没？」库茨先生问，一线乐观的微光在他愁苦的脸上闪了一瞬。

===Original===
“No, I haven’t. And I don’t want to. I’ve not got time to waste monkeying about with safes and maybe having the whole bunch pile on the back of my neck. I believe in getting things easy. Well, tonight this bimbo that calls himself McTodd is going to give a reading of his poems in the big drawing-room. You know where that is?”

===Chinese===
「没。我也不想摸。我可没工夫耗在保险柜上瞎抠索，回头再招一群人扑我后脖颈。我信奉把事情办省力。是这样：今天晚上，那个自称麦克托德的家伙，要在大客厅朗诵他的诗。那屋子在哪儿你知道不？」

===Original===
“I can find out.”

===Chinese===
「我能打听。」

===Original===
“And you better had find out,” said Miss Peavey vehemently. “And before tonight at that. Well, there you are. Do you begin to get wise?”

===Chinese===
「你最好打听出来，」皮维小姐激烈地说，「还得赶在今天晚上以前。好，你琢磨琢磨。开窍了没？」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes, his head protruding unhappily from the yew tree, would have given much to have been able to make the demanded claim to wisdom, for he knew of old the store his alert partner set upon quickness of intellect. He was compelled, however, to disturb the branches by shaking his head.

===Chinese===
库茨先生把脑袋凄凄惨惨地从紫杉里探着，本很想说句开了窍，好领受他那机警伙伴一向看重的机敏之名；无奈只得摇了摇头，摇得树枝乱颤。

===Original===
“You always were pretty dumb,” said Miss Peavey with scorn. “I’ll say that you’ve got good solid qualities, Ed﻿—from the neck up. Why, I’m going to sit behind Lady Constance while that goof is shooting his fool head off, and I’m going to reach out and grab that necklace off of her. See?”

===Chinese===
「你向来就是笨，」皮维小姐不屑地说。「我承认你有几样实打实的优点，埃德——全在脖子往上。这么回事：那个傻子在那儿摇头晃脑念他的糊涂诗的时候，我要坐在康斯坦丝夫人背后，一伸手，把项链从她脖子上薅下来。明白没？」

===Original===
“But, Liz”﻿—Mr. Cootes diffidently summoned up courage to point out what appeared to him to be a flaw in the scheme﻿—“if you start any strong-arm work in front of everybody like the way you say, won’t they﻿ ﻿… ?”

===Chinese===
「可是，莉兹，」库茨先生鼓起怯懦的勇气，指出方案中一个在他看来是漏洞的地方，「你要当着满屋子人动粗，人家不会……？」

===Original===
“No, they won’t. And I’ll tell you why they won’t. They aren’t going to see me do it, because when I do it it’s going to be good and dark in that room. And it’s going to be dark because you’ll be somewheres out at the back of the house, wherever they keep the main electric-light works, turning the switch as hard as you can go. See? That’s your end of it, and pretty soft for you at that. All you have to do is to find out where the thing is and what you have to do to it to put out all the lights in the joint. I guess I can trust you not to bungle that?”

===Chinese===
「不会。我告诉你为什么不会。他们瞧不见我下手，因为我下手那会儿，屋里准黑得严严实实。而它之所以黑，是因为你那会儿在宅子后头的什么鬼地方——他们搁总电闸的地方——把闸刀往死里拉。明白？这就是你那一头，对你够轻省的了。你只消打听出那玩意儿在哪儿、怎么弄能叫全宅的灯一齐瞎。这个我不指望你办砸吧？」

===Original===
“Liz,” said Mr. Cootes, and there was reverence in his voice, “you can do just that little thing. But what﻿ ﻿… ?”

===Chinese===
「莉兹，」库茨先生说，嗓音里透着敬畏，「这点小事我办得妥妥的。可是万一……？」

===Original===
“All right, I know what you’re going to say. What happens after that, and how do I get away with the stuff? Well, the window’ll be open, and I’ll just get to it and fling the necklace out. See? There’ll be a big fuss going on in the room on account of the darkness and all that, and while everybody’s cutting up and what-the-helling, you’ll pick up your dogs and run round as quick as you can make it and pouch the thing. I guess it won’t be hard for you to locate it. The window’s just over the terrace, all smooth turf, and it isn’t real dark nights now, and you ought to have plenty of time to hunt around before they can get the lights going again.﻿ ﻿… Well, what do you think of it?” There was a brief silence.

===Chinese===
「行了，我知道你要说什么。那之后呢？东西怎么脱手？这样：窗户开着，我凑过去，把项链往外一扔。明白？屋里那会儿正为着黑灯瞎火闹成一团，趁众人乱蹦乱骂的当口，你抬起狗腿，撒开丫子绕到前头，把货捡走。我谅你找得着。窗下就是露台，一色平草坪，眼下夜里又不算真黑，在他们把灯鼓捣亮之前，你工夫足着呢。……怎么样，你给评评？」

===Original===
“Liz,” said Mr. Cootes at length.

===Chinese===
一阵短暂的沉默。

===Original===
“Is it or is it not,” demanded Miss Peavey, “a ball of fire?”

===Chinese===
「莉兹，」库茨先生终于开口。

===Original===
“Liz,” said Mr. Cootes, and his voice was husky with such awe as some young officer of Napoleon’s staff might have felt on hearing the details of the latest plan of campaign, “Liz, I’ve said it before, and I’ll say it again. When it comes to the smooth stuff, old girl, you’re the oyster’s eyetooth!”

===Chinese===
「它是不是，」皮维小姐质问，「一团火？」

===Original===
And, reaching out an arm from the recesses of the yew, he took Miss Peavey’s hand in his and gave it a tender squeeze. A dreamy look came into the poetess’s fine eyes, and she giggled a little. Dumbbell though he was, she loved this man.

===Chinese===
「莉兹，」库茨先生说，嗓音因敬畏而沙哑，堪比拿破仑帐下某位青年军官聆听最新作战方案时的滋味，「莉兹，这话我说过，还要再说。论玩得漂亮，老姑娘，你是牡蛎的獠牙！」说着，他从紫杉深处伸出一条胳膊，把皮维小姐的手握进手里，温柔地捏了捏。女诗人那双秀眼里漾起梦意，她轻轻咯咯了两声。笨蛋归笨蛋，她爱这个男人。

### II / II

===Original===
“Mr. Baxter!”

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特先生！」

===Original===
“Yes, Miss Halliday?”

===Chinese===
「什么事，哈利迪小姐？」

===Original===
The Brains of Blandings looked abstractedly up from his desk. It was only some half-hour since luncheon had finished, but already he was in the library surrounded by large books like a sea-beast among rocks. Most of his time was spent in the library when the castle was full of guests, for his lofty mind was ill-attuned to the frivolous babblings of Society butterflies.

===Chinese===
布兰丁斯的大脑从案头心不在焉地抬起眼来。午饭结束才不过半个钟头，他已经泡在藏书室里，让大书四围，像海兽盘踞礁石。每逢城堡宾客盈门，他的光阴多在藏书室度过，因为他那高迈的心智，实在不惯于应酬场上蝴蝶们的浅薄絮叨。

===Original===
“I wonder if you could spare me this afternoon?” said Eve.

===Chinese===
「今天下午您能放我半天假吗？」伊芙说。

===Original===
Baxter directed the glare of his spectacles upon her inquisitorially.

===Chinese===
巴克斯特把镜片的寒光探询地罩到她身上。

===Original===
“The whole afternoon?”

===Chinese===
「一下午？」

===Original===
“If you don’t mind. You see, I had a letter by the second post from a great friend of mine, saying that she will be in Market Blandings this afternoon and asking me to meet her there. I must see her, Mr. Baxter, *please*. You’ve no notion how important it is.”

===Chinese===
「您若不介意。是这样，第二班邮递送来我一位好友的信，说她今天下午到布兰丁斯集镇，约我去见她。我必须见她，巴克斯特先生，*求您了*。您不知道这有多要紧。」

===Original===
Eve’s manner was excited, and her eyes as they met Baxter’s sparkled in a fashion that might have disturbed a man made of less stern stuff. If it had been the Hon. Freddie Threepwood, for instance, who had been gazing into their blue depths, that impulsive youth would have tied himself into knots and yapped like a dog. Baxter, the superman, felt no urge towards any such display. He reviewed her request calmly and judicially, and decided that it was a reasonable one.

===Chinese===
伊芙神情激动，迎着巴克斯特目光的那双眼睛闪闪发亮，换一个材料稍次的男人兴许就方寸大乱了。倘若方才凝望这泓蓝的是弗雷迪·思里普伍德少爷，那位冲动青年早把自己打成死结、学狗叫了。超人巴克斯特则毫无此类表演的冲动。他冷静而公正地审查了这项请求，裁定其合理。

===Original===
“Very well, Miss Halliday.”

===Chinese===
「可以，哈利迪小姐。」

===Original===
“Thank you ever so much. I’ll make up for it by working twice as hard tomorrow.”

===Chinese===
「太感谢了。明天我干双倍的活补上。」

===Original===
Eve flitted to the door, pausing there to bestow a grateful smile upon him before going out; and Baxter returned to his reading. For a moment he was conscious of a feeling of regret that this quite attractive and uniformly respectful girl should be the partner in crime of a man of whom he disapproved even more than he disapproved of most malefactors. Then he crushed down the weak emotion and was himself again.

===Chinese===
伊芙翩然出门，到门口又驻足回眸一笑，这才走了；巴克斯特重新开卷。有一瞬间，他心头掠过一丝惋惜：这样一位讨人喜欢、又始终恭谨的姑娘，竟是一个罪犯的同伙——那罪犯，连他厌恶的程度都超过他厌恶大多数歹徒。随即他碾碎这软弱的情感，恢复了本相。

===Original===
Eve trotted downstairs, humming happily to herself. She had expected a longer and more strenuous struggle before she obtained her order of release, and told herself that, despite a manner which seldom deviated from the forbidding, Baxter was really quite nice. In short, it seemed to her that nothing could possibly occur to mar the joyfulness of this admirable afternoon; and it was only when a voice hailed her as she was going through the hall a few minutes later that she realised that she was mistaken. The voice, which trembled throatily, was that of the Hon. Freddie; and her first look at him told Eve, an expert diagnostician, that he was going to propose to her again.

===Chinese===
伊芙一路小跑下楼，快活地哼着曲子。她原以为要费一场更久更艰苦的拉锯才能领到这张放行令，于是暗自认定，巴克斯特别看那副拒人千里的做派，其实人挺不错。总而言之，在她看来，再没有什么能败坏这个美妙下午的兴致了；直到几分钟后她穿过大厅时被一声呼喝叫住，才知道自己想错了。那嗓音喉音浓重、微微发颤，是弗雷迪少爷的；而第一眼望去，伊芙这位老练的诊断师便断定：他又要向她求婚了。

===Original===
“Well, Freddie?” said Eve resignedly.

===Chinese===
「怎么啦，弗雷迪？」伊芙听天由命地说。

===Original===
The Hon. Frederick Threepwood was a young man who was used to hearing people say “Well, Freddie?” resignedly when he appeared. His father said it; his Aunt Constance said it; all his other aunts and uncles said it. Widely differing personalities in every other respect, they all said “Well, Freddie?” resignedly directly they caught sight of him. Eve’s words, therefore, and the tone in which they were spoken, did not damp him as they might have damped another. His only feeling was one of solemn gladness at the thought that at last he had managed to get her alone for half a minute.

===Chinese===
弗雷德里克·思里普伍德少爷早听惯了人们一见他就听天由命地说「怎么啦，弗雷迪？」他父亲说，康斯坦丝姑妈说，其余各位叔伯姑姨都说。这些人在其他一切方面性情迥异，唯独一望见他便听天由命地说「怎么啦，弗雷迪？」这一点上高度一致。因此，伊芙的措辞连同说话的腔调，都没有像浇别人那样浇他一头冷水。他唯一的感觉是一种庄严的欣喜：总算设法把她单独抓住了半分钟。

===Original===
The fact that this was the first time he had been able to get her alone since her arrival at the castle had caused Freddie a good deal of sorrow. Bad luck was what he attributed it to, thereby giving the object of his affections less credit than was her due for a masterly policy of evasion. He sidled up, looking like a well-dressed sheep.

===Chinese===
自打她进堡以来一直无缘单独见她，这事给弗雷迪添了许多伤悲。他把这归咎于运气坏，从而低估了他爱慕对象的闪避功夫——那其实是一套高明的躲人方略。他蹭上前来，活像一只穿得体面的羊。

===Original===
“Going anywhere?” he inquired.

===Chinese===
「上哪儿去？」他问。

===Original===
“Yes. I’m going to Market Blandings. Isn’t it a lovely afternoon? I suppose you are busy all the time now that the house is full? Goodbye,” said Eve.

===Chinese===
「上布兰丁斯集镇去。多好的下午啊。宅子住满了人，你如今怕是一刻不得闲吧？回见。」伊芙说。

===Original===
“Eh?” said Freddie, blinking.

===Chinese===
「嗯？」弗雷迪眨着眼。

===Original===
“Goodbye. I must be hurrying.”

===Chinese===
「回见。我得赶紧了。」

===Original===
“Where did you say you were going?”

===Chinese===
「你刚说上哪儿去？」

===Original===
“Market Blandings.”

===Chinese===
「布兰丁斯集镇。」

===Original===
“I’ll come with you.”

===Chinese===
「我陪你去。」

===Original===
“No, I want to be alone. I’ve got to meet someone there.”

===Chinese===
「不，我想一个人。我要去见个人。」

===Original===
“Come with you as far as the gates,” said Freddie, the human limpet.

===Chinese===
「送你到大门口，」人形藤壶弗雷迪说。

===Original===
The afternoon sun seemed to Eve to be shining a little less brightly as they started down the drive. She was a kindhearted girl, and it irked her to have to be continually acting as a black frost in Freddie’s garden of dreams. There appeared, however, to be but two ways out of the thing: either she must accept him or he must stop proposing. The first of these alternatives she resolutely declined to consider, and, as far as was ascertainable from his actions, Freddie declined just as resolutely to consider the second. The result was that solitary interviews between them were seldom wholly free from embarrassing developments.

===Chinese===
两人沿车道出发时，午后的太阳在伊芙看来都黯了三分。她是个好心肠的姑娘，老在弗雷迪的梦想花园里扮演一场黑霜，令她于心不安。然而此事似只有两条出路：要么她收下他，要么他别再求。头一条她断然拒绝考虑，而第二条，就其行为观测，弗雷迪拒绝得同样坚决。结果是，两人的单独会面鲜有完全不闹尴尬的。

===Original===
They walked for a while in silence. Then:

===Chinese===
两人默然走了一程。然后：

===Original===
“You’re dashed hard on a fellow,” said Freddie.

===Chinese===
「你这家伙也真狠，」弗雷迪说。

===Original===
“How’s your putting coming on?” asked Eve.

===Chinese===
「你推杆练得怎么样了？」伊芙问。

===Original===
“Eh?”

===Chinese===
「嗯？」

===Original===
“Your putting. You told me you had so much trouble with it.”

===Chinese===
「推杆呀。你跟我说过你推杆老出毛病。」

===Original===
She was not looking at him, for she had developed a habit of not looking at him on these occasions; but she assumed that the odd sound which greeted her remark was a hollow, mirthless laugh.

===Chinese===
她没看他，因为每逢这种场合她已养成了不看他的习惯；但她估计，迎接她这句话的那声怪响是一记空洞无笑的干笑。

===Original===
“My putting!”

===Chinese===
「推杆！」

===Original===
“Well, you told me yourself it’s the most important part of golf.”

===Chinese===
「怎么，你自己说的，推杆是高尔夫最要紧的部分。」

===Original===
“Golf! Do you think I have time to worry about golf these days?”

===Chinese===
「高尔夫！你以为我这些天还有工夫操心高尔夫？」

===Original===
“Oh, how splendid, Freddie! Are you really doing some work of some kind? It’s quite time, you know. Think how pleased your father will be.”

===Chinese===
「哦，太好了，弗雷迪！你当真干起点什么正事了？也该到时候了，你知道。想想你父亲多高兴。」

===Original===
“I say,” said Freddie, “I do think you might marry a chap.”

===Chinese===
「我说，」弗雷迪说，「我还真觉得你可以嫁个哥们儿。」

===Original===
“I suppose I shall some day,” said Eve, “if I meet the right one.”

===Chinese===
「我想早晚会的，」伊芙说，「要是遇对了人的话。」

===Original===
“No, no!” said Freddie despairingly. She was not usually so dense as this. He had always looked on her as a dashed clever girl. “I mean *me*.”

===Chinese===
「不不！」弗雷迪绝望地说。她平时没有这么迟钝呀。他一向当地是个聪明透顶的姑娘。「我是说*我*。」

===Original===
Eve sighed. She had hoped to avert the inevitable.

===Chinese===
伊芙叹了口气。她本指望躲过这一劫。

===Original===
“Oh, Freddie!” she exclaimed, exasperated. She was still sorry for him, but she could not help being irritated. It was such a splendid afternoon and she had been feeling so happy. And now he had spoiled everything. It always took her at least half an hour to get over the nervous strain of refusing his proposals.

===Chinese===
「哦，弗雷迪！」她动气地喊道。她依旧可怜他，却忍不住要恼。多好的下午，她本多么快活。这下全让他搅了。每回拒绝他的求婚，她至少要半个钟头才能从神经紧张里缓过来。

===Original===
“I love you, dash it!” said Freddie.

===Chinese===
「我爱你，见鬼！」弗雷迪说。

===Original===
“Well, do stop loving me,” said Eve. “I’m an awful girl, really. I’d make you miserable.”

===Chinese===
「那你就别爱了呗，」伊芙说。「我是个糟糕透顶的姑娘，真的。我会叫你苦不堪言。」

===Original===
“Happiest man in the world,” corrected Freddie devoutly.

===Chinese===
「世上最幸福的男人，」弗雷迪虔诚地更正。

===Original===
“I’ve got a frightful temper.”

===Chinese===
「我脾气吓死人。」

===Original===
“You’re an angel.”

===Chinese===
「你是天使。」

===Original===
Eve’s exasperation increased. She always had a curious fear that one of these days, if he went on proposing, she might say “Yes” by mistake. She wished that there was some way known to science of stopping him once and for all. And in her desperation she thought of a line of argument which she had not yet employed.

===Chinese===
