“Yes. In the wood past the Yew Alley.”

===Chinese===
「就是那间。紫杉巷过去林子里的。」

===Original===
“Little cottage? I never heard of any little cottage.”

===Chinese===
「小屋？从没听说过什么小屋。」

===Original===
“Well, he’s got one,” said Eve. “And as soon as everybody has gone to the ball you and I are going to burgle it.”

===Chinese===
「反正他有这么一间，」伊芙说。「等大伙儿全去了舞会，你我就去撬了它。」

===Original===
“What!”

===Chinese===
「什么！」

===Original===
“Burgle it!”

===Chinese===
「撬了它！」

===Original===
“Burgle it?”

===Chinese===
「撬了它？」

===Original===
“Yes, *burgle* it!”

===Chinese===
「对，*撬了*它！」

===Original===
Freddie gulped.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪咽了口唾沫。

===Original===
“Look here, old thing,” he said plaintively. “This is a bit beyond me. It doesn’t seem to me to make sense.”

===Chinese===
「我说，老伙计，」他委屈地说。「这有点超纲了。我听着不对味儿。」

===Original===
Eve forced herself to be patient. After all, she reflected, perhaps she had been approaching the matter a little rapidly. The desire to beat Freddie violently over the head passed, and she began to speak slowly, and, as far as she could manage it, in words of one syllable.

===Chinese===
伊芙强压着性子。她想，毕竟，兴许自己讲得太急了点。想抄家伙照弗雷迪头上狠敲的冲动过去了，她放慢语速，尽可能用单音节的词讲起来。

===Original===
“I can make it quite clear if you will listen and not say a word till I’ve done. This man who calls himself McTodd is not Mr. McTodd at all. He is a thief who got into the place by saying that he was McTodd. He stole the jewels from me last night and hid them in his cottage.”

===Chinese===
「你只消听着、别插嘴，我就能讲清楚。这个自称麦克托德的家伙，根本不是麦克托德先生。他是个贼，冒充麦克托德混进来的。他昨晚从我手里把珠宝偷走，藏在了他的小屋里。」

===Original===
“But, I say!”

===Chinese===
「可是，我说！」

===Original===
“Don’t interrupt. I know he has them there, so when he has gone to the ball and the coast is clear you and I will go and search till we find them.”

===Chinese===
「别打岔。我知道东西就在那儿。所以等他去了舞会、人去屋空，你我就去搜，搜到为止。」

===Original===
“But, I say!”

===Chinese===
「可是，我说！」

===Original===
Eve crushed down her impatience once more.

===Chinese===
伊芙又压下一回不耐。

===Original===
“Well?”

===Chinese===
「又怎么了？」

===Original===
“Do you really think this cove has got the necklace?”

===Chinese===
「你真觉得这小子得了项链？」

===Original===
“I know he has.”

===Chinese===
「我知道他得了。」

===Original===
“Well, then, it’s jolly well the best thing that could possibly have happened, because I got him here to pinch it for Uncle Joseph.”

===Chinese===
「那敢情好啊，天底下再顺没有的事了，因为正是我把他弄来替约瑟夫姨父偷的呀。」

===Original===
“What!”

===Chinese===
「什么！」

===Original===
“Absolutely. You see, I began to have a doubt or two as to whether I was quite equal to the contract, so I roped in this bird by way of a gang.”

===Chinese===
「妥妥的。是这样，我起先有点犯嘀咕，怕这活儿我一个人嚼不动，就拉这位老兄入伙，凑个帮伙。」

===Original===
“You got him here? You mean you sent for him and arranged that he should pass himself off as Mr. McTodd?”

===Chinese===
「你把他弄来的？你是说，你叫人来安排他冒充麦克托德先生？」

===Original===
“Well, no, not exactly that. He was coming here as McTodd anyway, as far as I can gather. But I’d talked it over with him, you know, before that and asked him to pinch the necklace.”

===Chinese===
「这个，倒也不尽然。就我所知，他反正本来就要顶麦克托德的名来。可在这之前，我早跟他碰过头，你晓得，求他去偷项链。」

===Original===
“Then you know him quite well? He is a friend of yours?”

===Chinese===
「那你跟他很熟？他是你朋友？」

===Original===
“I wouldn’t say that exactly. But he said he was a great pal of Phyllis and her husband.”

===Chinese===
「那倒说不上。可他说他是菲利斯和她丈夫的铁哥们儿。」

===Original===
“Did he tell you that?”

===Chinese===
「他这么说的？」

===Original===
“Absolutely!”

===Chinese===
「妥妥的！」

===Original===
“When?”

===Chinese===
「什么时候？」

===Original===
“In the train.”

===Chinese===
「在火车上。」

===Original===
“I mean, was it before or after you had told him why you wanted the necklace stolen?”

===Chinese===
「我是说，在你告诉他你要偷项链的缘由之前，还是之后？」

===Original===
“Eh? Let me think. After.”

===Chinese===
「嗯？让我想想。之后。」

===Original===
“You’re sure?”

===Chinese===
「你敢肯定？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「敢。」

===Original===
“Tell me exactly what happened,” said Eve. “I can’t understand it at all at present.”

===Chinese===
「把经过原原本本讲给我听，」伊芙说。「我现在全然理不清。」

===Original===
Freddie marshalled his thoughts.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪把思绪列队。

===Original===
“Well, let’s see. Well, to start with, I told Uncle Joe I would pinch the necklace and slip it to him, and he said if I did he’d give me a thousand quid. As a matter of fact, he made it two thousand, and very decent of him, I thought it. Is that straight?”

===Chinese===
「这个，咱们这么着。首先，我跟乔姨父说我去偷项链、转手给他，他说我办成了就给我一千镑。实际上他给的是两千，我觉得他够意思。这笔账对不？」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「对。」

===Original===
“Then I sort of got cold feet. Began to wonder, don’t you know, if I hadn’t bitten off rather more than I could chew.”

===Chinese===
「后来我有点腿软。就开始琢磨，你懂的，是不是一口咬下了嚼不动的。」

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
「嗯。」

===Original===
“And then I saw this advertisement in the paper.”

===Chinese===
「再后来我在报上看见了那则广告。」

===Original===
“Advertisement? What advertisement?”

===Chinese===
「广告？什么广告？」

===Original===
“There was an advertisement in the paper saying if anybody wanted anything done simply apply to this chap. So I wrote him a letter and went up and had a talk with him in the lobby of the Piccadilly Palace. Only, unfortunately, I’d promised the guv’nor I’d catch the twelve-fifty home, so I had to dash off in the middle. Must have thought me rather an ass, it’s sometimes occurred to me since. I mean, practically all I said was, ‘Will you pinch my aunt’s necklace?’ and then buzzed off to catch the train. Never thought I’d see the man again, but when I got into the five o’clock train﻿—I missed the twelve-fifty﻿—there he was, as large as life, and the guv’nor suddenly trickled in from another compartment and introduced him to me as McTodd the poet. Then the guv’nor legged it, and this chap told me he wasn’t really McTodd, only pretending to be McTodd.”

===Chinese===
「报上有则广告，说谁有事要办，只管去找这位老兄。我就写了封信，又上皮卡迪利宫的门厅跟他谈了一回。只是不巧，我答应过老爸坐十二点五十的车回家，只得中途撤了。事后我有时想，他八成拿我当个大傻子。我是说，我拢共就说了句：『你肯不肯偷我姑妈的项链？』然后就赶车去了。本以为再也见不着这位了；可我一上五点的车——十二点五十我误了——他赫然就在那儿，活的；紧接着老爸从隔壁包厢晃进来，把他介绍给我，说是诗人麦克托德。后来老爸撤了，这位老兄就告诉我，他其实不是麦克托德，只是装作麦克托德。」

===Original===
“Didn’t that strike you as strange?”

===Chinese===
「你不觉得这事透着怪？」

===Original===
“Yes, rather rummy.”

===Chinese===
「觉得，够邪门。」

===Original===
“Did you ask him why he was doing such an extraordinary thing?”

===Chinese===
「你问过他干吗要干这种出奇的事吗？」

===Original===
“Oh, yes. But he wouldn’t tell me. And then he asked me why I wanted him to pinch Aunt Connie’s necklace, and it suddenly occurred to me that everything was working rather smoothly﻿—I mean, him being on his way to the castle like that. Right on the spot, don’t you know. So I told him all about Phyllis, and it was then that he said that he had been a pal of hers and her husband’s for years. So we fixed it up that he was to get the necklace and hand it over. I must say I was rather drawn to the chappie. He said he didn’t want any money for swiping the thing.”

===Chinese===
「哦，问了。可他不肯说。反过来他问我，干吗要他偷康妮姑妈的项链；我猛然觉得一切都顺得很——我是说，他正好就这么奔城堡来了。现成就在跟前，你懂的。于是我把菲利斯的事全告诉了他，就是那会儿，他说他跟她和她的丈夫是多年的哥们儿。于是我们说定：他取项链，交货。必须说，我对这老兄挺有好感。他说偷这玩意儿他分文不取。」

===Original===
Eve laughed bitterly.

===Chinese===
伊芙苦笑。

===Original===
“Why should he, when he was going to get twenty thousand pounds’ worth of diamonds and keep them? Oh, Freddie, I should have thought that even you would have seen through him. You go to this perfect stranger and tell him that there is a valuable necklace waiting here to be stolen, you find him on his way to steal it, and you trust him implicitly just because he tells you he knows Phyllis﻿—whom he had never heard of in his life till you mentioned her. Freddie, really!”

===Chinese===
「他取什么钱？两万镑的钻石眼看就要到手归他，还要钱干什么？哦，弗雷迪，我原以为连你也该看穿他。你跑去找这么个彻头彻尾的生人，告诉他这里有条值钱的项链等着偷；你发现他正在来偷的路上；然后仅仅因为他说他认识菲利斯——一个你不说他这辈子都没听说过的人——你就对他推心置腹。弗雷迪，真是的！」

===Original===
The Hon. Freddie scratched his beautifully shaven chin.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪挠了挠刮得干干净净的下巴。

===Original===
“Well, when you put it like that,” he said, “I must own it does sound a bit off. But he seemed such a dashed matey sort of bird. Cheery and all that. I liked the feller.”

===Chinese===
「这个，你这么一说，」他说，「我得承认，听着是有点离谱。可他瞧着那么够哥们儿。乐呵呵的什么的。我喜欢那家伙。」

===Original===
“What nonsense!”

===Chinese===
「胡说八道！」

===Original===
“Well, but you liked him, too. I mean to say, you were about with him a goodish lot.”

===Chinese===
「可是你也喜欢他呀。我是说，你跟他厮混了不老少。」

===Original===
“I hate him!” said Eve angrily. “I wish I had never seen him. And if I let him get away with that necklace and cheat poor little Phyllis out of her money, I’ll﻿—I’ll﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我恨他！」伊芙怒道。「但愿从没见过他。要是我由他带着项链跑了、坑了可怜的菲利斯的钱，我就——我就……」

===Original===
She raised a grimly determined chin to the stars. Freddie watched her admiringly.

===Chinese===
她朝星空扬起一副破釜沉舟的下巴。弗雷迪钦慕地望着她。

===Original===
“I say, you know, you are a wonderful girl,” he said.

===Chinese===
「我说，你可真是个了不起的姑娘，」他说。

===Original===
“He *shan’t* get away with it, if I have to pull the place down.”

===Chinese===
「他*休想*得手，哪怕我把这宅子拆了。」

===Original===
“When you chuck your head up like that you remind me a bit of What’s-her-name, the Famous Players star﻿—you know, girl who was in *Wed to a Satyr*. Only,” added Freddie hurriedly, “she isn’t half so pretty. I say, I was rather looking forward to that County Ball, but now this has happened I don’t mind missing it a bit. I mean, it seems to draw us closer together somehow, if you follow me. I say, honestly, all kidding aside, you think that love might some day awaken in﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「你这么一扬下巴，有点像那个谁来着——名演员公司的台柱——你知道吧，演《嫁给羊角怪》的那个姑娘。只不过，」弗雷迪赶紧补上，「她有你一半好看就不错了。我说，本来我还挺盼着郡舞会的，可出了这事，误了也就误了，我不大在乎了。我是说，这事好像让咱俩更近乎了，你要是能跟上我。我说，说真的，玩笑归玩笑，你觉得爱情有朝一日会不会在……」

===Original===
“We shall want a lamp, of course,” said Eve.

===Chinese===
「咱们当然得有盏灯，」伊芙说。

===Original===
“Eh?”

===Chinese===
「嗯？」

===Original===
“A lamp﻿—to see with when we are in the cottage. Can you get one?”

===Chinese===
「灯——在小屋里照着用的。你能弄到吗？」

===Original===
Freddie reluctantly perceived that the moment for sentiment had not arrived.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪悻悻地看出，抒情的时刻还没到。

===Original===
“A lamp? Oh, yes, of course. Rather.”

===Chinese===
「灯？哦，能，当然。小意思。」

===Original===
“Better get two,” said Eve. “And meet me here about half an hour after everybody has gone to the ball.”

===Chinese===
「最好弄两盏，」伊芙说。「大家去舞会后半个钟头，这儿见。」

### II / II

===Original===
The tiny sitting-room of Psmith’s haven of rest in the woods had never reached a high standard of decorativeness even in its best days; but as Eve paused from her labours and looked at it in the light of her lamp about an hour after her conversation with Freddie on the terrace, it presented a picture of desolation which would have startled the plain-living gamekeeper to whom it had once been a home. Even Freddie, though normally an unobservant youth, seemed awed by the ruin he had helped to create.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯林中休憩所的那间小起居间，全盛时期也从来没讲究到哪儿去；可当伊芙——与弗雷迪在露台上谈过约一个钟头后——停下手里的活，借灯光打量它时，它呈现的一片荒凉，足以把当年以它为家的那位清苦看守吓一大跳。连素来不长眼的弗雷迪，似乎也被自己参与制造的这堆废墟镇住了。

===Original===
“Golly!” he observed. “I say, we’ve rather mucked the place up a bit!”

===Chinese===
「乖乖！」他说。「我说，咱们把这地方祸害得不轻！」

===Original===
It was no overstatement. Eve had come to the cottage to search, and she had searched thoroughly. The torn carpet lay in a untidy heap against the wall. The table was overturned. Boards had been wrenched from the floor, bricks from the chimney-place. The horsehair sofa was in ribbons, and the one small cushion in the room lay limply in a corner, its stuffing distributed north, south, east and west. There was soot everywhere﻿—on the walls, on the floor, on the fireplace, and on Freddie. A brace of dead bats, the further result of the latter’s groping in a chimney which had not been swept for seven months, reposed in the fender. The sitting-room had never been luxurious; it was now not even cosy.

===Chinese===
这话毫不夸张。伊芙是来搜的，而且搜了个底朝天。撕开的地毯乱堆在墙根。桌子翻倒在地。地板撬起了几块，壁炉里的砖也掀了几块。马鬃沙发成了碎条，屋里唯一的小靠垫瘫在角落，肠肚分撒东西南北。到处是烟灰——墙上，地上，炉台上，弗雷迪身上。一双死蝙蝠——后者在七个月没扫过的烟囱里乱摸的又一战果——安息在炉围里。这间起居间从来谈不上奢华；现在连温馨也谈不上了。

===Original===
Eve did not reply. She was struggling with what she was fair-minded enough to see was an entirely unjust fever of irritation, with her courteous and obliging assistant as its object. It was wrong, she knew, to feel like this. That she should be furious at her failure to find the jewels was excusable, but she had no possible right to be furious with Freddie. It was not his fault that soot had poured from the chimney in lieu of diamonds. If he had asked for a necklace and been given a dead bat, he was surely more to be pitied than censured. Yet Eve, eyeing his grimy face, would have given very much to have been able to scream loudly and throw something at him. The fact was, the Hon. Freddie belonged to that unfortunate type of humanity which automatically gets blamed for everything in moments of stress.

===Chinese===
伊芙没有答话。她在同一股火气角力——她有足够的公道心，看得出这火气全然无理，而矛头对着的是她那位殷勤的助手。她知道，这样想不对。珠宝没搜着、一腔怒火，这可以原谅；可对弗雷迪发火毫无道理。烟囱里不淌钻石淌烟灰，不是他的错。倘若他讨项链、领了只死蝙蝠，他理应多得怜悯、少受责备。然而伊芙望着他那张灰脸，情愿付出很大代价换取放声尖叫、朝他扔点什么的自由。事实是，弗雷迪少爷属于人类里那类倒霉品种：一遇风浪，什么事都自动算到他头上。

===Original===
“Well, the bally thing isn’t here,” said Freddie. He spoke thickly, as a man will whose mouth is covered with soot.

===Chinese===
「唉，这鬼玩意儿不在这儿，」弗雷迪说。他说得含混，满嘴烟灰的人都这样。

===Original===
“I know it isn’t,” said Eve. “But this isn’t the only room in the house.”

===Chinese===
「我知道不在这儿，」伊芙说。「可这屋里不止一间房。」

===Original===
“Think he might have hidden the stuff upstairs?”

===Chinese===
「你觉得他可能把货藏楼上了？」

===Original===
“Or downstairs.”

===Chinese===
「要不楼下。」

===Original===
Freddie shook his head, dislodging a portion of a third bat.

===Chinese===
弗雷迪摇摇头，摇下来第三只蝙蝠的一部分。

===Original===
“Must be upstairs, if it’s anywhere. Mean to say, there isn’t any downstairs.”

===Chinese===
「要藏只可能在楼上。我是说，楼下没有别的了。」

===Original===
“There’s the cellar,” said Eve. “Take your lamp and go and have a look.”

===Chinese===
「还有地窖，」伊芙说。「拿上灯，去看看。」

===Original===
For the first time in the proceedings a spirit of disaffection seemed to manifest itself in the bosom of her assistant. Up till this moment Freddie had taken his orders placidly and executed them with promptness and civility. Even when the first shower of soot had driven him choking from the fireplace, his manly spirit had not been crushed; he had merely uttered a startled “Oh, I say!” and returned gallantly to the attack. But now he obviously hesitated.

===Chinese===
程序进行到此刻，她的助手胸中头一回显出一点不满情绪。直到这会儿，弗雷迪一直安顺地领命，办得又快又客气。就连头一阵烟灰把他呛出壁炉时，他的男子气概也不曾垮；他只惊呼了一声「哦，我说！」，便勇敢地重新投入战斗。可现在他显然踌躇了。

===Original===
“Go on,” said Eve impatiently.

===Chinese===
「去呀，」伊芙不耐烦地说。

===Original===
“Yes, but, I say, you know﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「是，可是，我说，你知道……」

===Original===
“What’s the matter?”

===Chinese===
「怎么回事？」

===Original===
“I don’t think the chap would be likely to hide a necklace in the cellar. I vote we give it a miss and try upstairs.”

===Chinese===
「我觉得那家伙不至于把项链藏地窖。我投票跳过它，上楼碰碰。」

===Original===
“Don’t be silly, Freddie. He may have hidden it anywhere.”

===Chinese===
「别犯傻，弗雷迪。他哪儿都可能藏。」

===Original===
“Well, to be absolutely honest, I’d much rather not go into any bally cellar, if it’s all the same to you.”

===Chinese===
「这个，跟你说掏心窝子的话，要依着我，哪个鬼地窖我也不想进。」

===Original===
“Why ever not?”

===Chinese===
「到底为什么？」

===Original===
“Beetles. Always had a horror of beetles. Ever since I was a kid.”

===Chinese===
「甲虫。打小就怕甲虫。从小就这样。」

===Original===
Eve bit her lip. She was feeling, as Miss Peavey had so often felt when associated in some delicate undertaking with Edward Cootes, that exasperating sense of man’s inadequacy which comes to high-spirited girls at moments such as these. To achieve the end for which she had started out that night she would have waded waist-high through a sea of beetles. But, divining with that sixth sense which tells women when the male has been pushed just so far and can be pushed no farther, that Freddie, wax though he might be in her hands in any other circumstances, was on this one point adamant, she made no further effort to bend him to her will.

===Chinese===
伊芙咬住嘴唇。她此刻的心情，正同皮维小姐与爱德华·库茨合伙办细密之事时常常尝到的一样——那种血气方刚的姑娘在此等时刻尝到的、对男人不中用的切齿。为达成今夜出发的目的，让她蹚齐腰深的甲虫海她也干。但女人有一种第六感，知道男人被推到了哪一步便再推不动；她凭这第六感断定：弗雷迪任凭旁的事上她搓圆捏扁，唯独这一条是块生铁。她便不再设法弯他。

===Original===
“All right,” she said. “I’ll go down into the cellar. You go and look upstairs.”

===Chinese===
「行，」她说。「我下地窖。你上楼搜。」

===Original===
“No. I say, sure you don’t mind?”

===Chinese===
「别。我说，你真的不介意？」

===Original===
Eve took up her lamp and left the craven.

===Chinese===
伊芙提起灯，撇下这个孬种走了。

===Original===
---

===Chinese===
---

===Original===
For a girl of iron resolution and unswerving purpose, Eve’s inspection of the cellar was decidedly cursory. A distinct feeling of relief came over her as she stood at the top of the steps and saw by the light of the lamp how small and bare it was. For, impervious as she might be to the intimidation of beetles, her armour still contained a chink. She was terribly afraid of rats. And even when the rays of the lamp disclosed no scuttling horrors, she still lingered for a moment before descending. You never knew with rats. They pretended not to be there just to lure you on, and then came out and whizzed about your ankles. However, the memory of her scorn for Freddie’s pusillanimity forced her on, and she went down.

===Chinese===
以伊芙的铁决心和不回头的目的性而论，她那趟地窖视察草率得可以。立在阶顶、借灯光看清它有多小多空时，她心头一阵如释重负。因为，她纵然不怕甲虫的威吓，甲胄上终究有一道缝：她怕老鼠，怕得厉害。哪怕灯的光芒照不出任何窸窣的恐怖，她下去前仍滞了片刻。老鼠的事谁也说不准。它们先装作不在，专诱你深入，然后窜出来在你脚踝边嗖嗖地转。然而，想起自己对弗雷迪胆怯的鄙夷，逼着她走了下去。

===Original===
The word “cellar” is an elastic one. It can be applied equally to the acres of bottle-fringed vaults which lie beneath a great pile like Blandings Castle and to a hole in the ground like the one in which she now found herself. This cellar was easily searched. She stamped on its stone flags with an ear strained to detect any note of hollowness, but none came. She moved the lamp so that it shone into every corner, but there was not even a crack in which a diamond necklace could have been concealed. Satisfied that the place contained nothing but a little coal-dust and a smell of damp decay, Eve passed thankfully out.

===Chinese===
「地窖」是个有弹性的词。它既可指布兰丁斯城堡这类巨宅底下那几英亩瓶影森然的地下酒窖，也可指她此刻置身其中的这么一个土坑。这地窖好搜得很。她跺着石板地，耳朵绷紧了听有没有空膛的音响——没有。她移着灯照遍每个角落，连一道能藏下钻石项链的裂缝也没有。确信此地除了一点煤灰和一股潮湿的霉味别无长物，伊芙心怀感激地退了出去。

===Original===
The law of elimination was doing its remorseless work. It had ruled out the cellar, the kitchen, and the living-room﻿—that is to say, the whole of the lower of the two floors which made up the cottage. There now remained only the rooms upstairs. There were probably not more than two, and Freddie must already have searched one of these. The quest seemed to be nearing its end. As Eve made for the narrow staircase that led to the second floor, the lamp shook in her hand and cast weird shadows. Now that success was in sight, the strain was beginning to affect her nerves.

===Chinese===
排除法在做它无情的工作。它已排除了地窖、厨房和起居间——也就是说，小屋两层里的整个下层。剩下的只有楼上的房间。多半不过两间，其中一间弗雷迪想必已搜过了。搜寻似乎临近终点。伊芙朝通往二层的窄楼梯走去时，灯在她手里发抖，投下怪影。胜利在望，紧张开始咬她的神经了。

===Original===
It was to nerves that in the first instant of hearing it she attributed what sounded like a soft cough in the sitting-room, a few feet from where she stood. Then a chill feeling of dismay gripped her. It could only, she thought, be Freddie, returned from his search; and if Freddie had returned from his search already, what could it mean except that those upstairs rooms, on which she had counted so confidently, had proved as empty as the others? Freddie was not one of your restrained, unemotional men. If he had found the necklace he would have been downstairs in two bounds, shouting. His silence was ominous. She opened the door and went quickly in.

===Chinese===
起初听见那声像是轻轻咳嗽的动静时——就在离她几英尺的起居间里——她把它归给了神经。随即一股冰凉的沮丧攫住了她。她想，只能是弗雷迪，搜完了回来；而弗雷迪若已经搜完回来，除了说明她寄望最殷的楼上房间也同别处一样空空如也，还能有什么意思？弗雷迪不是那种内敛寡情的男人。他若找到了项链，两蹦就下了楼，一路喊到底。他这份安静是不祥之兆。她推门而入。

===Original===
“Freddie,” she began, and broke off with a gasp.

===Chinese===
「弗雷迪，」她开了口，一口气噎住了。

===Original===
It was not Freddie who had coughed. It was Psmith. He was seated on the remains of the horsehair sofa, toying with an automatic pistol and gravely surveying through his monocle the ruins of a home.

===Chinese===
咳嗽的不是弗雷迪。是皮史密斯。他坐在马鬃沙发的残骸上，手里把玩一把自动手枪，正隔着单片眼镜，庄严检阅一座家园的废墟。

### III / III

===Original===
“Good evening,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「晚上好，」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
It was not for a philosopher like himself to display astonishment. He was, however, undeniably feeling it. When, a few minutes before, he had encountered Freddie in this same room, he had received a distinct shock; but a rough theory which would account for Freddie’s presence in his home-from-home he had been able to work out. He groped in vain for one which would explain Eve.

===Chinese===
似他这般哲人，不当流露惊异。然而他确凿地感到了惊异。几分钟前，他在这同一间屋里撞见弗雷迪时，他着实吃了一惊；但为弗雷迪出现在他这个第二家园里编一套说得通的理论，他还办得到。要编一套解释伊芙的，他搜肠刮肚而不得。

===Original===
Mere surprise, however, was never enough to prevent Psmith talking. He began at once.

===Chinese===
不过，单凭惊讶从来拦不住皮史密斯说话。他立刻开讲。

===Original===
“It was nice of you,” he said, rising courteously, “to look in. Won’t you sit down? On the sofa, perhaps? Or would you prefer a brick?”

===Chinese===
「诸位光临，」他起身致意道，「真是太好了。请坐？坐沙发，还是挑一块砖？」

===Original===
Eve was not yet equal to speech. She had been so firmly convinced that he was ten miles away at Shifley that his presence here in the sitting-room of the cottage had something of the breathtaking quality of a miracle. The explanation, if she could have known it, was simple. Two excellent reasons had kept Psmith from gracing the County Ball with his dignified support. In the first place, as Shifley was only four miles from the village where he had spent most of his life, he had regarded it as probable, if not certain, that he would have encountered there old friends to whom it would have been both tedious and embarrassing to explain why he had changed his name to McTodd. And secondly, though he had not actually anticipated a nocturnal raid on his little nook, he had thought it well to be on the premises that evening in case Mr. Edward Cootes should have been getting ideas into his head. As soon, therefore, as the castle had emptied itself and the wheels of the last car had passed away down the drive, he had pocketed Mr. Cootes’s revolver and proceeded to the cottage.

===Chinese===
伊芙一时还说不出话。她先前那样笃定他在十英里外的希夫利，此刻他现身小屋起居间，简直带着奇迹的夺人气势。个中缘由——倘若她能知晓——其实简单。两条过硬的理由，使皮史密斯未能以他尊严的加持为郡舞会增光。其一，希夫利距他度过大半生的村子只有四英里，他认定，若非必然，至少多半会在那儿撞见旧识，跟人家解释他何以改姓麦克托德，既乏味又难堪。其二，他虽不曾料到会有人夜袭他的小窝，但也想到不妨当晚在房子里坐镇，以防爱德华·库茨先生脑袋里生出什么主意。所以，城堡一空、最后一辆车的轮声沿车道消逝，他便揣上库茨先生的左轮，移驾小屋。

===Original===
Eve recovered her self-possession. She was not a girl given to collapse in moments of crisis. The first shock of amazement had passed; a humiliating feeling of extreme foolishness, which came directly after, had also passed; she was now grimly ready for battle.

===Chinese===
伊芙恢复了自持。她不是危机一来便瘫软的姑娘。第一阵惊愕已经过去；紧随其后那阵觉得自己蠢透了的羞辱感，也过去了；此刻她冷冷地准备开战。

===Original===
“Where is Mr. Threepwood?” she asked.

===Chinese===
「思里普伍德先生呢？」她问。

===Original===
“Upstairs. I have put him in storage for a while. Do not worry about Comrade Threepwood. He has lots to think about. He is under the impression that if he stirs out he will be instantly shot.”

===Chinese===
「楼上。我把他入库存放一阵子了。不必为思里普伍德同志担心。他有的是可想的。他目前的认识是：敢挪窝，立刻吃枪子。」

===Original===
“Oh? Well, I want to put this lamp down. Will you please pick up that table?”

===Chinese===
「哦？那好，我要把灯放下。请你把那张桌子扶起来，可否？」

===Original===
“By all means. But﻿—I am a novice in these matters﻿—ought I not first to say ‘Hands up!’ or something?”

===Chinese===
「遵命。不过——我于此道还是新手——按规矩，我是不是该先来一句『举起手来！』什么的？」

===Original===
“Will you please pick up that table?”

===Chinese===
「请你把那张桌子扶起来。」

===Original===
“A friend of mine﻿—one Cootes﻿—you must meet him some time﻿—generally remarks ‘Hey!’ in a sharp, arresting voice on these occasions. Personally I consider the expression too abrupt. Still, he has had great experience﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我的一位朋友——姓库茨——改天你务必见见——每逢这种场合，总用尖利而叫人不敢不从的嗓音说一声『喂！』。个人以为，这表达太突兀。不过，人家经验丰富……」

===Original===
“Will you please pick up that table?”

===Chinese===
「请你把那张桌子扶起来。」

===Original===
“Most certainly. I take it, then, that you would prefer to dispense with the usual formalities. In that case, I will park this revolver on the mantelpiece while we chat. I have taken a curious dislike to the thing. It makes me feel like Dangerous Dan McGrew.”

===Chinese===
「遵命遵命。那么，我理解，例行客套你一概免了。这样的话，谈话期间，我把这把左轮寄放在壁炉台上。我对这东西已经生出一种说不出的厌恶。它害我觉得自己是危险人物丹·麦格鲁。」

===Original===
Eve put down the lamp, and there was silence for a moment. Psmith looked about him thoughtfully. He picked up one of the dead bats and covered it with his handkerchief.

===Chinese===
伊芙放下灯，一时无话。皮史密斯若有所思地环顾四周。他拾起一只死蝙蝠，用手帕把它盖好。

===Original===
“Somebody’s mother,” he murmured reverently.

===Chinese===
「哪位母亲，」他肃然低语。

===Original===
Eve sat down on the sofa.

===Chinese===
伊芙在沙发上坐下。

===Original===
“Mr.﻿ ﻿…” She stopped. “I can’t call you Mr. McTodd. Will you please tell me your name?”

===Chinese===
「……先生，」她顿住了。「我没法叫你麦克托德先生。可否请你告知尊姓大名？」

===Original===
“Ronald,” said Psmith. “Ronald Eustace.”

===Chinese===
「罗纳德，」皮史密斯说。「罗纳德·尤斯塔斯。」

===Original===
“I suppose you have a surname?” snapped Eve. “Or an alias?”

===Chinese===
「想必还有个姓？」伊芙厉声说。「要不，是个化名？」

===Original===
Psmith eyed her with a pained expression.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯带着受伤的神情看她。

===Original===
“I may be hypersensitive,” he said, “but that last remark sounded to me like a dirty dig. You seem to imply that I am some sort of a criminal.”

===Chinese===
「也许我神经过敏，」他说，「可我听那句里有股阴阳怪气的挖苦。你似乎在暗示，我是某种罪犯。」

===Original===
Eve laughed shortly.

===Chinese===
伊芙短促一笑。

===Original===
“I’m sorry if I hurt your feelings. There’s not much sense in pretending now, is there? What is your name?”

===Chinese===
「伤了你的感情，抱歉。事到如今再装，没什么意思了吧？你到底姓什么？」

===Original===
“Psmith. The *p* is silent.”

===Chinese===
「皮史密斯。*皮*字头的 P，不发音。」

===Original===
“Well, Mr. Smith, I imagine you understand why I am here?”

===Chinese===
「好吧，史密斯先生，我想你明白我为什么来？」

===Original===
“I took it for granted that you had come to fulfil your kindly promise of doing the place up a bit. Will you be wounded if I say frankly that I preferred it the way it was before? All this may be the last word in ultramodern interior decoration, but I suppose I am old-fashioned. The whisper flies round Shropshire and adjoining counties, ‘Psmith is hidebound. He is not attuned to up-to-date methods.’ Honestly, don’t you think you have rather unduly stressed the bizarre note? This soot﻿ ﻿… these dead bats﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我本想当然地以为，你是来兑现你好心的承诺，把这地方拾掴拾掴。倘我直言更喜欢原先的样子，你可会见怪？这一切兴许是超现代室内装修的最新境界，但我大概是个老古板。耳语正飞遍什罗普郡及邻近各郡：『皮史密斯脑子僵化。他不合时务。』说老实话，你不觉得这古怪的调子上得太重了吗？这些烟灰……这些死蝙蝠……」

===Original===
“I have come to get that necklace.”

===Chinese===
「我是来取项链的。」

===Original===
“Ah! The necklace!”

===Chinese===
「啊！项链！」

===Original===
“I’m going to get it, too.”

===Chinese===
「而且我一定要取到。」

===Original===
Psmith shook his head gently.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯轻轻摇头。

===Original===
“There,” he said, “if you will pardon me, I take issue with you. There is nobody to whom I would rather give that necklace than you, but there are special circumstances connected with it which render such an action impossible. I fancy, Miss Halliday, that you have been misled by your young friend upstairs. No; let me speak,” he said, raising a hand. “You know what a treat it is to me. The way I envisage the matter is thus. I still cannot understand as completely as I could wish how you come to be mixed up in the affair, but it is plain that in some way or other Comrade Threepwood has enlisted your services, and I regret to be obliged to inform you that the motives animating him in this quest are not pure. To put it crisply, he is engaged in what Comrade Cootes, to whom I alluded just now, would call ‘funny business.’ ”

===Chinese===
「这一点，」他说，「恕我不敢苟同。天下我宁肯把项链奉送的，头一个就是你；无奈它连着几桩特殊情形，使此举办不到。我猜，哈利迪小姐，你是让楼上你那位小朋友带进沟里了。不；容我说，」他抬手道。「你知道这于我是何等的享受。依我看，事情是这样的。你是怎么卷进这桩事的，我至今未能尽如所愿地理清；但思里普伍德同志显然是想方设法征用了你，而我遗憾地不得不奉告：他此番行动的动机，不纯。说干脆些，他正在干的，用我方才提到的库茨同志的话说，叫『滑稽戏法』。」

===Original===
“I﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我……」

===Original===
“Pardon me,” said Psmith. “If you will be patient for a few minutes more, I shall have finished and shall then be delighted to lend an attentive ear to any remarks you may wish to make. As it occurs to me﻿—indeed, you hinted as much yourself just now﻿—that my own position in this little matter has an appearance which to the uninitiated might seem tolerably rummy, I had better explain how I come to be guarding a diamond necklace which does not belong to me. I rely on your womanly discretion to let the thing go no further.”

===Chinese===
「恕我，」皮史密斯说。「再耐心几分钟，我就讲完了，然后你有什么高见，我洗耳恭听。我忽然想到——其实你方才自己也有此暗示——我本人在这桩小事里的处境，在外行人看来兴许相当蹊跷，我还是解释一下我何以在守护一条不属于我的钻石项链为好。我仰仗你女性家的口风，勿使外传。」

===Original===
“Will you please﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「请你……」

===Original===
“In one moment. The facts are as follows. Our mutual friend Mr. Keeble, Miss Halliday, has a stepdaughter who is married to one Comrade Jackson who, if he had no other claim to fame, would go ringing down through history for this reason, that he and I were at school together and that he is my best friend. We two have sported on the green﻿—ooh, a lot of times. Well, owing to one thing and another, the Jackson family is rather badly up against it at the present﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「稍安。事实如下。咱们共同的朋友基布尔先生，哈利迪小姐，有位继女，嫁与了杰克逊同志；此君若无别的可夸，凭这一条也将名垂青史：他与我同过学，且是我最好的朋友。我们两个在草地上耍过——哎呀，耍过的次数海了去了。是这样，因着种种缘故，杰克逊一家目前手头相当拮据……」

===Original===
Eve jumped up angrily.

===Chinese===
伊芙愤然跃起。

===Original===
“I don’t believe a word of it,” she cried. “What is the use of trying to fool me like this? You had never heard of Phyllis before Freddie spoke about her in the train﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我一个字也不信，」她喊。「这样糊弄我有什么用？弗雷迪在火车上提菲利斯的头里，你根本没听说过她……」

===Original===
“Believe me﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「请相信我……」

===Original===
“I won’t. Freddie got you down here to help him steal that necklace and give it to Mr. Keeble so that he could help Phyllis, and now you’ve got it and are trying to keep it for yourself.”

===Chinese===
「我不。是弗雷迪把你请下来帮他偷项链、交给基布尔先生、好让他帮菲利斯的；现在东西到了你手里，你想自己昧下。」

===Original===
Psmith started slightly. His monocle fell from its place.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯微微一震。单片眼镜滑了下来。

===Original===
“Is *everybody* in this little plot! Are you also one of Comrade Keeble’s corps of assistants?”

===Chinese===
「这小小的密谋里莫非*人人有份*！你莫非也是基布尔同志麾下助手军团的一员？」

===Original===
“Mr. Keeble asked me to try to get the necklace for him.”

===Chinese===
「基布尔先生请我试着替他把项链弄到手。」

===Original===
Psmith replaced his monocle thoughtfully.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯若有所思地把单片眼镜归位。

===Original===
“This,” he said, “opens up a new line of thought. Can it be that I have been wronging Comrade Threepwood all this time? I must confess that, when I found him here just now standing like Marius among the ruins of Carthage (the allusion is a classical one, and the fruit of an expensive education), I jumped﻿—I may say, sprang﻿—to the conclusion that he was endeavouring to double-cross both myself and the boss by getting hold of the necklace with a view to retaining it for his own benefit. It never occurred to me that he might be crediting me with the same sinful guile.”

===Chinese===
「这，」他说，「开辟了一条新的思路。莫非我一直冤枉了思里普伍德同志？我必须招认，方才在这儿撞见他、瞧他那副站在迦太基废墟中的马略的架势（此典出自古典，是高价教育的果实）时，我一跳——不妨说，一蹦——便得出结论：他这是想黑吃黑，连我带东家一锅端，把项链据为己有。我压根没想到，他兴许正以同样的罪恶心机揣度我呢。」

===Original===
Eve ran to him and clutched his arm.

===Chinese===
伊芙跑上前，抓住他的胳膊。

===Original===
“Mr. Smith, is this really true? Are you really a friend of Phyllis?”

===Chinese===
「史密斯先生，这是真的？你真是菲利斯的朋友？」

===Original===
“She looks on me as a grandfather. Are *you* a friend of hers?”

===Chinese===
「她拿我当爷爷看。*你*才是，是她的朋友？」

===Original===
“We were at school together.”

===Chinese===
「我们同学。」

===Original===
“This,” said Psmith cordially, “is one of the most gratifying moments of my life. It makes us all seem like one great big family.”

===Chinese===
「这，」皮史密斯由衷地说，「是我平生最畅快的时刻之一。咱们这下全成一家人了。」

===Original===
“But I never heard Phyllis speak about you.”

===Chinese===
「可我从没听菲利斯提起过你。」

===Original===
“Strange!” said Psmith. “Strange. Surely she was not ashamed of her humble friend?”

===Chinese===
「怪哉！」皮史密斯说。「怪哉。她不至于以她卑微的朋友为耻吧？」

===Original===
“Her what?”

===Chinese===
「她的什么？」

===Original===
“I must explain,” said Psmith, “that until recently I was earning a difficult livelihood by slinging fish about in Billingsgate Market. It is possible that some snobbish strain in Comrade Jackson’s bride, which I confess I had not suspected, kept her from admitting that she was accustomed to hobnob with one in the fish business.”

===Chinese===
「我必须解释，」皮史密斯说，「直到不久前，我还靠在比林斯门市场抛鱼谋生，日子艰难。杰克逊同志的新娘身上兴许有那么一点——我招认我此前未曾察觉——势利气，使她不愿承认她素有鱼行一位朋友的过从。」

===Original===
“Good gracious!” cried Eve.

===Chinese===
「天哪！」伊芙喊。

===Original===
“I beg your pardon?”

===Chinese===
「你说什么？」

===Original===
“Smith﻿ ﻿… Fish business﻿ ﻿… Why, it was you who called at Phyllis’s house while I was there. Just before I came down here. I remember Phyllis saying how sorry she was that we had not met. She said you were just my sort of﻿ ﻿… I mean, she said she wanted me to meet you.”

===Chinese===
「史密斯……鱼行……哎呀，去菲利斯家拜访、正赶上我在场的那个人，就是你。就在我下来这儿之前。菲利斯还念叨，说咱们没碰上面真可惜。她说你正是我这一路的……我是说，她说她正想让我见见你。」

===Original===
“This,” said Psmith, “is becoming more and more gratifying every moment. It seems to me that you and I were made for each other. I am your best friend’s best friend and we both have a taste for stealing other people’s jewellery. I cannot see how you can very well resist the conclusion that we are twin-souls.”

===Chinese===
「这，」皮史密斯说，「是一刻比一刻振奋了。依我看，你我是天造地设。你最好朋友的最好朋友，而且咱俩都好偷旁人的珠宝。我看你很难逃出这个结论：咱们是双生灵魂。」

===Original===
“Don’t be silly.”

===Chinese===
「别犯傻。」

===Original===
“We shall get into that series of ‘Husbands and Wives Who Work Together.’ ”

===Chinese===
「咱们要上《并肩劳作的夫妻们》那套系列了。」

===Original===
“Where is the necklace?”

===Chinese===
「项链在哪儿？」

===Original===
Psmith sighed.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯叹了口气。

===Original===
“The business note. Always the business note. Can’t we keep all that till later?”

===Chinese===
「公事腔。永远是公事腔。这些不能留待以后吗？」

===Original===
“No. We can’t.”

===Chinese===
「不能。」

===Original===
“Ah, well!”

===Chinese===
「唉，好吧！」

===Original===
Psmith crossed the room, and took down from the wall the case of stuffed birds.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯穿过房间，从墙上摘下那只标本鸟玻璃罩。

===Original===
“The one place,” said Eve, with mortification, “where we didn’t think of looking!”

===Chinese===
「唯一一处，」伊芙懊恼地说，「我们没想到找的地方！」

===Original===
Psmith opened the case and removed the centre bird, a depressed-looking fowl with glass eyes which stared with a haunting pathos. He felt in its interior and pulled out something that glittered and sparkled in the lamplight.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯打开罩子，取出正中那只鸟——一只蔫头蔫脑、玻璃眼睛含着萦绕哀愁的家禽。他往它肚里一摸，拽出一件在灯光下闪闪烁烁、火树银花的东西。

===Original===
“Oh!”

===Chinese===
「哦！」

===Original===
Eve ran her fingers almost lovingly through the jewels as they lay before her on the little table.

===Chinese===
项链摊在小桌上，伊芙的手指近乎爱怜地插进珠宝里。

===Original===
“Aren’t they beautiful!”

===Chinese===
「多美呀！」

===Original===
“Distinctly. I think I may say that of all the jewels I have ever stolen﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「可不是。在我偷过的全部珠宝里，我可以说……」

===Original===
“**Hey**!”

===Chinese===
「**喂**！」

===Original===
Eve let the necklace fall with a cry. Psmith spun round. In the doorway stood Mr. Edward Cootes, pointing a pistol.

===Chinese===
伊芙一声惊呼，项链脱了手。皮史密斯霍地转身。门口立着爱德华·库茨先生，端着一支手枪。

### IV / IV

===Original===
“Hands up!” said Mr. Cootes with the uncouth curtness of one who has not had the advantages of a refined home and a nice upbringing. He advanced warily, preceded by the revolver. It was a dainty, miniature weapon, such as might have been the property of some gentle lady. Mr. Cootes had, in fact, borrowed it from Miss Peavey, who at this juncture entered the room in a black and silver dinner-dress surmounted by a Rose du Barri wrap, her spiritual face glowing softly in the subdued light.

===Chinese===
「举起手来！」库茨先生说，带着那种没有优越家境与良好教养者的粗鲁简捷。他小心前进，左轮开路。那是一柄精巧的袖珍武器，像是哪位淑女的私物。库茨先生的确是从皮维小姐处借来的；后者此刻随他进屋，一身黑银相间的晚装，外罩一件杜巴里玫瑰色的披肩，那张灵性的脸在幽光里柔柔发亮。

===Original===
“Attaboy, Ed,” observed Miss Peavey crisply.

===Chinese===
「好样儿的，埃德，」皮维小姐脆生生地评了一句。

===Original===
She swooped on the table and gathered up the necklace. Mr. Cootes, though probably gratified by the tribute, made no acknowledgment of it, but continued to direct an austere gaze at Eve and Psmith.

===Chinese===
她扑向桌子，把项链拢进手中。库茨先生对这嘉许想必受用，却未致谢，只继续朝伊芙和皮史密斯投以严峻的注视。

===Original===
“No funny business,” he advised.

===Chinese===
「别耍花样，」他忠告道。

===Original===
“I would be the last person,” said Psmith agreeably, “to advocate anything of the sort. This,” he said to Eve, “is Comrade Cootes, of whom you have heard so much.”

===Chinese===
「我将是最后一个，」皮史密斯和悦地说，「主张耍任何花样的人。这位，」他对伊芙说，「就是库茨同志，你久闻大名的。」

===Original===
Eve was staring, bewildered, at the poetess, who, satisfied with the manner in which the preliminaries had been conducted, had begun looking about her with idle curiosity.

===Chinese===
伊芙正愕然瞪着女诗人——后者对开局的手法颇为满意，已开始带着闲散的好奇四下打量。

===Original===
“Miss Peavey!” cried Eve. Of all the events of this eventful night the appearance of Lady Constance’s emotional friend in the role of criminal was the most disconcerting. “Miss *Peavey*!”

===Chinese===
「皮维小姐！」伊芙喊。今夜这许多事变里，最叫人措手不及的，莫过于康斯坦丝夫人那位多情的朋友以罪犯身份登场。「皮维*小姐*！」

===Original===
“Hallo?” responded that lady agreeably.

===Chinese===
「怎么了？」那位太太和气地应。

===Original===
“I﻿ ﻿… I﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我……我……」

===Original===
“What, I think, Miss Halliday is trying to say,” cut in Psmith, “is that she is finding it a little difficult to adjust her mind to the present development. I, too, must confess myself somewhat at a loss. I knew, of course, that Comrade Cootes had﻿—shall I say an acquisitive streak in him, but you I had always supposed to be one hundred percent soul﻿—and snowy white at that.”

===Chinese===
「我想，哈利迪小姐想说的是，」皮史密斯插话，「她的头脑一时半会儿还转不过这道弯。我自己也必须承认相当茫然。我当然知道库茨同志身上有——怎么说呢——一股占有欲；可你，我一向认定是百分之百的灵魂——而且还是雪白的。」

===Original===
“Yeah?” said Miss Peavey, but faintly interested.

===Chinese===
「是吗？」皮维小姐说，兴致缺缺。

===Original===
“I imagined that you were a poetess.”

===Chinese===
「我一直当你是位女诗人。」

===Original===
“So I am a poetess,” retorted Miss Peavey hotly. “Just you start in joshing my poems and see how quick I’ll bean you with a brick. Well, Ed, no sense in sticking around here. Let’s go.”

===Chinese===
「我本来就是女诗人，」皮维小姐炸了。「有种你就来笑话我的诗，看我不拿砖头开了你的瓢。行了，埃德，在这儿耗着没意思。走。」

===Original===
“We’ll have to tie these birds up,” said Mr. Cootes. “Otherwise we’ll have them squealing before I can make a getaway.”

===Chinese===
「这俩得绑起来，」库茨先生说。「不然我还没脱身他们就要喊。」

===Original===
“Ed,” said Miss Peavey with the scorn which her colleague so often excited in her, “try to remember sometimes that that thing balanced on your collar is a head, not a hubbard squash. And be careful what you’re doing with that gat! Waving it about like it was a bouquet or something. How are they going to squeal? They can’t say a thing without telling everyone they snitched the stuff first.”

===Chinese===
「埃德，」皮维小姐说，带着同事在她身上常激起的那种鄙夷，「麻烦你偶尔记一记：你领子上顶着的那玩意儿是颗脑袋，不是只金瓜。还有，摆弄那家伙小心点！抡得跟捧花束似的。他们怎么喊？他们敢吭一声，就得先招自己先摸了货。」

===Original===
“That’s right,” admitted Mr. Cootes.

===Chinese===
「这话对，」库茨先生认了。

===Original===
“Well, then, don’t come butting in.”

===Chinese===
「那你就别瞎掺和。」

===Original===
The silence into which this rebuke plunged Mr. Cootes gave Psmith the opportunity to resume speech. An opportunity of which he was glad, for, while he had nothing of definitely vital import to say, he was optimist enough to feel that his only hope of recovering the necklace was to keep the conversation going on the chance of something turning up. Affable though his manner was, he had never lost sight of the fact that one leap would take him across the space of floor separating him from Mr. Cootes. At present, that small but effective revolver precluded anything in the nature of leaps, however short, but if in the near future anything occurred to divert his adversary’s vigilance even momentarily.﻿ ﻿… He pursued a policy of watchful waiting, and in the meantime started to talk again.

===Chinese===
这顿抢白把库茨先生抢进了沉默，也给了皮史密斯重开话头的机会。他乐得有此机会；因为，虽说没有什么一言九鼎的大事要讲，但他足够乐观地觉得：夺回项链的唯一希望，就在于让谈话持续下去，静候事变。他态度虽然一团和气，心里却从未丢开一个事实：一步就能跨过他与库茨先生之间那片地板。目前，那柄小而顶用的左轮排除了一切跳——不论短跳长跳；但不远的将来，只要有什么分散对手警戒片刻……他奉行静观待变的方针，同时接着说了下去。

===Original===
“If, before you go,” he said, “you can spare us a moment of your valuable time, I should be glad of a few words. And, first, may I say that I cordially agree with your condemnation of Comrade Cootes’s recent suggestion. The man is an ass.”

===Chinese===
「二位动身之前，」他说，「若能从宝贵的光阴里拨我片刻，我有几句话奉告。首先，容我说：方才你对库茨同志那项提议的驳斥，我由衷赞同。此人是头驴。」

===Original===
“Say!” cried Mr. Cootes, coming to life again, “that’ll be about all from you. If there wasn’t ladies present, I’d bust you one.”

===Chinese===
「喂！」库茨先生喊，又活了，「你也就配说到这儿。要不是有女士在场，我揍你个满地找牙。」

===Original===
“Ed,” said Miss Peavey with quiet authority, “shut your trap!”

===Chinese===
「埃德，」皮维小姐以沉静的威权说，「闭上你的臭嘴！」

===Original===
Mr. Cootes subsided once more. Psmith gazed at him through his monocle, interested.

===Chinese===
库茨先生再次蔫了。皮史密斯隔着单片眼镜，兴致盎然地端详他。

===Original===
“Pardon me,” he said, “but﻿—if it is not a rude question﻿—are you two married?”

===Chinese===
「恕我冒昧，」他说，「——若这问题不算失礼——二位是夫妻吗？」

===Original===
“Eh?”

===Chinese===
「嗯？」

===Original===
“You seemed to me to talk to him like a wife. Am I addressing Mrs. Cootes?”

===Chinese===
「我听你对他的口气很像太太。我是在同库茨太太说话吗？」

===Original===
“You will be if you stick around a while.”

===Chinese===
「你再黏一会儿就是了。」

===Original===
“A thousand congratulations to Comrade Cootes. Not quite so many to you, possibly, but fully that number of good wishes.” He moved towards the poetess with extended hand. “I am thinking of getting married myself shortly.”

===Chinese===
「向库茨同志致以千份恭贺。给你的或许少几百份，但祝愿十足千份。」他朝女诗人伸出手去。「我自己不久也打算结婚。」

===Original===
“Keep those hands up,” said Mr. Cootes.

===Chinese===
「手举着别放，」库茨先生说。

===Original===
“Surely,” said Psmith reproachfully, “these conventions need not be observed among friends? You will find the only revolver I have ever possessed over there on the mantelpiece. Go and look at it.”

===Chinese===
「哪里的话，」皮史密斯责备道，「朋友之间，还讲究这套虚文？我平生唯一的一把左轮，就在那边壁炉台上。去看看嘛。」

===Original===
“Yes, and have you jumping on my back the moment I took my eyes off you!”

===Chinese===
「看个头！我一转眼你就扑我背上！」

===Original===
“There is a suspicious vein in your nature, Comrade Cootes,” sighed Psmith, “which I do not like to see. Fight against it.” He turned to Miss Peavey once more. “To resume a pleasanter topic, you will let me know where to send the plated fish-slice, won’t you?”

===Chinese===
「你天性里有条多疑的筋，库茨同志，」皮史密斯叹道，「我不乐见。要跟它斗。」他又转向皮维小姐。「换个愉快点的话题：镀银鱼刀寄哪儿，回头告诉我一声，可好？」

===Original===
“Huh?” said the lady.

===Chinese===
「啊？」那位太太说。

===Original===
“I was hoping,” proceeded Psmith, “if you do not think it a liberty on the part of one who has known you but a short time, to be allowed to send you a small wedding-present in due season. And one of these days, perhaps, when I too am married, you and Comrade Cootes will come and visit us in our little home. You will receive a hearty, unaffected welcome. You must not be offended if, just before you say goodbye, we count the spoons.”

===Chinese===
「我本希望，」皮史密斯接着说，「——倘若你不认为一个相识不久的人太唐突——能容我在合适的时候奉上一份小小的结婚礼物。而且哪一天，等我也成了家，你和库茨同志务必来我们的寒舍做客。你们将受到热忱而不掺假的欢迎。只是临别之际我们要数一数勺子，万勿见怪。」

===Original===
One would scarcely have supposed Miss Peavey a sensitive woman, yet at this remark an ominous frown clouded her white forehead. Her careless amiability seemed to wane. She raked Psmith with a glittering eye.

===Chinese===
几乎没人会把皮维小姐当作敏感的女人，可这话一出，一道不祥的阴云罩上了她白皙的额头。她那随和的悦色似乎淡了下去。她用一只雪亮的眼睛把皮史密斯来回耙。

===Original===
“You’re talking a dam’ lot,” she observed coldly.

===Chinese===
「你话可真多，」她冷冷地说。

===Original===
“An old failing of mine,” said Psmith apologetically, “and one concerning which there have been numerous complaints. I see now that I have been boring you, and I hope that you will allow me to express.﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「老毛病了，」皮史密斯赔罪道，「为此收到的投诉也很多。我这就明白了，我烦着你了，请容我表达……」

===Original===
He broke off abruptly, not because he had reached the end of his remarks, but because at this moment there came from above their heads a sudden sharp cracking sound, and almost simultaneously a shower of plaster fell from the ceiling, followed by the startling appearance of a long, shapely leg, which remained waggling in space. And from somewhere out of sight there filtered down a sharp and agonised oath.

===Chinese===
他猛地收住，不是因为讲完了，而是因为头顶上突然炸响一声脆裂，几乎同时，天花板簌簌落下一阵灰雨，紧接着，一条修长秀美的腿破顶而出，在半空晃荡。看不见的什么地方，滤下来一声尖锐痛楚的咒骂。

===Original===
Time and neglect had done their work with the flooring of the room in which Psmith had bestowed the Hon. Freddie Threepwood, and, creeping cautiously about in the dark, he had had the misfortune to go through.

===Chinese===
光阴与疏于保养，早已对皮史密斯安置弗雷迪·思里普伍德少爷那间房的房板做足了工夫；他在黑暗里小心爬动，不幸一脚踏穿。

===Original===
But, as so often happens in this life, the misfortune of one is the good fortune of another. Badly as the accident had shaken Freddie, from the point of view of Psmith it was almost ideal. The sudden appearance of a human leg through the ceiling at a moment of nervous tension is enough to unman the stoutest-hearted, and Edward Cootes made no attempt to conceal his perturbation. Leaping a clear six inches from the floor, he jerked up his head and quite unintentionally pulled the trigger of his revolver. A bullet ripped through the plaster.

===Chinese===
然而，正如人生中屡见不鲜的：一人的不幸即另一人的幸运。这一跤虽摔得弗雷迪七荤八素，从皮史密斯的角度看却近乎理想。神经紧绷之际，天花板陡然降下一条人腿，足以瓦解最硬的好汉；爱德华·库茨毫不掩饰他的慌乱。他离地蹦起足足六英寸，猛一抬头，鬼使神差扣了扳机。一颗子弹撕开灰顶。

===Original===
The leg disappeared. Not for an instant since he had been shut in that upper room had Freddie Threepwood ceased to be mindful of Psmith’s parting statement that he would be shot if he tried to escape, and Mr. Cootes’ bullet seemed to him a dramatic fulfilment of that promise. Wrenching his leg with painful energy out of the abyss, he proceeded to execute a backward spring which took him to the far wall﻿—at which point, as it was impossible to get any farther away from the centre of events, he was compelled to halt his retreat. Having rolled himself up into as small a ball as he could manage, he sat where he was, trying not to breathe. His momentary intention of explaining through the hole that the entire thing had been a regrettable accident, he prudently abandoned. Unintelligent though he had often proved himself in other crises of his life, he had the sagacity now to realise that the neighbourhood of the hole was unhealthy and should be avoided. So, preserving a complete and unbroken silence, he crouched there in the darkness, only asking to be left alone.

===Chinese===
腿缩回去了。自打被关进那间上房，弗雷迪·思里普伍德一刻不曾忘怀皮史密斯临别的交代：敢逃就枪毙。库茨先生这颗子弹，在他看来正是那句承诺的戏剧性兑现。他忍痛发力，把腿从深渊里拔出来，随即一个后跃，直抵远墙——到了这一步，再远也离不了事件中心半分，撤退只好到此为止。他把身子团成尽可能小的一团，坐在原地，力求不呼吸。他本有一闪之念，想隔着窟窿解释一下这一切纯属遗憾的意外，又明智地打消了。他平生历次危机虽常显智力不逮，此刻却有这份机灵：窟窿附近不宜久留。于是他保持彻底的、不间断的静默，蜷在黑暗里，只求别来惹他。

===Original===
And it seemed, as the moments slipped by, that this modest wish was to be gratified. Noises and the sound of voices came up to him from the room below, but no more bullets. It would be paltering with the truth to say that this put him completely at his ease, but still it was something. Freddie’s pulse began to return to the normal.

===Chinese===
而随着时间流逝，这个朴素的愿望似乎真要实现了。楼下房间的动静和人声不断传上来，却再没有子弹。说这让他完全安了心，未免不实，但总归是点安慰。弗雷迪的脉搏开始回归正常。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes’, on the other hand, was beating with a dangerous quickness. Swift and objectionable things had been happening to Edward Cootes in that lower room. His first impression was that the rift in the plaster above him had been instantly followed by the collapse of the entire ceiling, but this was a mistaken idea. All that had occurred was that Psmith, finding Mr. Cootes’ eye and pistol functioning in another direction, had sprung forward, snatched up a chair, hit the unfortunate man over the head with it, relieved him of his pistol, leaped to the mantelpiece, removed the revolver which lay there, and now, holding both weapons in an attitude of menace, was regarding him censoriously through a gleaming eyeglass.

===Chinese===
库茨先生的脉搏，另一边厢，正跳得危险地快。就在楼下那间屋里，疾风骤雨的坏事接连落在爱德华·库茨头上。他的第一印象是，头顶灰顶一裂，整面天花板随即塌了；这是误判。实情是：皮史密斯见库茨先生的眼睛和手枪双双转向别处，一跃而前，抄起一把椅子，照那位倒霉人的头顶砸落，卸了他的枪，再一个箭步蹿上壁炉台，收走搁在那儿的左轮，此刻双枪在手、摆足威慑的架势，正用一只闪亮的单片眼镜对他行注目礼。

===Original===
“No funny business, Comrade Cootes,” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「别耍花样，库茨同志，」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
Mr. Cootes picked himself up painfully. His head was singing. He looked at the revolvers, blinked, opened his mouth and shut it again. He was oppressed with a sense of defeat. Nature had not built him for a man of violence. Peaceful manipulation of a pack of cards in the smoke-room of an Atlantic liner was a thing he understood and enjoyed: rough-and-tumble encounters were alien to him and distasteful. As far as Mr. Cootes was concerned, the war was over.

===Chinese===
库茨先生艰难地爬起来。他脑袋嗡嗡作响。他看看双枪，眨眨眼，张开嘴，又合上。他被挫败感压弯了。老天爷没把他造成暴力型人才。在「大西洋」号吸烟室里温文尔雅地摆弄一副纸牌，是他懂行而享受的营生：拳脚相加的混战，于他既陌生又倒胃。就库茨先生而言，战争结束了。

===Original===
But Miss Peavey was a woman of spirit. Her hat was still in the ring. She clutched the necklace in a grasp of steel, and her fine eyes glared defiance.

===Chinese===
但皮维小姐是个有血性的女人。她的帽子还留在拳台上。她钢铁般攥紧项链，一双秀眼燃烧着不服。

===Original===
“You think yourself smart, don’t you?” she said.

===Chinese===
「你觉得自己很机灵，是吧？」她说。

===Original===
Psmith eyed her commiseratingly. Her valorous attitude appealed to him. Nevertheless, business was business.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯怜惜地看着她。她这副骁勇的姿态很得他欣赏。然而公事是公事。

===Original===
“I am afraid,” he said regretfully, “that I must trouble you to hand over that necklace.”

===Chinese===
「恐怕，」他遗憾地说，「只好有劳你交出那条项链了。」

===Original===
“Try and get it,” said Miss Peavey.

===Chinese===
「有本事来拿，」皮维小姐说。

===Original===
Psmith looked hurt.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯露出受伤之色。

===Original===
“I am a child in these matters,” he said, “but I had always gathered that on these occasions the wishes of the man behind the gun were automatically respected.”

===Chinese===
「此道我是小儿科，」他说，「但我素来的理解是：这种场合，枪后面那位的意愿是自动受到尊重的。」

===Original===
“I’ll call your bluff,” said Miss Peavey firmly. “I’m going to walk straight out of here with this collection of ice right now, and I’ll bet you won’t have the nerve to start any shooting. Shoot a woman? Not you!”

===Chinese===
「我就掀你的底，」皮维小姐斩钉截铁。「我这就要带着这批冰大摇大摆走出这扇门，我赌你没胆子开枪。朝娘们开枪？你才不敢！」

===Original===
Psmith nodded gravely.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯郑重点头。

===Original===
“Your knowledge of psychology is absolutely correct. Your trust in my sense of chivalry rests on solid ground. But,” he proceeded, cheering up, “I fancy that I see a way out of the difficulty. An idea has been vouchsafed to me. I shall shoot﻿—not you, but Comrade Cootes. This will dispose of all unpleasantness. If you attempt to edge out through that door I shall immediately proceed to plug Comrade Cootes in the leg. At least, I shall try. I am a poor shot and may hit him in some more vital spot, but at least he will have the consolation of knowing that I did my best and meant well.”

===Chinese===
「你的心理学造诣完全正确。你对我骑士精神的信任，基础牢固。不过，」他精神一振，接着说，「我似乎瞧出了一条解困之路。天启了一个主意。我要开枪——不打你，打库茨同志。这将一举了却一切不快。你若试图蹭出那扇门，我立刻朝库茨同志腿上来一枪。至少，我尽力。我枪法很臭，没准打中更要害的部位；但他至少能得一点安慰：我是尽了力的，本意是好的。」

===Original===
“Hey!” cried Mr. Cootes. And never, in a life liberally embellished with this favourite ejaculation of his, had he uttered it more feelingly. He shot a feverish glance at Miss Peavey; and, reading in her face indecision rather than that instant acquiescence which he had hoped to see, cast off his customary attitude of respectful humility and asserted himself. He was no caveman, but this was one occasion when he meant to have his own way. With an agonised bound he reached Miss Peavey’s side, wrenched the necklace from her grasp and flung it into the enemy’s camp. Eve stooped and picked it up.

===Chinese===
「喂！」库茨先生喊。在他以这句口头禅作饰的一生里，从没有哪一次喊得这样动情。他火辣辣地瞥向皮维小姐；读出她脸上的不是他所盼的立刻应允，而是犹豫，便抛开了惯常的恭顺谦卑，自己做了一回主。他不是什么穴居野人，但这是他打定要自己说了算的一回。他一个痛楚的箭步抢到皮维小姐身旁，从她掌握里夺下项链，掷进敌方阵地。伊芙俯身拾起。

===Original===
“I thank you,” said Psmith with a brief bow in her direction.

===Chinese===
「谢谢你，」皮史密斯朝她一简短的躬身。

===Original===
Miss Peavey breathed heavily. Her strong hands clenched and unclenched. Between her parted lips her teeth showed in a thin white line. Suddenly she swallowed quickly, as if draining a glass of unpalatable medicine.

===Chinese===
皮维小姐呼吸粗重。她有力的双手一握一张。齿间一线薄白。突然她急促一咽，像灌下一杯苦药。

===Original===
“Well,” she said in a low, even voice, “that seems to be about all. Guess we’ll be going. Come along, Ed, pick up the Henries.”

===Chinese===
「好吧，」她用低而平的声音说，「看来就这样了。咱们走。跟上，埃德，抬起你的亨利们。」

===Original===
“Coming, Liz,” replied Mr. Cootes humbly.

===Chinese===
「来了，莉兹，」库茨先生谦卑地应。

===Original===
They passed together into the night.

===Chinese===
两人相偕步入夜色。

### V / V

===Original===
Silence followed their departure. Eve, weak with the reaction from the complex emotions which she had undergone since her arrival at the cottage, sat on the battered sofa, her chin resting in her hands. She looked at Psmith, who, humming a light air, was delicately piling with the toe of his shoe a funeral mound over the second of the dead bats.

===Chinese===
他们一走，寂静降临。伊芙被进屋以来百感交集的反折腾得浑身发软，坐在砸烂的沙发上，双手托着下巴。她看着皮史密斯——他正哼着一支轻快小调，用鞋尖精细地给第二只死蝙蝠培起一座坟茔。

===Original===
“So that’s that!” she said.

===Chinese===
「这下结了！」她说。

===Original===
Psmith looked up with a bright and friendly smile.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯抬起头，明亮而友善地一笑。

===Original===
“You have a very happy gift of phrase,” he said. “That, as you sensibly say, is that.”

===Chinese===
「你的措辞天赋令人愉快，」他说。「诚如你的明智断语：这下结了。」

===Original===
Eve was silent for awhile. Psmith completed the obsequies and stepped back with the air of a man who has done what he can for a fallen friend.

===Chinese===
伊芙沉默了一阵。皮史密斯完成了葬仪，退后一步，一副已为亡友尽了人事的神气。

===Original===
“Fancy Miss Peavey being a thief!” said Eve. She was somehow feeling a disinclination to allow the conversation to die down, and yet she had an idea that, unless it was permitted to die down, it might become embarrassingly intimate. Subconsciously, she was endeavouring to analyse her views on this long, calm person who had so recently added himself to the list of those who claimed to look upon her with affection.

===Chinese===
「真想不到皮维小姐是个贼！」伊芙说。不知怎么，她不大愿意让谈话冷下去；可又隐隐觉得，若不任它冷下去，它可能变得尴尬地亲昵。潜意识里，她正努力分析自己对这位修长的、心平气和的人的看法——此人新近跻身于自称对她含情脉脉者的名单。

===Original===
“I confess it came as something of a shock to me also,” said Psmith. “In fact, the revelation that there was this other, deeper side to her nature materially altered the opinion I had formed of her. I found myself warming to Miss Peavey. Something that was akin to respect began to stir within me. Indeed, I almost wish that we had not been compelled to deprive her of the jewels.”

===Chinese===
「我承认，这于我也是一惊，」皮史密斯说。「事实上，她天性里这另一更深侧面的曝光，实质性地修正了我对她的既有评价。我发现自己对皮维小姐热络起来。一种近于敬意的东西开始在我胸中萌动。说实话，我几乎遗憾，咱们竟不得不剥夺她的珠宝。」

===Original===
“ ‘We’?” said Eve. “I’m afraid I didn’t do much.”

===Chinese===
「『咱们』？」伊芙说。「我怕是没出什么力。」

===Original===
“Your attitude was exactly right,” Psmith assured her. “You afforded just the moral support which a man needs in such a crisis.”

===Chinese===
「你的姿态恰到好处，」皮史密斯向她保证。「在这种危机里，男人需要的精神支撑，你给的分量刚刚好。」

===Original===
Silence fell once more. Eve returned to her thoughts. And then, with a suddenness which surprised her, she found that she had made up her mind.

===Chinese===
又是一阵沉默。伊芙回到自己的思绪里。然后，连她自己都吃惊地突然，她发觉自己拿定了主意。

===Original===
“So you’re going to be married?” she said.

===Chinese===
「这么说，你要结婚了？」她说。

===Original===
Psmith polished his monocle thoughtfully.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯若有所思地擦拭单片眼镜。

===Original===
“I think so,” he said. “I think so. What do *you* think?”

===Chinese===
「我想是的，」他说。「我想是的。*你*怎么看？」

===Original===
Eve regarded him steadfastly. Then she gave a little laugh.

===Chinese===
伊芙定定地看他。随即她轻轻一笑。

===Original===
“Yes,” she said, “I think so, too.” She paused. “Shall I tell you something?”

===Chinese===
「是，」她说，「我也这么看。」她顿了顿。「要不要告诉你一件事？」

===Original===
“You could tell me nothing more wonderful than that.”

===Chinese===
「再没有比这更动听的了。」

===Original===
“When I met Cynthia in Market Blandings, she told me what the trouble was which made her husband leave her. What do you suppose it was?”

===Chinese===
「我在布兰丁斯集镇见辛西娅的时候，她告诉了我，是什么祸事叫她丈夫离开了她。你猜是什么？」

===Original===
“From my brief acquaintance with Comrade McTodd, I would hazard the guess that he tried to stab her with the bread-knife. He struck me as a murderous-looking specimen.”

===Chinese===
「凭我同麦克托德同志的一面之缘，我猜他拿面包刀捅了她。他瞧着就像个杀气腾腾的样本。」

===Original===
“They had some people to dinner, and there was chicken, and Cynthia gave all the giblets to the guests, and her husband bounded out of his seat with a wild cry, and, shouting ‘You *know* I love those things better than anything in the world!’ rushed from the house, never to return!”

===Chinese===
「他们请人吃饭，有鸡，辛西娅把鸡杂全分给了客人，她丈夫狂叫一声蹦起来，喊着『你*明知道*我爱那玩意儿胜过世上一切！』冲出家门，一去不回！」

===Original===
“Precisely how I would have wished him to rush, had I been Mrs. McTodd.”

===Chinese===
「换了我是麦克托德太太，他要冲，我也是这个冲法，正中下怀。」

===Original===
“Cynthia told me that he had rushed from the house, never to return, six times since they were married.”

===Chinese===
「辛西娅告诉我，结婚以来，他这样冲出家门一去不回，已经六回了。」

===Original===
“May I mention﻿—in passing﻿—” said Psmith, “that I do not like chicken giblets?”

===Chinese===
「可否容我——顺带——提一句，」皮史密斯说，「我不爱吃鸡杂？」

===Original===
“Cynthia advised me,” proceeded Eve, “if ever I married, to marry someone eccentric. She said it was such fun. Well, I don’t suppose I am ever likely to meet anyone more eccentric than you, am I?”

===Chinese===
「辛西娅劝我，」伊芙接着说，「将来若结婚，就嫁个怪人。她说怪人过日子才有趣。这个嘛，我估摸这辈子再碰不上比你更怪的了，对不对？」

===Original===
“I think you would be unwise to wait on the chance.”

===Chinese===
「你要坐等良机，我看是不明智的。」

===Original===
“The only thing is﻿ ﻿… ,” said Eve reflectively. “ ‘Mrs. Smith’﻿ ﻿… It doesn’t *sound* much, does it?”

===Chinese===
「只一样……」伊芙若有所思地说，「『史密斯太太』……听着*不够*响亮，是吧？」

===Original===
Psmith beamed encouragingly.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯笑吟吟地给她打气。

===Original===
“We must look into the future,” he said. “We must remember that I am only at the beginning of what I am convinced is to be a singularly illustrious career. ‘Lady Psmith’ is better﻿ ﻿… ‘Baroness Psmith’ better still﻿ ﻿… And﻿—who knows?﻿—‘The Duchess of Psmith’﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「咱们要把眼光放远，」他说。「要记住：我此刻不过站在这条——我确信——将格外辉煌的事业的起点。『皮史密斯夫人』就好些……『皮史密斯男爵夫人』更佳……而且——谁知将来？——『皮史密斯公爵夫人』……」

===Original===
“Well, anyhow,” said Eve, “you were wonderful just now, simply wonderful. The way you made one spring﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「反正，」伊芙说，「你方才真是棒极了，简直棒极了。你那一步窜……」

===Original===
“Your words,” said Psmith, “are music to my ears, but we must not forget that the foundations of the success of the manoeuvre were laid by Comrade Threepwood. Had it not been for the timely incursion of his leg﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「你的话，」皮史密斯说，「于我如仙乐；但咱们不可忘了，此役成功的地基，是思里普伍德同志打的。若没有他那条腿及时杀到……」

===Original===
“Good gracious!” cried Eve. “Freddie! I had forgotten all about him!”

===Chinese===
「天哪！」伊芙喊。「弗雷迪！我把他忘得精光！」

===Original===
“The right spirit,” said Psmith. “Quite the right spirit.”

===Chinese===
「这就对了，」皮史密斯说。「完全对路。」

===Original===
“We must go and let him out.”

===Chinese===
「咱们得去放他出来。」

===Original===
“Just as you say. And then he can come with us on the stroll I was about to propose that we should take through the woods. It is a lovely night, and what could be jollier than to have Comrade Threepwood prattling at our side? I will go and let him out at once.”

===Chinese===
「悉听尊便。然后他正好同去——我正想提议咱们在林中散个步。夜色这么好，有思里普伍德同志在身旁叽叽喳喳，还有什么更快活的？我这就去放他。」

===Original===
“No, don’t bother,” said Eve.

===Chinese===
「不，不用麻烦了，」伊芙说。

## XIV Psmith Accepts Employment / 皮史密斯接受聘任

===Original===
The golden stillness of a perfect summer morning brooded over Blandings Castle and its adjacent pleasure-grounds. From a sky of unbroken blue the sun poured down its heartening rays on all those roses, pinks, pansies, carnations, hollyhocks, columbines, larkspurs, London pride and Canterbury bells which made the gardens so rarely beautiful. Flannelled youths and maidens in white serge sported in the shade; gay cries arose from the tennis-courts behind the shrubbery; and birds, bees, and butterflies went about their business with a new energy and zip. In short, the casual observer, assuming that he was addicted to trite phrases, would have said that happiness reigned supreme.

===Chinese===
一个完美夏日清晨的金色静谧，笼罩着布兰丁斯城堡及其左近的园林。太阳从万里无云的碧空倾下暖人的光芒，照拂着玫瑰、石竹、三色堇、康乃馨、蜀葵、耧斗菜、飞燕草、虎耳草和坎特伯雷钟——正是它们把花园妆点得这般罕有的美丽。法兰绒装的青年与白呢料的姑娘们在树荫里嬉戏；灌木网球场后欢声四起；鸟、蜂、蝶以崭新的劲头与锐气各忙各的营生。总而言之，一个爱用俗套话的随便什么旁观者，就会说：幸福君临一切。

===Original===
But happiness, even on the finest mornings, is seldom universal. The strolling youths and maidens were happy; the tennis-players were happy; the birds, bees, and butterflies were happy. Eve, walking in pleasant meditation on the terrace, was happy. Freddie Threepwood was happy as he lounged in the smoking-room and gloated over the information, received from Psmith in the small hours, that his thousand pounds was safe. Mr. Keeble, writing to Phyllis to inform her that she might clinch the purchase of the Lincolnshire farm, was happy. Even Head-gardener Angus McAllister was as happy as a Scotsman can ever be. But Lord Emsworth, drooping out of the library window, felt only a nervous irritation more in keeping with the blizzards of winter than with the only fine July that England had known in the last ten years.

===Chinese===
然而幸福，即便在最晴美的早晨，也难得普及。闲逛的青年与姑娘是幸福的；打网球的是幸福的；鸟、蜂、蝶是幸福的。在露台上愉快出神地散步的伊芙，是幸福的。弗雷迪·思里普伍德瘫在吸烟室里，咂摸着凌晨从皮史密斯处得来的消息——他那一千镑稳了——也是幸福的。基布尔先生正给菲利斯写信，告知她可以落定林肯郡农场的买卖了，是幸福的。连首席园丁安格斯·麦卡利斯特，也幸福到了一个苏格兰人所能达到的极限。唯独埃姆斯沃思勋爵，蔫在藏书室窗口，满心只有一种神经质的烦躁——那情绪配给冬天的暴风雪还差不多，配给英国十年来头一个晴美的七月，实在错位。

===Original===
We have seen his lordship in a similar attitude and a like frame of mind on a previous occasion; but then his melancholy had been due to the loss of his glasses. This morning these were perched firmly on his nose and he saw all things clearly. What was causing his gloom now was the fact that some ten minutes earlier his sister Constance had trapped him in the library, full of jarring rebuke on the subject of the dismissal of Rupert Baxter, the world’s most efficient secretary. It was to avoid her compelling eye that Lord Emsworth had turned to the window. And what he saw from that window thrust him even deeper into the abyss of gloom. The sun, the birds, the bees, the butterflies, and the flowers called to him to come out and have the time of his life, but he just lacked the nerve to make a dash for it.

===Chinese===
类似的姿势、相似的心绪，我们前文已见勋爵大人展示过一回；不过那次他的忧郁源于丢了眼镜。今晨眼镜端踞鼻梁，万物看得分明。此刻压弯他的，是这样一个事实：约十分钟前，他妹妹康斯坦丝在藏书室把他堵了个正着，就辞退鲁珀特·巴克斯特——世上最高效的秘书——一事，发表了一大篇刺耳的数落。埃姆斯沃思勋爵正是为躲避她那双不容分说的眼睛，才扭头朝向窗口的。而窗外所见，把他推进了更深的阴郁之渊。太阳、鸟、蜂、蝶、花，齐声唤他出去、享享人生，可他就是没胆子夺路而逃。

===Original===
“I think you must be mad,” said Lady Constance bitterly, resuming her remarks and starting at the point where she had begun before.

===Chinese===
「我看你准是疯了，」康斯坦丝夫人恨恨地说，重新开讲，并从她此前开头的那一点讲起。

===Original===
“Baxter’s mad,” retorted his lordship, also retreading old ground.

===Chinese===
「巴克斯特才疯了，」勋爵大人反诘，也重炒起自家的旧地。

===Original===
“You are too absurd!”

===Chinese===
「你真是岂有此理！」

===Original===
“He threw flowerpots at me.”

===Chinese===
「他朝我扔花盆。」

===Original===
“Do please stop talking about those flowerpots. Mr. Baxter has explained the whole thing to me, and surely even you can see that his behaviour was perfectly excusable.”

===Chinese===
「请你别再提那些花盆了。巴克斯特先生已经把一切向我解释清楚了，想来连你也看得出，他的行为完全情有可原。」

===Original===
“I don’t like the fellow,” cried Lord Emsworth, once more retreating to his last line of trenches﻿—the one line from which all Lady Constance’s eloquence had been unable to dislodge him.

===Chinese===
「我不喜欢那家伙，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵喊，又一次退进他最后一道堑壕——康斯坦丝夫人纵有三寸不烂之舌，始终没能把他从这道壕里撼出去的那一道。

===Original===
There was a silence, as there had been a short while before when the discussion had reached this same point.

===Chinese===
一阵沉默，同方才讨论抵达同一点时一样。

===Original===
“You will be helpless without him,” said Lady Constance.

===Chinese===
「没有他，你将寸步难行，」康斯坦丝夫人说。

===Original===
“Nothing of the kind,” said his lordship.

===Chinese===
「哪至于，」勋爵大人说。

===Original===
“You know you will. Where will you ever get another secretary capable of looking after everything like Mr. Baxter? You know you are a perfect child, and unless you have someone whom you can trust to manage your affairs I cannot see what will happen.”

===Chinese===
「你自己知道至于。你上哪儿再找一个样样照管得来的秘书？你知道你是个十足的孩子，身边没有一个信得过、替你打理事务的人，我看不出会闹成什么样。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth made no reply. He merely gazed wanly from the window.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵不答。他只是惨淡地望着窗外。

===Original===
“Chaos,” moaned Lady Constance.

===Chinese===
「天下大乱，」康斯坦丝夫人呻吟道。

===Original===
His lordship remained mute, but now there was a gleam of something approaching pleasure in his pale eyes; for at this moment a car rounded the corner of the house from the direction of the stables and stood purring at the door. There was a trunk on the car and a suitcase. And almost simultaneously the Efficient Baxter entered the library, clothed and spatted for travel.

===Chinese===
勋爵大人依旧哑着，可他苍白的眼里此刻掠过一丝近于快慰的光；因为恰在此刻，一辆汽车从马厩方向绕过屋角，嗡嗡停在门前。车上有衣箱一只、提箱一只。几乎同时，能干的巴克斯特走进藏书室，衣冠齐整，鞋罩护腿，一副远行装束。

===Original===
“I have come to say goodbye, Lady Constance,” said Baxter coldly and precisely, flashing at his late employer through his spectacles a look of stern reproach. “The car which is taking me to the station is at the door.”

===Chinese===
「我是来道别的，康斯坦丝夫人，」巴克斯特冷峻而精确地说，隔着镜片朝旧雇主闪去一瞥严厉的责难。「送我去车站的车已在门口。」

===Original===
“Oh, Mr. Baxter.” Lady Constance, strong woman though she was, fluttered with distress. “Oh, Mr. Baxter.”

===Chinese===
「哦，巴克斯特先生。」康斯坦丝夫人虽是强人，也慌乱得直颤。「哦，巴克斯特先生。」

===Original===
“Goodbye.” He gripped her hand in brief farewell and directed his spectacles for another tense instant upon the sagging figure at the window. “Goodbye, Lord Emsworth.”

===Chinese===
「再会。」他匆匆一握她的手，又把镜片朝窗边那具瘫软的身影钉了一瞬。「再会，埃姆斯沃思勋爵。」

===Original===
“Eh? What? Oh! Ah, yes. Goodbye, my dear fel﻿—, I mean, goodbye. I﻿—er﻿—hope you will have a pleasant journey.”

===Chinese===
「嗯？什么？哦！啊，是。再会，亲爱的伙——我是说，再会。我——呃——祝你旅途愉快。」

===Original===
“Thank you,” said Baxter.

===Chinese===
「谢谢，」巴克斯特说。

===Original===
“But, Mr. Baxter,” said Lady Constance.

===Chinese===
「可是，巴克斯特先生，」康斯坦丝夫人说。

===Original===
“Lord Emsworth,” said the ex-secretary icily, “I am no longer in your employment﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「埃姆斯沃思勋爵，」前秘书冷若冰霜地说，「我已不在您的雇用之列……」

===Original===
“But, Mr. Baxter,” moaned Lady Constance, “surely﻿ ﻿… even now﻿ ﻿… misunderstanding﻿ ﻿… talk it all over quietly﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「可是，巴克斯特先生，」康斯坦丝夫人哀声道，「说真的……事到如今……误会……心平气和地谈一谈……」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth started violently.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵猛地一惊。

===Original===
“Here!” he protested, in much the same manner as that in which the recent Mr. Cootes had been wont to say “Hey!”

===Chinese===
「慢着！」他抗议道，那腔调同不久前库茨先生说「喂！」时的惯用腔如出一辙。

===Original===
“I fear it is too late,” said Baxter, to his infinite relief, “to talk things over. My arrangements are already made and cannot be altered. Ever since I came here to work for Lord Emsworth, my former employer﻿—an American millionaire named Jevons﻿—has been making me flattering offers to return to him. Until now a mistaken sense of loyalty has kept me from accepting these offers, but this morning I telegraphed to Mr. Jevons to say that I was at liberty and could join him at once. It is too late now to cancel this promise.”

===Chinese===
「恐怕，」巴克斯特说——这话说得他无限宽慰，「谈得太迟了。我的安排已定，无可更改。自打我来为埃姆斯沃思勋爵工作，我旧日的东家——一位姓杰文斯的美国百万富翁——一直许以优渥的条件邀我回去。此前是一念之差的愚忠绊着我未允。可今晨我已电告杰文斯先生：我已自由之身，即刻可以到他那儿赴任。此刻再毁约，迟了。」

===Original===
“Quite, quite, oh certainly, quite, mustn’t dream of it, my dear fellow. No, no, no, indeed no,” said Lord Emsworth with an effervescent cordiality which struck both his hearers as in the most dubious taste.

===Chinese===
「正是，正是，哦当然，正是，想都别想，亲爱的伙计。不不不，当真不，」埃姆斯沃思勋爵说，那阵沸腾的热忱，听得两位听者齐齐觉得俗不可耐。

===Original===
Baxter merely stiffened haughtily, but Lady Constance was so poignantly affected by the words and the joyous tone in which they were uttered that she could endure her brother’s loathly society no longer. Shaking Baxter’s hand once more and gazing stonily for a moment at the worm by the window, she left the room.

===Chinese===
巴克斯特只是倨傲地绷紧了身子；康斯坦丝夫人却被这番话连同话里那股欢天喜地的腔调刺痛，再也无法忍受她哥哥这副嘴脸。她再握了握巴克斯特的手，又朝窗边那条虫投去片刻石般的凝视，离屋而去。

===Original===
For some seconds after she had gone, there was silence﻿—a silence which Lord Emsworth found embarrassing. He turned to the window again and took in with one wistful glance the roses, the pinks, the pansies, the carnations, the hollyhocks, the columbines, the larkspurs, the London pride and the Canterbury bells. And then suddenly there came to him the realisation that with Lady Constance gone there no longer existed any reason why he should stay cooped up in this stuffy library on the finest morning that had ever been sent to gladden the heart of man. He shivered ecstatically from the top of his bald head to the soles of his roomy shoes, and, bounding gleefully from the window, started to amble across the room.

===Chinese===
她走后好几秒，屋里静着——这份静令埃姆斯沃思勋爵如坐针毡。他又转向窗口，怅然一瞥，把玫瑰、石竹、三色堇、康乃馨、蜀葵、耧斗菜、飞燕草、虎耳草和坎特伯雷钟尽收眼底。蓦地，他醒悟了：康斯坦丝夫人既走，在这上天赐予世人赏心的最好的早晨，就再没有任何理由把他闷在这间不透风的藏书室里了。他从光头顶到宽鞋底出神地一个寒噤，欢快地蹦离窗口，蹒跚着横穿房间。

===Original===
“Lord Emsworth!”

===Chinese===
「埃姆斯沃思勋爵！」

===Original===
His lordship halted. His was a one-track mind, capable of accommodating only one thought at a time﻿—if that, and he had almost forgotten that Baxter was still there. He eyed his late secretary peevishly.

===Chinese===
勋爵大人刹住。他那是一副单轨头脑，一次只容得下一个念头——有时还容不下——他几乎忘了巴克斯特还在。他没好气地瞥着他昔日的秘书。

===Original===
“Yes, yes? Is there anything﻿ ﻿… ?”

===Chinese===
「是，是？有什么……？」

===Original===
“I should like to speak to you for a moment.”

===Chinese===
「我想同您讲一句话。」

===Original===
“I have a most important conference with McAllister﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我同麦卡利斯特有个顶要紧的会议……」

===Original===
“I will not detain you long. Lord Emsworth, I am no longer in your employment, but I think it my duty to say before I go﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「误不了您多久。埃姆斯沃思勋爵，我已不在您麾下，但我觉得临走前有责任说一句……」

===Original===
“No, no, my dear fellow, I quite understand. Quite, quite, quite. Constance has been going over all that. I know what you are trying to say. That matter of the flowerpots. Please do not apologise. It is quite all right. I was startled at the time, I own, but no doubt you had excellent motives. Let us forget the whole affair.”

===Chinese===
「不不，亲爱的伙计，我完全明白。完全，完全，完全。康斯坦丝方才把这一切都过了一遍。我知道你要说什么。花盆那件事。请不必道歉。没什么的。当时我确实吃了一惊，这我认，可你无疑出于极好的动机。咱们把整件事忘了吧。」

===Original===
Baxter ground an impatient heel into the carpet.

===Chinese===
巴克斯特一只不耐烦的鞋跟碾进地毯。

===Original===
“I had no intention of referring to the matter to which you allude,” he said. “I merely wished﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「我无意提及您影射的那件事，」他说。「我只是想……」

===Original===
“Yes, yes, of course.” A vagrant breeze floated in at the window, languid with summer scents, and Lord Emsworth, sniffing, shuffled restlessly. “Of course, of course, of course. Some other time, eh? Yes, yes, that will be capital. Capital, capital, cap﻿—”

===Chinese===
「是是，当然。」一缕贪懒的夏风从窗口飘入，带着慵懒的花香，埃姆斯沃思勋爵抽了抽鼻子，焦躁地挪着步。「当然，当然，当然。改日再说，嗯？是是，那就好极了。好极了，好极了，好——」

===Original===
The Efficient Baxter uttered a sound that was partly a cry, partly a snort. Its quality was so arresting that Lord Emsworth paused, his fingers on the door-handle, and peered back at him, startled.

===Chinese===
能干的巴克斯特发出一声半是呐喊半是鼻音的声响。其音色之惊人，令埃姆斯沃思勋爵手抚门柄刹住，惊惶地回头张望。

===Original===
“Very well,” said Baxter shortly. “Pray do not let me keep you. If you are not interested in the fact that Blandings Castle is sheltering a criminal﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「很好，」巴克斯特短促地说。「请勿因我耽搁。倘若您对『布兰丁斯城堡正窝藏一名罪犯』这一事实不感兴趣……」

===Original===
It was not easy to divert Lord Emsworth when in quest of Angus McAllister, but this remark succeeded in doing so. He let go of the door-handle and came back a step or two into the room.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵寻访安格斯·麦卡利斯特心切，本不易分神，这句话却办到了。他松开门柄，退回屋里一两步。

===Original===
“Sheltering a criminal?”

===Chinese===
「窝藏一名罪犯？」

===Original===
“Yes.” Baxter glanced at his watch. “I must go now or I shall miss my train,” he said curtly. “I was merely going to tell you that this fellow who calls himself Ralston McTodd is not Ralston McTodd at all.”

===Chinese===
「是。」巴克斯特看了看表。「我必须走了，否则误车，」他简短地说。「我原本只想告诉您：这个自称拉尔斯顿·麦克托德的家伙，根本不是拉尔斯顿·麦克托德。」

===Original===
“Not Ralston McTodd?” repeated his lordship blankly. “But﻿—” He suddenly perceived a flaw in the argument. “But he *said* he was,” he pointed out cleverly. “Yes, I remember distinctly. He said he was McTodd.”

===Chinese===
「不是拉尔斯顿·麦克托德？」勋爵大人茫然复述。「可是——」他猛然看出论证里的破绽。「可是他*自己说*他是，」他机敏地指出。「是，我记得清清楚楚。他说他是麦克托德。」

===Original===
“He is an impostor. And I imagine that if you investigate you will find that it is he and his accomplices who stole Lady Constance’s necklace.”

===Chinese===
「他是个冒名顶替的骗子。而且我想，您若查一查便会发现，偷康斯坦丝夫人项链的，正是他及其同伙。」

===Original===
“But, my dear fellow﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「可是，亲爱的伙计……」

===Original===
Baxter walked briskly to the door.

===Chinese===
巴克斯特快步走向门口。

===Original===
“You need not take my word for it,” he said. “What I say can easily be proved. Get this so-called McTodd to write his name on a piece of paper and then compare it with the signature to the letter which the real McTodd wrote when accepting Lady Constance’s invitation to the castle. You will find it filed away in the drawer of that desk there.”

===Chinese===
「您无须信我一面之词，」他说。「我说的事轻而易举就能证实。叫这个所谓的麦克托德在一张纸上写下名字，再同真麦克托德答复康斯坦丝夫人城堡之邀那封信上的签名比对。那封信就归档在那边书桌的抽屉里。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth adjusted his glasses and stared at the desk as if he expected it to do a conjuring-trick.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵正了正眼镜，瞪着那张书桌，仿佛等它变个戏法。

===Original===
“I will leave you to take what steps you please,” said Baxter. “Now that I am no longer in your employment, the thing does not concern me one way or another. But I thought you might be glad to hear the facts.”

===Chinese===
「后续步骤悉听尊便，」巴克斯特说。「我既已不在您麾下，此事于我两不相干。但我想，您或许乐闻事实。」

===Original===
“Oh, I *am*!” responded his lordship, still peering vaguely. “Oh, I *am*! Oh, yes, yes, yes. Oh, yes, yes﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「哦，我*乐闻*！」勋爵大人应道，还在茫然地瞪视。「哦，我*乐闻*！哦，是，是，是。哦，是，是……」

===Original===
“Goodbye.”

===Chinese===
「再会。」

===Original===
“But, Baxter﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
「可是，巴克斯特……」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth trotted out on to the landing, but Baxter had got off to a good start and was almost out of sight round the bend of the stairs.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵小跑追到楼梯口，可巴克斯特起步快，已在楼梯拐弯处几乎没了影。

===Original===
“But, my dear fellow﻿ ﻿…” bleated his lordship plaintively over the banisters.

===Chinese===
「可是，亲爱的伙计……」勋爵大人扶着栏杆，哀哀地朝下喊。

===Original===
From below, out on the drive, came the sound of an automobile getting into gear and moving off, than which no sound is more final. The great door of the castle closed with a soft but significant bang﻿—as doors close when handled by an untipped butler. Lord Emsworth returned to the library to wrestle with his problem unaided.

===Chinese===
楼下车道上，传来汽车挂挡起步的声响——天下再没有比这更一锤定音的声响。城堡大门带着一声轻而意味深长的碰响合上了——没拿到赏钱的管家关门，就是这个关法。埃姆斯沃思勋爵回到藏书室，独自角力他的难题去了。

===Original===
He was greatly disturbed. Apart from the fact that he disliked criminals and impostors as a class, it was a shock to him to learn that the particular criminal and impostor then in residence at Blandings was the man for whom, brief as had been the duration of their acquaintance, he had conceived a warm affection. He was fond of Psmith. Psmith soothed him. If he had had to choose any member of his immediate circle for the role of criminal and impostor, he would have chosen Psmith last.

===Chinese===
他大为不安。撇开作为一类人他本不喜欢罪犯与骗子不谈，得知布兰丁斯现行在住的那位特定罪犯兼骗子，竟是他一见如故便生出温情的那位——虽说他俩相识时短——这于他是一记闷棍。他喜欢皮史密斯。皮史密斯安他的神。倘若非要他从身边人里点一个来充当罪犯兼骗子，他点谁也不会点皮史密斯。

===Original===
He went to the window again and looked out. There was the sunshine, there were the birds, there were the hollyhocks, carnations, and Canterbury bells, all present and correct; but now they failed to cheer him. He was wondering dismally what on earth he was going to do. What *did* one do with criminals and impostors? Had ’em arrested, he supposed. But he shrank from the thought of arresting Psmith. It seemed so deuced unfriendly.

===Chinese===
他又走到窗口外望。阳光在，鸟在，蜀葵、康乃馨、坎特伯雷钟在，全数点卯；可它们再鼓不起他的劲了。他凄凄惶惶地琢磨：这可如何是好。罪犯与骗子，该怎么处置？我想是逮捕吧。可逮捕皮史密斯这个念头，他缩手。未免太不够朋友了。

===Original===
He was still meditating gloomily when a voice spoke behind him.

===Chinese===
他正闷闷盘算，身后一个声音开了口。

===Original===
“Good morning. I am looking for Miss Halliday. You have not seen her by any chance? Ah, there she is down there on the terrace.”

===Chinese===
「早上好。我在找哈利迪小姐。您没碰巧见着她吧？啊，她在下面露台上呢。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth was aware of Psmith beside him at the window, waving cordially to Eve, who waved back.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵发觉皮史密斯已立在窗边，正朝伊芙热诚挥手，伊芙也挥手回应。

===Original===
“I thought possibly,” continued Psmith, “that Miss Halliday would be in her little room yonder”﻿—he indicated the dummy bookshelves through which he had entered. “But I am glad to see that the morning is so fine that she has given toil the miss-in-baulk. It is the right spirit,” said Psmith. “I like to see it.”

===Chinese===
「我原想，」皮史密斯接着说，「哈利迪小姐多半在她那边的小屋里」——他指了指自己进来的那排假书架。「可我很高兴地看到，今晨天气这样好，她给劳作吃了个闭门羹。这才是对的精神，」皮史密斯说。「我爱看这个。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth peered at him nervously through his glasses. His embarrassment and his distaste for the task that lay before him increased as he scanned his companion in vain for those signs of villainy which all well-regulated criminals and impostors ought to exhibit to the eye of discernment.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵隔着镜片紧张地瞅他。他把同伴上下打量，徒劳地搜寻一切循规蹈矩的罪犯与骗子理应展示给慧眼的那些恶相，而一无所获；他的窘迫、他对眼前差事的不情愿，随之俱增。

===Original===
“I am surprised to find you indoors,” said Psmith, “on so glorious a morning. I should have supposed that you would have been down there among the shrubs, taking a good sniff at a hollyhock or something.”

===Chinese===
「这么美好的早晨，竟在屋里碰见你，我真意外，」皮史密斯说。「我还只道你早在下面灌木丛里，对着哪株蜀葵什么的好好嗅上一阵了呢。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth braced himself for the ordeal.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵硬着头皮迎向酷刑。

===Original===
“Er, my dear fellow﻿ ﻿… that is to say﻿ ﻿…” He paused. Psmith was regarding him almost lovingly through his monocle, and it was becoming increasingly difficult to warm up to the work of denouncing him.

===Chinese===
「呃，亲爱的伙计……我是说……」他顿住。皮史密斯隔着单片眼镜几乎是脉脉含情地望着他，要烧起揭发他的热忱，越来越难了。

===Original===
“You were observing﻿ ﻿… ?” said Psmith.

===Chinese===
「您方才正说到……？」皮史密斯说。

===Original===
Lord Emsworth uttered curious buzzing noises.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵发出一阵古怪的嗡嗡声。

===Original===
“I have just parted from Baxter,” he said at length, deciding to approach the subject in more roundabout fashion.

===Chinese===
「我刚同巴克斯特分手，」他终于说，决定绕个更迂回的弯子进入正题。

===Original===
“Indeed?” said Psmith courteously.

===Chinese===
「当真？」皮史密斯谦和地说。

===Original===
“Yes. Baxter has gone.”

===Chinese===
「是啊。巴克斯特走了。」

===Original===
“For ever?”

===Chinese===
「永不回来？」

===Original===
“Er﻿—yes.”

===Chinese===
「呃——是。」

===Original===
“Splendid!” said Psmith. “Splendid, splendid.”

===Chinese===
「妙极！」皮史密斯说。「妙极，妙极。」

===Original===
Lord Emsworth removed his glasses, twiddled them on their cord, and replaced them on his nose.

===Chinese===
埃姆斯沃思勋爵摘下眼镜，捻着镜绳转了转，又架回鼻梁。

===Original===
“He made﻿ ﻿… He﻿—er﻿—the fact is, he made﻿ ﻿… Before he went Baxter made a most remarkable statement﻿ ﻿… a charge﻿ ﻿… Well, in short, he made a very strange statement about you.”

===Chinese===
「他曾……他——呃——实情是，他曾……临走前，巴克斯特发表了一篇极不寻常的声明……一项指控……总而言之，他就你发表了一篇很离奇的声明。」

===Original===
Psmith nodded gravely.

===Chinese===
皮史密斯庄重地点头。

===Original===
“I had been expecting something of the kind,” he said. “He said, no doubt, that I was not really Ralston McTodd?”

===Chinese===
「我早料到有此一出，」他说。「他无疑说了：我并不是真的拉尔斯顿·麦克托德？」

===Original===
His lordship’s mouth opened feebly.

===Chinese===
勋爵大人的嘴无力地张着。

===Original===
“Er﻿—yes,” he said.

===Chinese===
「呃——是，」他说。

===Original===
“I’ve been meaning to tell you about that,” said Psmith amiably. “It is quite true. I am not Ralston McTodd.”
